43/**
44 * \brief Returns the coordinates of points on the surface of a cube.
45 * \param[in] p Number of points on an edge of the cube is (n+1)
46 * \param[in] c Coordinates to the centre of the cube (3D array).
47 * \param[in] alpha Scaling factor for the size of the cube.
48 * \param[in] depth Depth of the cube in the octree.
49 * \return Vector with coordinates of points on the surface of the cube in the
50 * format [x0 y0 z0 x1 y1 z1 .... ].
51 */
52template <class Real_t>
53std::vector<Real_t> surface(int p, Real_t* c, Real_t alpha, int depth){
54 size_t n_=(6*(p-1)*(p-1)+2); //Total number of points.
55
56 std::vector<Real_t> coord(n_*3);
57 coord[0]=coord[1]=coord[2]=-1.0;
58 size_t cnt=1;
59 for(int i=0;i<p-1;i++)
60 for(int j=0;j<p-1;j++){
61 coord[cnt*3 ]=-1;
62 coord[cnt*3+1]=(2*(i+1)-p+1)/(Real_t)(p-1);
63 coord[cnt*3+2]=(2*j-p+1)/(Real_t)(p-1);
64 cnt++;
65 }
66 for(int i=0;i<p-1;i++)
67 for(int j=0;j<p-1;j++){
68 coord[cnt*3 ]=(2*i-p+1)/(Real_t)(p-1);
69 coord[cnt*3+1]=-1;
70 coord[cnt*3+2]=(2*(j+1)-p+1)/(Real_t)(p-1);
71 cnt++;
72 }
73 for(int i=0;i<p-1;i++)
74 for(int j=0;j<p-1;j++){
75 coord[cnt*3 ]=(2*(i+1)-p+1)/(Real_t)(p-1);
76 coord[cnt*3+1]=(2*j-p+1)/(Real_t)(p-1);
77 coord[cnt*3+2]=-1;
78 cnt++;
79 }
80 for(size_t i=0;i<(n_/2)*3;i++)
81 coord[cnt*3+i]=-coord[i];
82
83 Real_t r = (Real_t)0.5*sctl::pow<Real_t>(0.5,depth);
84 Real_t b = alpha*r;
85 for(size_t i=0;i<n_;i++){
86 coord[i*3+0]=(coord[i*3+0]+1)*b+c[0];
87 coord[i*3+1]=(coord[i*3+1]+1)*b+c[1];
88 coord[i*3+2]=(coord[i*3+2]+1)*b+c[2];
89 }
90 return coord;
91}
92
93/**
94 * \brief Returns the coordinates of points on the upward check surface of cube.
95 * \see surface()
96 */
97template <class Real_t>
98std::vector<Real_t> u_check_surf(int p, Real_t* c, int depth){
99 Real_t r=(Real_t)0.5*sctl::pow<Real_t>(0.5,depth);
100 Real_t coord[3]={(Real_t)(c[0]-r*(PVFMM_RAD1-1.0)),(Real_t)(c[1]-r*(PVFMM_RAD1-1.0)),(Real_t)(c[2]-r*(PVFMM_RAD1-1.0))};
101 return surface(p,coord,(Real_t)PVFMM_RAD1,depth);
102}
103
104/**
105 * \brief Returns the coordinates of points on the upward equivalent surface of cube.
106 * \see surface()
107 */
108template <class Real_t>
109std::vector<Real_t> u_equiv_surf(int p, Real_t* c, int depth){
110 Real_t r=(Real_t)0.5*sctl::pow<Real_t>(0.5,depth);
111 Real_t coord[3]={(Real_t)(c[0]-r*(PVFMM_RAD0-1.0)),(Real_t)(c[1]-r*(PVFMM_RAD0-1.0)),(Real_t)(c[2]-r*(PVFMM_RAD0-1.0))};
112 return surface(p,coord,(Real_t)PVFMM_RAD0,depth);
113}
114
115/**
116 * \brief Returns the coordinates of points on the downward check surface of cube.
117 * \see surface()
118 */
119template <class Real_t>
120std::vector<Real_t> d_check_surf(int p, Real_t* c, int depth){
121 Real_t r=(Real_t)0.5*sctl::pow<Real_t>(0.5,depth);
122 Real_t coord[3]={(Real_t)(c[0]-r*(PVFMM_RAD0-1.0)),(Real_t)(c[1]-r*(PVFMM_RAD0-1.0)),(Real_t)(c[2]-r*(PVFMM_RAD0-1.0))};
123 return surface(p,coord,(Real_t)PVFMM_RAD0,depth);
124}
125
126/**
127 * \brief Returns the coordinates of points on the downward equivalent surface of cube.
128 * \see surface()
129 */
130template <class Real_t>
131std::vector<Real_t> d_equiv_surf(int p, Real_t* c, int depth){
132 Real_t r=(Real_t)0.5*sctl::pow<Real_t>(0.5,depth);
133 Real_t coord[3]={(Real_t)(c[0]-r*(PVFMM_RAD1-1.0)),(Real_t)(c[1]-r*(PVFMM_RAD1-1.0)),(Real_t)(c[2]-r*(PVFMM_RAD1-1.0))};
134 return surface(p,coord,(Real_t)PVFMM_RAD1,depth);
135}
136
137/**
138 * \brief Defines the 3D grid for convolution in FFT acceleration of V-list.
139 * \see surface()
140 */
141template <class Real_t>
142std::vector<Real_t> conv_grid(int p, Real_t* c, int depth){
143 Real_t r=sctl::pow<Real_t>(0.5,depth);
144 Real_t a=r*(Real_t)PVFMM_RAD0;
145 Real_t coord[3]={c[0],c[1],c[2]};
146 int n1=p*2;
147 int n2=sctl::pow<int>(n1,2);
148 int n3=sctl::pow<int>(n1,3);
149 std::vector<Real_t> grid(n3*3);
150 for(int i=0;i<n1;i++)
151 for(int j=0;j<n1;j++)
152 for(int k=0;k<n1;k++){
153 grid[(i+n1*j+n2*k)*3+0]=(i-p)*a/(p-1)+coord[0];
154 grid[(i+n1*j+n2*k)*3+1]=(j-p)*a/(p-1)+coord[1];
155 grid[(i+n1*j+n2*k)*3+2]=(k-p)*a/(p-1)+coord[2];
156 }
157 return grid;
158}
159
160template <class Real_t>
161void FMM_Data<Real_t>::Clear(){
162 upward_equiv.ReInit(0);
163}
164
165template <class Real_t>
166PackedData FMM_Data<Real_t>::PackMultipole(void* buff_ptr){
167 PackedData p0; p0.data=buff_ptr;
168 p0.length=upward_equiv.Dim()*sizeof(Real_t);
169 if(p0.length==0) return p0;
170
171 if(p0.data==NULL) p0.data=(char*)&upward_equiv[0];
172 else memcpy(p0.data,&upward_equiv[0],p0.length);
173 return p0;
174}
175
176template <class Real_t>
177void FMM_Data<Real_t>::AddMultipole(PackedData p0){
178 Real_t* data=(Real_t*)p0.data;
179 size_t n=p0.length/sizeof(Real_t);
180 assert((size_t)upward_equiv.Dim()==n);
181 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> v0(1,n, sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>(&upward_equiv[0], (1)*(n)),false);
182 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> v1(1,n, sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>(data, (1)*(n)),false);
183 v0+=v1;
184}
185
186template <class Real_t>
187void FMM_Data<Real_t>::InitMultipole(PackedData p0, bool own_data){
188 Real_t* data=(Real_t*)p0.data;
189 size_t n=p0.length/sizeof(Real_t);
190 if(n==0) return;
191 if(own_data){
192 // Deep copy, writing through existing storage when the size matches
193 // (upward_equiv may alias node_data_buff; see MPI_Node::Unpack).
194 if((size_t)upward_equiv.Dim()!=n) upward_equiv.ReInit(n);
195 sctl::omp_par::copy(data, data+(sctl::Long)n, upward_equiv.begin());
196 }else{
197 upward_equiv.ReInit(n, sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>(&data[0], n), false);
198 }
199}

201template <class FMMNode>
202FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::~FMM_Pts() {
203 if(mat!=NULL){
204// if(this->sctl_comm.Rank()==0) mat->Save2File("Precomp.data");
205 delete mat;
206 mat=NULL;
207 }
208 {
209 vlist_fft_flag =false;
210 vlist_ifft_flag=false;
211 }
212}

216template <class FMMNode>
217void FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::Initialize(int mult_order, const sctl::Comm& comm_, const Kernel<Real_t>* kernel_){
218 sctl_comm = comm_;
219 sctl::Profile::Tic("InitFMM_Pts",&this->sctl_comm,true);{
220
221 int rank = this->sctl_comm.Rank();
222 bool verbose=false;
223 #ifndef PVFMM_NDEBUG
224 #ifdef SCTL_VERBOSE
225 if(!rank) verbose=true;
226 #endif
227 #endif
228 sctl::Profile::Tic("InitKernel",&this->sctl_comm,false,4);
229 if(kernel_) kernel_->Initialize(verbose);
230 sctl::Profile::Toc();
231
232 multipole_order=mult_order;
233 kernel=kernel_;
234 assert(kernel!=NULL);
235
236 { // Discard data cached for a previous multipole_order, so that Initialize()
237 // can be called again on the same object.
238 if(mat_fname_auto) mat_fname.clear(); // else the old order's precomputed file is reloaded
239 vprecomp_fft_flag=false; // else the FFT plans keep the old transform size
240 vlist_fft_flag =false;
241 vlist_ifft_flag =false;
242 vlist_fft_map .ReInit(0);
243 vlist_ifft_map.ReInit(0);
244 }
245
246 bool save_precomp=false;
247 if (mat) delete mat;
248 mat=new PrecompMat<Real_t>(ScaleInvar());
249 if(this->mat_fname.size()==0){// && !this->ScaleInvar()){
250 std::stringstream st;
251 st<<PVFMM_PRECOMP_DATA_PATH;
252
253 if(!st.str().size()){ // look in PVFMM_DIR
254 char* pvfmm_dir = getenv ("PVFMM_DIR");
255 if(pvfmm_dir) st<<pvfmm_dir;
256 }
257
258 #ifndef STAT_MACROS_BROKEN
259 if(st.str().size()){ // check if the path is a directory
260 struct stat stat_buff;
261 if(stat(st.str().c_str(), &stat_buff) || !S_ISDIR(stat_buff.st_mode)){
262 std::cout<<"error: path not found: "<<st.str()<<'\n';
263 exit(0);
264 }
265 }
266 #endif
267
268 if(st.str().size()) st<<'/';
269 st<<"Precomp_"<<kernel->ker_name.c_str()<<"_m"<<mult_order;
270 if(sizeof(Real_t)==8) st<<"";
271 else if(sizeof(Real_t)==4) st<<"_f";
272 else st<<"_t"<<sizeof(Real_t);
273 st<<".data";
274 this->mat_fname=st.str();
275 this->mat_fname_auto=true;
276 save_precomp=true;
277 }
278 this->mat->LoadFile(mat_fname.c_str(), this->sctl_comm);
279
280 interac_list.Initialize(PVFMM_COORD_DIM, this->mat);
281
282 sctl::Profile::Tic("PrecompUC2UE",&this->sctl_comm,false,4);
283 this->PrecompAll(UC2UE0_Type);
284 this->PrecompAll(UC2UE1_Type);
285 sctl::Profile::Toc();
286
287 sctl::Profile::Tic("PrecompDC2DE",&this->sctl_comm,false,4);
288 this->PrecompAll(DC2DE0_Type);
289 this->PrecompAll(DC2DE1_Type);
290 sctl::Profile::Toc();
291
292 sctl::Profile::Tic("PrecompBC",&this->sctl_comm,false,4);
293 { /*
294 int type=BC_Type;
295 for(int l=0;l<sctl::MAX_DEPTH;l++)
296 for(size_t indx=0;indx<this->interac_list.ListCount((Mat_Type)type);indx++){
297 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& M=this->mat->Mat(l, (Mat_Type)type, indx);
298 M.ReInit(0,0);
299 } // */
300 mat->Mat(0, BC_Type, BoundaryType::BoundaryTypeCount-1);
301 for (int mat_indx = 0; mat_indx < BoundaryType::BoundaryTypeCount; mat_indx++) {
302 Precomp(0, BC_Type, mat_indx);
303 }
304 }
305 sctl::Profile::Toc();
306
307 sctl::Profile::Tic("PrecompU2U",&this->sctl_comm,false,4);
308 this->PrecompAll(U2U_Type);
309 sctl::Profile::Toc();
310
311 sctl::Profile::Tic("PrecompD2D",&this->sctl_comm,false,4);
312 this->PrecompAll(D2D_Type);
313 sctl::Profile::Toc();
314
315 if(save_precomp){
316 sctl::Profile::Tic("Save2File",&this->sctl_comm,false,4);
317 if(!rank){
318 FILE* f=fopen(this->mat_fname.c_str(),"r");
319 if(f==NULL) { //File does not exists.
320 this->mat->Save2File(this->mat_fname.c_str());
321 }else fclose(f);
322 }
323 sctl::Profile::Toc();
324 }
325
326 sctl::Profile::Tic("PrecompV",&this->sctl_comm,false,4);
327 this->PrecompAll(V_Type);
328 sctl::Profile::Toc();
329 sctl::Profile::Tic("PrecompV1",&this->sctl_comm,false,4);
330 this->PrecompAll(V1_Type);
331 sctl::Profile::Toc();
332
333 }sctl::Profile::Toc();
334}
