336template <class Real_t>
337sctl::Permutation<Real_t> equiv_surf_perm(size_t m, size_t p_indx, const sctl::Permutation<Real_t>& ker_perm, const sctl::Vector<Real_t>* scal_exp=NULL){
338 Real_t eps=(Real_t)1e-10;
339 int dof=ker_perm.Dim();
340
341 Real_t c[3]={-0.5,-0.5,-0.5};
342 std::vector<Real_t> trg_coord=d_check_surf(m,c,0);
343 int n_trg=trg_coord.size()/3;
344
345 sctl::Permutation<Real_t> P=sctl::Permutation<Real_t>(n_trg*dof);
346 if(p_indx==ReflecX || p_indx==ReflecY || p_indx==ReflecZ){ // Set P.perm
347 for(int i=0;i<n_trg;i++)
348 for(int j=0;j<n_trg;j++){
349 if(sctl::fabs<Real_t>(trg_coord[i*3+0]-trg_coord[j*3+0]*(p_indx==ReflecX?-1:1))<eps)
350 if(sctl::fabs<Real_t>(trg_coord[i*3+1]-trg_coord[j*3+1]*(p_indx==ReflecY?-1:1))<eps)
351 if(sctl::fabs<Real_t>(trg_coord[i*3+2]-trg_coord[j*3+2]*(p_indx==ReflecZ?-1:1))<eps){
352 for(int k=0;k<dof;k++){
353 P.perm[j*dof+k]=i*dof+ker_perm.perm[k];
354 }
355 }
356 }
357 }else if(p_indx==SwapXY || p_indx==SwapXZ){
358 for(int i=0;i<n_trg;i++)
359 for(int j=0;j<n_trg;j++){
360 if(sctl::fabs<Real_t>(trg_coord[i*3+0]-trg_coord[j*3+(p_indx==SwapXY?1:2)])<eps)
361 if(sctl::fabs<Real_t>(trg_coord[i*3+1]-trg_coord[j*3+(p_indx==SwapXY?0:1)])<eps)
362 if(sctl::fabs<Real_t>(trg_coord[i*3+2]-trg_coord[j*3+(p_indx==SwapXY?2:0)])<eps){
363 for(int k=0;k<dof;k++){
364 P.perm[j*dof+k]=i*dof+ker_perm.perm[k];
365 }
366 }
367 }
368 }else{
369 for(int j=0;j<n_trg;j++){
370 for(int k=0;k<dof;k++){
371 P.perm[j*dof+k]=j*dof+ker_perm.perm[k];
372 }
373 }
374 }
375
376 if(scal_exp && p_indx==Scaling){ // Set level-by-level scaling
377 assert(dof==(int)scal_exp->Dim());
378 sctl::Vector<Real_t> scal(scal_exp->Dim());
379 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<scal.Dim();i++){
380 scal[i]=sctl::pow<Real_t>(2.0,(*scal_exp)[i]);
381 }
382 for(int j=0;j<n_trg;j++){
383 for(int i=0;i<dof;i++){
384 P.scal[j*dof+i]*=scal[i];
385 }
386 }
387 }
388 { // Set P.scal
389 for(int j=0;j<n_trg;j++){
390 for(int i=0;i<dof;i++){
391 P.scal[j*dof+i]*=ker_perm.scal[i];
392 }
393 }
394 }
395
396 return P;
397}
398
399template <class FMMNode>
400sctl::Permutation<typename FMMNode::Real_t>& FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::PrecompPerm(Mat_Type type, Perm_Type perm_indx){
401
402 //Check if the matrix already exists.
403 sctl::Permutation<Real_t>& P_ = mat->Perm((Mat_Type)type, perm_indx);
404 if(P_.Dim()!=0) return P_;
405
406
407 size_t m=this->MultipoleOrder();
408 size_t p_indx=perm_indx % C_Perm;
409
410 //Compute the matrix.
411 sctl::Permutation<Real_t> P;
412 switch (type){
413 case U2U_Type:
414 {
415 sctl::Vector<Real_t> scal_exp;
416 sctl::Permutation<Real_t> ker_perm;
417 if(perm_indx<C_Perm){ // Source permutation
418 ker_perm=kernel->k_m2m->perm_vec[0 +p_indx];
419 scal_exp=kernel->k_m2m->src_scal;
420 }else{ // Target permutation
421 ker_perm=kernel->k_m2m->perm_vec[0 +p_indx];
422 scal_exp=kernel->k_m2m->src_scal;
423 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<scal_exp.Dim();i++) scal_exp[i]=-scal_exp[i];
424 }
425 P=equiv_surf_perm(m, p_indx, ker_perm, (this->ScaleInvar()?&scal_exp:NULL));
426 break;
427 }
428 case D2D_Type:
429 {
430 sctl::Vector<Real_t> scal_exp;
431 sctl::Permutation<Real_t> ker_perm;
432 if(perm_indx<C_Perm){ // Source permutation
433 ker_perm=kernel->k_l2l->perm_vec[C_Perm+p_indx];
434 scal_exp=kernel->k_l2l->trg_scal;
435 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<scal_exp.Dim();i++) scal_exp[i]=-scal_exp[i];
436 }else{ // Target permutation
437 ker_perm=kernel->k_l2l->perm_vec[C_Perm+p_indx];
438 scal_exp=kernel->k_l2l->trg_scal;
439 }
440 P=equiv_surf_perm(m, p_indx, ker_perm, (this->ScaleInvar()?&scal_exp:NULL));
441 break;
442 }
443 default:
444 break;
445 }
446
447 //Save the matrix for future use.
448 #pragma omp critical(PVFMM_PRECOMP_MATRIX_PTS)
449 {
450 if(P_.Dim()==0) P_=P;
451 }
452
453 return P_;
454}
455
456template <class FMMNode>
457sctl::Matrix<typename FMMNode::Real_t>& FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::Precomp(int level, Mat_Type type, size_t mat_indx){
458 if(this->ScaleInvar()) level=0;
459
460 //Check if the matrix already exists.
461 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& M_ = this->mat->Mat(level, type, mat_indx);
462 if(M_.Dim(0)!=0 && M_.Dim(1)!=0) return M_;
463 else{ //Compute matrix from symmetry class (if possible).
464 size_t class_indx = this->interac_list.InteracClass(type, mat_indx);
465 if(class_indx!=mat_indx){
466 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& M0 = this->Precomp(level, type, class_indx);
467 if(M0.Dim(0)==0 || M0.Dim(1)==0) return M_;
468
469 for(size_t i=0;i<Perm_Count;i++) this->PrecompPerm(type, (Perm_Type) i);
470 sctl::Permutation<Real_t>& Pr = this->interac_list.Perm_R(abs(level), type, mat_indx);
471 sctl::Permutation<Real_t>& Pc = this->interac_list.Perm_C(abs(level), type, mat_indx);
472 if(Pr.Dim()>0 && Pc.Dim()>0 && M0.Dim(0)>0 && M0.Dim(1)>0) return M_;
473 }
474 }
475
476 //Compute the matrix.
477 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M;
478 //int omp_p=omp_get_max_threads();
479 switch (type){
480
481 case UC2UE0_Type:
482 {
483 if(MultipoleOrder()==0) break;
484 const int* ker_dim=kernel->k_m2m->ker_dim;
485 // Coord of upward check surface
486 Real_t c[3]={0,0,0};
487 std::vector<Real_t> uc_coord=u_check_surf(MultipoleOrder(),c,level);
488 size_t n_uc=uc_coord.size()/3;
489
490 // Coord of upward equivalent surface
491 std::vector<Real_t> ue_coord=u_equiv_surf(MultipoleOrder(),c,level);
492 size_t n_ue=ue_coord.size()/3;
493
494 // Evaluate potential at check surface due to equivalent surface.
495 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_e2c(n_ue*ker_dim[0],n_uc*ker_dim[1]);
496 kernel->k_m2m->BuildMatrix(&ue_coord[0], n_ue,
497 &uc_coord[0], n_uc, &(M_e2c[0][0]));
498
499 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> U,S,V;
500 M_e2c.SVD(U,S,V);
501 Real_t eps=1, max_S=0;
502 while(eps*(Real_t)0.5+(Real_t)1>1) eps*=(Real_t)0.5;
503 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<std::min(S.Dim(0),S.Dim(1));i++){
504 if(sctl::fabs<Real_t>(S[i][i])>max_S) max_S=sctl::fabs<Real_t>(S[i][i]);
505 }
506 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<S.Dim(0);i++) S[i][i]=(S[i][i]>eps*max_S*4?1/S[i][i]:0);
507 M=V.Transpose()*S;//*U.Transpose();
508 break;
509 }
510 case UC2UE1_Type:
511 {
512 if(MultipoleOrder()==0) break;
513 const int* ker_dim=kernel->k_m2m->ker_dim;
514 // Coord of upward check surface
515 Real_t c[3]={0,0,0};
516 std::vector<Real_t> uc_coord=u_check_surf(MultipoleOrder(),c,level);
517 size_t n_uc=uc_coord.size()/3;
518
519 // Coord of upward equivalent surface
520 std::vector<Real_t> ue_coord=u_equiv_surf(MultipoleOrder(),c,level);
521 size_t n_ue=ue_coord.size()/3;
522
523 // Evaluate potential at check surface due to equivalent surface.
524 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_e2c(n_ue*ker_dim[0],n_uc*ker_dim[1]);
525 kernel->k_m2m->BuildMatrix(&ue_coord[0], n_ue,
526 &uc_coord[0], n_uc, &(M_e2c[0][0]));
527
528 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> U,S,V;
529 M_e2c.SVD(U,S,V);
530 M=U.Transpose();
531 break;
532 }
533 case DC2DE0_Type:
534 {
535 if(MultipoleOrder()==0) break;
536 const int* ker_dim=kernel->k_l2l->ker_dim;
537 // Coord of downward check surface
538 Real_t c[3]={0,0,0};
539 std::vector<Real_t> check_surf=d_check_surf(MultipoleOrder(),c,level);
540 size_t n_ch=check_surf.size()/3;
541
542 // Coord of downward equivalent surface
543 std::vector<Real_t> equiv_surf=d_equiv_surf(MultipoleOrder(),c,level);
544 size_t n_eq=equiv_surf.size()/3;
545
546 // Evaluate potential at check surface due to equivalent surface.
547 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_e2c(n_eq*ker_dim[0],n_ch*ker_dim[1]);
548 kernel->k_l2l->BuildMatrix(&equiv_surf[0], n_eq,
549 &check_surf[0], n_ch, &(M_e2c[0][0]));
550
551 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> U,S,V;
552 M_e2c.SVD(U,S,V);
553 Real_t eps=1, max_S=0;
554 while(eps*(Real_t)0.5+(Real_t)1.0>1.0) eps*=(Real_t)0.5;
555 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<std::min(S.Dim(0),S.Dim(1));i++){
556 if(sctl::fabs<Real_t>(S[i][i])>max_S) max_S=sctl::fabs<Real_t>(S[i][i]);
557 }
558 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<S.Dim(0);i++) S[i][i]=(S[i][i]>eps*max_S*4?1/S[i][i]:0);
559 M=V.Transpose()*S;//*U.Transpose();
560 break;
561 }
562 case DC2DE1_Type:
563 {
564 if(MultipoleOrder()==0) break;
565 const int* ker_dim=kernel->k_l2l->ker_dim;
566 // Coord of downward check surface
567 Real_t c[3]={0,0,0};
568 std::vector<Real_t> check_surf=d_check_surf(MultipoleOrder(),c,level);
569 size_t n_ch=check_surf.size()/3;
570
571 // Coord of downward equivalent surface
572 std::vector<Real_t> equiv_surf=d_equiv_surf(MultipoleOrder(),c,level);
573 size_t n_eq=equiv_surf.size()/3;
574
575 // Evaluate potential at check surface due to equivalent surface.
576 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_e2c(n_eq*ker_dim[0],n_ch*ker_dim[1]);
577 kernel->k_l2l->BuildMatrix(&equiv_surf[0], n_eq,
578 &check_surf[0], n_ch, &(M_e2c[0][0]));
579
580 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> U,S,V;
581 M_e2c.SVD(U,S,V);
582 M=U.Transpose();
583 break;
584 }
585 case U2U_Type:
586 {
587 if(MultipoleOrder()==0) break;
588 const int* ker_dim=kernel->k_m2m->ker_dim;
589 // Coord of upward check surface
590 Real_t c[3]={0,0,0};
591 std::vector<Real_t> check_surf=u_check_surf(MultipoleOrder(),c,level);
592 size_t n_uc=check_surf.size()/3;
593
594 // Coord of child's upward equivalent surface
595 Real_t s=sctl::pow<Real_t>(0.5,(level+2));
596 int* coord=interac_list.RelativeCoord(type,mat_indx);
597 Real_t child_coord[3]={(coord[0]+1)*s,(coord[1]+1)*s,(coord[2]+1)*s};
598 std::vector<Real_t> equiv_surf=u_equiv_surf(MultipoleOrder(),child_coord,level+1);
599 size_t n_ue=equiv_surf.size()/3;
600
601 // Evaluate potential at check surface due to equivalent surface.
602 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_ce2c(n_ue*ker_dim[0],n_uc*ker_dim[1]);
603 kernel->k_m2m->BuildMatrix(&equiv_surf[0], n_ue,
604 &check_surf[0], n_uc, &(M_ce2c[0][0]));
605 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& M_c2e0 = Precomp(level, UC2UE0_Type, 0);
606 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& M_c2e1 = Precomp(level, UC2UE1_Type, 0);
607 M=(M_ce2c*M_c2e0)*M_c2e1;
608 break;
609 }
610 case D2D_Type:
611 {
612 if(MultipoleOrder()==0) break;
613 const int* ker_dim=kernel->k_l2l->ker_dim;
614 // Coord of downward check surface
615 Real_t s=sctl::pow<Real_t>(0.5,level+1);
616 int* coord=interac_list.RelativeCoord(type,mat_indx);
617 Real_t c[3]={(coord[0]+1)*s,(coord[1]+1)*s,(coord[2]+1)*s};
618 std::vector<Real_t> check_surf=d_check_surf(MultipoleOrder(),c,level);
619 size_t n_dc=check_surf.size()/3;
620
621 // Coord of parent's downward equivalent surface
622 Real_t parent_coord[3]={0,0,0};
623 std::vector<Real_t> equiv_surf=d_equiv_surf(MultipoleOrder(),parent_coord,level-1);
624 size_t n_de=equiv_surf.size()/3;
625
626 // Evaluate potential at check surface due to equivalent surface.
627 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_pe2c(n_de*ker_dim[0],n_dc*ker_dim[1]);
628 kernel->k_l2l->BuildMatrix(&equiv_surf[0], n_de,
629 &check_surf[0], n_dc, &(M_pe2c[0][0]));
630 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_c2e0=Precomp(level-1,DC2DE0_Type,0);
631 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_c2e1=Precomp(level-1,DC2DE1_Type,0);
632 if(ScaleInvar()){ // Scale M_c2e0 for level-1
633 sctl::Permutation<Real_t> ker_perm=this->kernel->k_l2l->perm_vec[C_Perm+Scaling];
634 sctl::Vector<Real_t> scal_exp=this->kernel->k_l2l->trg_scal;
635 sctl::Permutation<Real_t> P=equiv_surf_perm(MultipoleOrder(), Scaling, ker_perm, &scal_exp);
636 M_c2e0=P*M_c2e0;
637 }
638 if(ScaleInvar()){ // Scale M_c2e1 for level-1
639 sctl::Permutation<Real_t> ker_perm=this->kernel->k_l2l->perm_vec[0 +Scaling];
640 sctl::Vector<Real_t> scal_exp=this->kernel->k_l2l->src_scal;
641 sctl::Permutation<Real_t> P=equiv_surf_perm(MultipoleOrder(), Scaling, ker_perm, &scal_exp);
642 M_c2e1=M_c2e1*P;
643 }
644 M=M_c2e0*(M_c2e1*M_pe2c);
645 break;
646 }
647 case D2T_Type:
648 {
649 if(MultipoleOrder()==0) break;
650 const int* ker_dim=kernel->k_l2t->ker_dim;
651 std::vector<Real_t>& rel_trg_coord=mat->RelativeTrgCoord();
652
653 // Coord of target points
654 Real_t r=sctl::pow<Real_t>(0.5,level);
655 size_t n_trg=rel_trg_coord.size()/3;
656 std::vector<Real_t> trg_coord(n_trg*3);
657 for(size_t i=0;i<n_trg*PVFMM_COORD_DIM;i++) trg_coord[i]=rel_trg_coord[i]*r;
658
659 // Coord of downward equivalent surface
660 Real_t c[3]={0,0,0};
661 std::vector<Real_t> equiv_surf=d_equiv_surf(MultipoleOrder(),c,level);
662 size_t n_eq=equiv_surf.size()/3;
663
664 // Evaluate potential at target points due to equivalent surface.
665 {
666 M.ReInit(n_eq*ker_dim [0], n_trg*ker_dim [1]);
667 kernel->k_l2t->BuildMatrix(&equiv_surf[0], n_eq, &trg_coord[0], n_trg, &(M [0][0]));
668 }
669 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& M_c2e0=Precomp(level,DC2DE0_Type,0);
670 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& M_c2e1=Precomp(level,DC2DE1_Type,0);
671 M=M_c2e0*(M_c2e1*M);
672 break;
673 }
674 case V_Type:
675 {
676 if(MultipoleOrder()==0) break;
677 const int* ker_dim=kernel->k_m2l->ker_dim;
678 int n1=MultipoleOrder()*2;
679 int n3 =n1*n1*n1;
680 int n3_=n1*n1*(n1/2+1);
681
682 //Compute the matrix.
683 Real_t s=sctl::pow<Real_t>(0.5,level);
684 int* coord2=interac_list.RelativeCoord(type,mat_indx);
685 Real_t coord_diff[3]={coord2[0]*s,coord2[1]*s,coord2[2]*s};
686
687 //Evaluate potential.
688 std::vector<Real_t> r_trg(PVFMM_COORD_DIM,0.0);
689 sctl::Vector<Real_t> conv_poten(n3*ker_dim[0]*ker_dim[1]);
690 std::vector<Real_t> conv_coord=conv_grid(MultipoleOrder(),coord_diff,level);
691 kernel->k_m2l->BuildMatrix(&conv_coord[0],n3,&r_trg[0],1,&conv_poten[0]);
692
693 //Rearrange data: transpose conv_poten in place.
694 MatrixTranspose<Real_t>(n3,ker_dim[0]*ker_dim[1],conv_poten.begin(),conv_poten.begin());
695
696 //Compute FFT.
697 sctl::ScratchBuf<Real_t> fftw_in_scratch ( n3 *ker_dim[0]*ker_dim[1]);
698 sctl::ScratchBuf<Real_t> fftw_out_scratch(2*n3_*ker_dim[0]*ker_dim[1]);
699 Real_t* fftw_in = &fftw_in_scratch .begin()[0];
700 Real_t* fftw_out = &fftw_out_scratch.begin()[0];
701 #pragma omp critical(PVFMM_FFTW_PLAN)
702 {
703 if (!vprecomp_fft_flag){
704 sctl::Vector<sctl::Long> dim_vec(PVFMM_COORD_DIM); dim_vec[0]=n1; dim_vec[1]=n1; dim_vec[2]=n1;
705 vprecomp_fft.Setup(sctl::FFT_Type::R2C, ker_dim[0]*ker_dim[1], dim_vec);
706 vprecomp_fft_flag=true;
707 }
708 }
709
710 sctl::omp_par::memcpy(fftw_in, &conv_poten[0], n3*ker_dim[0]*ker_dim[1]);
711 {
712 sctl::Vector<Real_t> in_ ( n3 *ker_dim[0]*ker_dim[1], fftw_in_scratch.begin(), false);
713 sctl::Vector<Real_t> out_(2*n3_*ker_dim[0]*ker_dim[1], fftw_out_scratch.begin(), false);
714 vprecomp_fft.Execute(in_, out_);
715 }
716 // sctl::FFT is unnormalized (raw FFTW), so no normalization compensation is needed.
717 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_(2*n3_*ker_dim[0]*ker_dim[1],1, sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>((Real_t*)fftw_out, (2*n3_*ker_dim[0]*ker_dim[1])*(1)),false);
718 M=M_;
719 // fftw_in, fftw_out freed automatically at scope exit.
720 break;
721 }
722 case V1_Type:
723 {
724 if(MultipoleOrder()==0) break;
725 const int* ker_dim=kernel->k_m2l->ker_dim;
726 size_t mat_cnt =interac_list.ListCount( V_Type);
727 for(size_t k=0;k<mat_cnt;k++) Precomp(level, V_Type, k);
728
729 constexpr int chld_cnt=1UL<<PVFMM_COORD_DIM;
730 size_t n1=MultipoleOrder()*2;
731 size_t M_dim=n1*n1*(n1/2+1);
732 size_t n3=n1*n1*n1;
733
734 sctl::Vector<Real_t> zero_vec(M_dim*ker_dim[0]*ker_dim[1]*2);
735 zero_vec.SetZero();
736
737 sctl::Vector<Real_t*> M_ptr(chld_cnt*chld_cnt);
738 for(size_t i=0;i<chld_cnt*chld_cnt;i++) M_ptr[i]=&zero_vec[0];
739
740 int* rel_coord_=interac_list.RelativeCoord(V1_Type, mat_indx);
741 for(int j1=0;j1<chld_cnt;j1++)
742 for(int j2=0;j2<chld_cnt;j2++){
743 int rel_coord[3]={rel_coord_[0]*2-(j1/1)%2+(j2/1)%2,
744 rel_coord_[1]*2-(j1/2)%2+(j2/2)%2,
745 rel_coord_[2]*2-(j1/4)%2+(j2/4)%2};
746 for(size_t k=0;k<mat_cnt;k++){
747 int* ref_coord=interac_list.RelativeCoord(V_Type, k);
748 if(ref_coord[0]==rel_coord[0] &&
749 ref_coord[1]==rel_coord[1] &&
750 ref_coord[2]==rel_coord[2]){
751 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& M = this->mat->Mat(level, V_Type, k);
752 M_ptr[j2*chld_cnt+j1]=&M[0][0];
753 break;
754 }
755 }
756 }
757
758 // Build matrix ker_dim0 x ker_dim1 x M_dim x 8 x 8
759 M.ReInit(ker_dim[0]*ker_dim[1]*M_dim, 2*chld_cnt*chld_cnt);
760 for(size_t j=0;j<ker_dim[0]*ker_dim[1]*M_dim;j++){
761 for(size_t k=0;k<chld_cnt*chld_cnt;k++){
762 M[j][k*2+0]=M_ptr[k][j*2+0]/n3;
763 M[j][k*2+1]=M_ptr[k][j*2+1]/n3;
764 }
765 }
766 break;
767 }
768 case W_Type:
769 {
770 if(MultipoleOrder()==0) break;
771 const int* ker_dim=kernel->k_m2t->ker_dim;
772 std::vector<Real_t>& rel_trg_coord=mat->RelativeTrgCoord();
773
774 // Coord of target points
775 Real_t s=sctl::pow<Real_t>(0.5,level);
776 size_t n_trg=rel_trg_coord.size()/3;
777 std::vector<Real_t> trg_coord(n_trg*3);
778 for(size_t j=0;j<n_trg*PVFMM_COORD_DIM;j++) trg_coord[j]=rel_trg_coord[j]*s;
779
780 // Coord of downward equivalent surface
781 int* coord2=interac_list.RelativeCoord(type,mat_indx);
782 Real_t c[3]={(Real_t)((coord2[0]+1)*s*0.25),(Real_t)((coord2[1]+1)*s*0.25),(Real_t)((coord2[2]+1)*s*0.25)};
783 std::vector<Real_t> equiv_surf=u_equiv_surf(MultipoleOrder(),c,level+1);
784 size_t n_eq=equiv_surf.size()/3;
785
786 // Evaluate potential at target points due to equivalent surface.
787 {
788 M.ReInit(n_eq*ker_dim [0],n_trg*ker_dim [1]);
789 kernel->k_m2t->BuildMatrix(&equiv_surf[0], n_eq, &trg_coord[0], n_trg, &(M [0][0]));
790 }
791 break;
792 }
793 case BC_Type:
794 {
795 if(!this->ScaleInvar() || MultipoleOrder()==0) break;
796 if(kernel->k_m2l->ker_dim[0]!=kernel->k_m2m->ker_dim[0]) break;
797 if(kernel->k_m2l->ker_dim[1]!=kernel->k_l2l->ker_dim[1]) break;
798 int ker_dim[2]={kernel->k_m2l->ker_dim[0],kernel->k_m2l->ker_dim[1]};
799 size_t n_surf=(6*(MultipoleOrder()-1)*(MultipoleOrder()-1)+2); //Total number of points.
800
801 if(((size_t)M.Dim(0)!=n_surf*ker_dim[0] || (size_t)M.Dim(1)!=n_surf*ker_dim[1]) && level==0){
802 #ifndef PVFMM_EXTENDED_BC
803 if(PVFMM_BC_LEVELS==0 || mat_indx == BoundaryType::FreeSpace){ // Set M=0 and break;
804 M.ReInit(n_surf*ker_dim[0],n_surf*ker_dim[1]);
805 M.SetZero();
806 break;
807 }
808
809 const auto compute_pinv = [](sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& Minv0, sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& Minv1, const sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& M) {
810 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> U,S,V;
811 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>(M).SVD(U,S,V);
812 Real_t eps=1, max_S=0;
813 while(eps*(Real_t)0.5+(Real_t)1>1) eps*=(Real_t)0.5;
814 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<std::min(S.Dim(0),S.Dim(1));i++){
815 if(sctl::fabs<Real_t>(S[i][i])>max_S) max_S=sctl::fabs<Real_t>(S[i][i]);
816 }
817 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<S.Dim(0);i++) S[i][i]=(S[i][i]>eps*max_S*4?1/S[i][i]:0);
818 Minv0 = V.Transpose() * S;
819 Minv1 = U.Transpose();
820 };
821
822 const auto compute_uc2ue = [this,&compute_pinv](sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& M_uc2ue0, sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& M_uc2ue1, int level, int mult_order) {
823 int ker_dim[2] = {kernel->k_m2m->ker_dim[0], kernel->k_m2m->ker_dim[1]};
824 const size_t n_surf = 6 * (mult_order-1) * (mult_order-1) + 2;
825
826 const Real_t box_length = sctl::pow<Real_t>((Real_t)2, level);
827 Real_t trg_box[3] = {-box_length/2, -box_length/2, -box_length/2};
828 std::vector<Real_t> equiv_coord = u_equiv_surf(mult_order, trg_box, level);
829 std::vector<Real_t> check_coord = u_check_surf(mult_order, trg_box, level);
830
831 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_ue2uc(n_surf*ker_dim[0], n_surf*ker_dim[1]);
832 kernel->k_m2m->BuildMatrix(&equiv_coord[0], n_surf, &check_coord[0], n_surf, &(M_ue2uc[0][0]));
833 compute_pinv(M_uc2ue0, M_uc2ue1, M_ue2uc);
834 };
835 const auto compute_dc2de = [this,&compute_pinv](sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& M_dc2de0, sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& M_dc2de1, int level, int mult_order) {
836 int ker_dim[2] = {kernel->k_l2l->ker_dim[0], kernel->k_l2l->ker_dim[1]};
837 const size_t n_surf = 6 * (mult_order-1) * (mult_order-1) + 2;
838
839 const Real_t box_length = sctl::pow<Real_t>((Real_t)2, level);
840 Real_t trg_box[3] = {-box_length/2, -box_length/2, -box_length/2};
841 std::vector<Real_t> equiv_coord = d_equiv_surf(mult_order, trg_box, level);
842 std::vector<Real_t> check_coord = d_check_surf(mult_order, trg_box, level);
843
844 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_de2dc(n_surf*ker_dim[0], n_surf*ker_dim[1]);
845 kernel->k_l2l->BuildMatrix(&equiv_coord[0], n_surf, &check_coord[0], n_surf, &(M_de2dc[0][0]));
846 compute_pinv(M_dc2de0, M_dc2de1, M_de2dc);
847 };
848
849 const auto compute_bc_matrix = [&compute_uc2ue,&compute_dc2de,&mat_indx](const Kernel<Real_t>* kernel, int mult_order0, int mult_upsample) {
850 const int mult_order = mult_order0 + mult_upsample;
851
852 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_uc2ue0, M_uc2ue1;
853 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_uc2ue0_, M_uc2ue1_;
854 compute_uc2ue(M_uc2ue0, M_uc2ue1, 0, mult_order);
855 compute_uc2ue(M_uc2ue0_, M_uc2ue1_, -1, mult_order);
856
857 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_dc2de0, M_dc2de1;
858 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_dc2de0_, M_dc2de1_;
859 compute_dc2de(M_dc2de0, M_dc2de1, 0, mult_order);
860 compute_dc2de(M_dc2de0_, M_dc2de1_, -1, mult_order);
861
862 const size_t n_surf = 6 * (mult_order-1) * (mult_order-1) + 2;
863
864 const auto compute_equiv_zero_proj = [&kernel,&mult_order,&n_surf,&M_uc2ue0,&M_uc2ue1]() { // Set average multipole charge to zero (projection for non-zero total source density)
865 int ker_dim[2] = {kernel->k_m2m->ker_dim[0], kernel->k_m2m->ker_dim[1]};
866
867 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_s2c;
868 { // Compute M_s2c
869 M_s2c.ReInit(ker_dim[0],n_surf*ker_dim[1]);
870 std::vector<Real_t> uc_coord;
871 { // Coord of upward check surface
872 Real_t c[3]={0,0,0};
873 uc_coord=u_check_surf(mult_order,c,0);
874 }
875 #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic)
876 for(size_t i=0;i<n_surf;i++){
877 std::vector<Real_t> M_=cheb_integ<Real_t>(0, &uc_coord[i*3], 1.0, *kernel->k_m2m);
878 for(int j=0; j<ker_dim[0]; j++)
879 for(int k=0; k<ker_dim[1]; k++)
880 M_s2c[j][i*ker_dim[1]+k] = M_[j+k*ker_dim[0]];
881 }
882 }
883
884 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_s2e=(M_s2c*M_uc2ue0)*M_uc2ue1;
885 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<M_s2e.Dim(0);i++){ // Normalize each row to 1
886 Real_t s=0;
887 for(sctl::Long j=0;j<M_s2e.Dim(1);j++) s+=M_s2e[i][j];
888 s=1/s;
889 for(sctl::Long j=0;j<M_s2e.Dim(1);j++) M_s2e[i][j]*=s;
890 }
891
892 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_equiv_zero_avg(n_surf*ker_dim[0],n_surf*ker_dim[0]);
893 M_equiv_zero_avg.SetZero();
894 for(size_t i=0;i<n_surf*ker_dim[0];i++)
895 M_equiv_zero_avg[i][i]=1;
896 for(size_t i=0;i<n_surf;i++)
897 for(int k=0;k<ker_dim[0];k++)
898 for(size_t j=0;j<n_surf*ker_dim[0];j++)
899 M_equiv_zero_avg[i*ker_dim[0]+k][j]-=M_s2e[k][j];
900 return M_equiv_zero_avg;
901 };
902 const auto compute_check_zero_proj = [&kernel,&n_surf]() { // Set average check potential to zero. (improves stability for large PVFMM_BC_LEVELS)
903 int ker_dim1 = kernel->k_l2l->ker_dim[1];
904 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_check_zero_avg(n_surf*ker_dim1,n_surf*ker_dim1);
905 M_check_zero_avg.SetZero();
906 for(size_t i=0;i<n_surf*ker_dim1;i++)
907 M_check_zero_avg[i][i]+=1;
908 for(size_t i=0;i<n_surf;i++)
909 for(size_t j=0;j<n_surf;j++)
910 for(int k=0;k<ker_dim1;k++)
911 M_check_zero_avg[i*ker_dim1+k][j*ker_dim1+k]-=1/(Real_t)n_surf;
912 return M_check_zero_avg;
913 };
914 auto M_equiv_zero_avg = compute_equiv_zero_proj();
915 auto M_check_zero_avg = compute_check_zero_proj();
916
917 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M2M, L2L, M2L;
918 { // Set M2M
919 int ker_dim[2] = {kernel->k_m2m->ker_dim[0], kernel->k_m2m->ker_dim[1]};
920 Real_t box_length = (Real_t)1;
921 Real_t trg_box[3] = {-box_length, -box_length, -box_length};
922 std::vector<Real_t> check_coord = u_check_surf(mult_order, trg_box, -1);
923
924 for (int k0 = 0; k0 < 2; k0++) {
925 for (int k1 = 0; k1 < 2; k1++) {
926 for (int k2 = 0; k2 < 2; k2++) {
927 if (mat_indx == BoundaryType::PX ) if (k1 != 0 || k2 != 0) continue;
928 if (mat_indx == BoundaryType::PXY) if (k2 != 0) continue;
929
930 Real_t src_box[3] = {trg_box[0] + k0*box_length,
931 trg_box[1] + k1*box_length,
932 trg_box[2] + k2*box_length};
933
934 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M0(n_surf*ker_dim[0], n_surf*ker_dim[1]);
935 std::vector<Real_t> src_coord = u_equiv_surf(mult_order, src_box, 0);
936 kernel->k_m2m->BuildMatrix(&src_coord[0], n_surf, &check_coord[0], n_surf, &(M0[0][0]));
937
938 if (M2M.Dim(0) == 0) M2M = M0;
939 else M2M += M0;
940 }
941 }
942 }
943 M2M = (M2M * M_uc2ue0_) * M_uc2ue1_;
944 }
945 { // Set L2L
946 int ker_dim[2] = {kernel->k_l2l->ker_dim[0], kernel->k_l2l->ker_dim[1]};
947 Real_t box_length = (Real_t)1;
948 Real_t trg_box[3] = {-box_length, -box_length, -box_length};
949 std::vector<Real_t> equiv_coord = d_equiv_surf(mult_order, trg_box, -1);
950 std::vector<Real_t> child_check_coord = d_check_surf(mult_order, trg_box, 0);
951
952 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> MM(n_surf*ker_dim[0], n_surf*ker_dim[1]);
953 kernel->k_l2l->BuildMatrix(&equiv_coord[0], n_surf, &child_check_coord[0], n_surf, &(MM[0][0]));
954 L2L = M_dc2de0_ * (M_dc2de1_ * MM);
955 }
956 { // Set M2L
957 int ker_dim[2] = {kernel->k_m2l->ker_dim[0], kernel->k_m2l->ker_dim[1]};
958 Real_t root_coord[3]={0,0,0};
959 const Real_t box_length = (Real_t)(1UL << 0);
960 std::vector<Real_t> dn_check_coord = d_check_surf(mult_order, root_coord, 0);
961
962 for (int k0 = -2; k0 < 4; k0++) {
963 for (int k1 = -2; k1 < 4; k1++) {
964 for (int k2 = -2; k2 < 4; k2++) {
965 if (mat_indx == BoundaryType::PX ) if (k1 != 0 || k2 != 0) continue;
966 if (mat_indx == BoundaryType::PXY) if (k2 != 0) continue;
967 if (abs(k0) <= 1 && abs(k1) <= 1 && abs(k2) <= 1) continue;
968
969 Real_t src_box[3] = {root_coord[0] + k0*box_length,
970 root_coord[1] + k1*box_length,
971 root_coord[2] + k2*box_length};
972
973 std::vector<Real_t> src_coord = u_equiv_surf(mult_order, src_box, 0);
974 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M0(src_coord.size()/PVFMM_COORD_DIM*ker_dim[0], dn_check_coord.size()/PVFMM_COORD_DIM*ker_dim[1]);
975 kernel->k_m2l->BuildMatrix(&src_coord[0], src_coord.size()/PVFMM_COORD_DIM, &dn_check_coord[0], dn_check_coord.size()/PVFMM_COORD_DIM, &(M0[0][0]));
976
977 if (M2L.Dim(0) == 0) M2L = M0;
978 else M2L += M0;
979 }
980 }
981 }
982 }
983 M2M = M_equiv_zero_avg * M2M * M_equiv_zero_avg;
984 L2L = M_check_zero_avg * L2L * M_check_zero_avg;
985 M2L = M_equiv_zero_avg * M2L * M_check_zero_avg;
986
987 sctl::Permutation<Real_t> Pr, Pc; // scaling for next level
988 { // Set Pr
989 sctl::Permutation<Real_t> ker_perm = kernel->k_m2l->perm_vec[0 + Scaling];
990 sctl::Vector<Real_t> scal_exp = kernel->k_m2l->src_scal;
991 for(sctl::Long i = 0; i < scal_exp.Dim(); i++) scal_exp[i] = -scal_exp[i];
992 Pr = equiv_surf_perm(mult_order, Scaling, ker_perm, &scal_exp);
993 }
994 { // Set Pc
995 sctl::Permutation<Real_t> ker_perm = kernel->k_m2l->perm_vec[C_Perm + Scaling];
996 sctl::Vector<Real_t> scal_exp = kernel->k_m2l->trg_scal;
997 for(sctl::Long i = 0; i < scal_exp.Dim(); i++) scal_exp[i] = -scal_exp[i];
998 Pc = equiv_surf_perm(mult_order, Scaling, ker_perm, &scal_exp);
999 }
1000
1001 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M = M2L;
1002 for (int level = 1; level < PVFMM_BC_LEVELS; level++) M = M2L + M2M * (Pr * M * Pc) * L2L;
1003
1004 if (mat_indx == BoundaryType::PXYZ && kernel->k_m2l->vol_poten && kernel->k_m2t->vol_poten) { // Correction for far-field of analytical volume potential
1005 int ker_dim[2] = {kernel->k_m2l->ker_dim[0], kernel->k_m2l->ker_dim[1]};
1006 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_far;
1007 { // Compute M_far
1008 // kernel->k_m2l->vol_poten is the analytical particular solution for uniform source density=1
1009 // We already corrected far-field above with M_equiv_zero_avg, so we don't need the far field of the analytical solutions.
1010 // We take the analytical solution and subtract the near interaction (3x3x3 boxes) from it to get the far-field
1011 // Then, we add the far-field correction for the analytical solution to be subtracted later.
1012
1013 std::vector<Real_t> dc_coord;
1014 { // Coord of upward check surface
1015 Real_t c[3]={1.0,1.0,1.0};
1016 dc_coord=d_check_surf(mult_order,c,0);
1017 }
1018 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_near(ker_dim[0],n_surf*ker_dim[1]);
1019 #pragma omp parallel for schedule(dynamic)
1020 for(size_t i=0;i<n_surf;i++){ // Compute near-interaction part
1021 std::vector<Real_t> M_=cheb_integ<Real_t>(0, &dc_coord[i*3], 3.0, *kernel->k_m2l);
1022 for(int j=0; j<ker_dim[0]; j++)
1023 for(int k=0; k<ker_dim[1]; k++)
1024 M_near[j][i*ker_dim[1]+k] = M_[j+k*ker_dim[0]];
1025 }
1026 { // M_far = M_analytic - M_near
1027 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_analytic(ker_dim[0],n_surf*ker_dim[1]); M_analytic.SetZero();
1028 kernel->k_m2l->vol_poten(&dc_coord[0],n_surf,&M_analytic[0][0]);
1029 M_far=M_analytic-M_near;
1030 }
1031 }
1032 { // Add far-field correction to M
1033 for(size_t i=0;i<n_surf;i++)
1034 for(int k=0;k<ker_dim[0];k++)
1035 for(size_t j=0;j<n_surf*ker_dim[1];j++)
1036 M[i*ker_dim[0]+k][j]+=M_far[k][j];
1037 }
1038 }
1039
1040 const auto compute_corner_correction = [&kernel,&mult_order,&mat_indx,&n_surf,&M_dc2de0,&M_dc2de1](sctl::Matrix<Real_t> Mbc) {
1041 // Mbc: matrix from upward equivalent density to downward check potential (n_surf*ker_dim[0] x n_surf*ker_dim[1])
1042 //
1043 // Fit a polynomial a+bx+cy+dz+exy+fxz+gyz+hxyz to the potential
1044 // values on the corners of a cube. Evaluate that polynomial (with
1045 // selected terms enabled) at points on the check surface. This
1046 // matrix must be subtracted from the BC matrix to correct for the
1047 // corner values.
1048
1049 constexpr int n_corner = 8;
1050 std::array<int,n_corner> enable_flag = {0,0,0,0,0,0,0,0}; // array indicating which correction terms to enable
1051 if (mat_indx == BoundaryType::PXYZ) enable_flag = {1,1,1,1,0,0,0,0};
1052 else if (mat_indx == BoundaryType::PXY) enable_flag = {1,1,1,0,0,0,0,0};
1053 else if (mat_indx == BoundaryType::PX) enable_flag = {1,1,0,0,0,0,0,0};
1054
1055 std::vector<Real_t> corner_pts(n_corner*PVFMM_COORD_DIM);
1056 for (int i = 0; i < n_corner; i++) {
1057 corner_pts[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+0] = ( i %2);
1058 corner_pts[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+1] = ((i/2)%2);
1059 corner_pts[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+2] = ((i/4)%2);
1060 }
1061
1062 Real_t c[3]={0,0,0}; // Coord of downward equivalent surface
1063 std::vector<Real_t> up_equiv_surf=u_equiv_surf(mult_order,c,0);
1064 std::vector<Real_t> dn_equiv_surf=d_equiv_surf(mult_order,c,0);
1065 std::vector<Real_t> dn_check_surf=d_check_surf(mult_order,c,0);
1066 int ker_dim[2] = {kernel->k_m2l->ker_dim[0], kernel->k_m2l->ker_dim[1]};
1067
1068 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> corner_vals; // matrix with rows containing corner values
1069 { // Evaluate potential at corner due to upward and dnward equivalent surface.
1070 { // Potential from local expansion.
1071 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_e2pt(n_surf * kernel->k_l2l->ker_dim[0], n_corner * kernel->k_l2l->ker_dim[1]);
1072 kernel->k_l2l->BuildMatrix(&dn_equiv_surf[0], n_surf, &corner_pts[0], n_corner, &(M_e2pt[0][0]));
1073 corner_vals = (Mbc * M_dc2de0) * (M_dc2de1 * M_e2pt);
1074 }
1075 for(size_t k = 0; k < n_corner; k++) { // Potential from colleagues of root.
1076 for(int j0 = -1; j0 <= 1; j0++)
1077 for(int j1 = -1; j1 <= 1; j1++)
1078 for(int j2 = -1; j2 <= 1; j2++) {
1079 if (mat_indx == BoundaryType::PXY && (j2 != 0)) continue;
1080 if (mat_indx == BoundaryType::PX && (j1 != 0 || j2 != 0)) continue;
1081
1082 Real_t pt_coord[3] = {corner_pts[k*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+0]-j0,
1083 corner_pts[k*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+1]-j1,
1084 corner_pts[k*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+2]-j2};
1085 if (sctl::fabs(pt_coord[0]-0.5)<1 && sctl::fabs(pt_coord[1]-0.5)<1 && sctl::fabs(pt_coord[2]-0.5)<1) continue;
1086
1087 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_e2pt(n_surf * ker_dim[0], ker_dim[1]);
1088 kernel->k_m2l->BuildMatrix(&up_equiv_surf[0], n_surf, &pt_coord[0], 1, &(M_e2pt[0][0]));
1089 for(sctl::Long i = 0; i < M_e2pt.Dim(0); i++)
1090 for(sctl::Long j = 0; j < M_e2pt.Dim(1); j++)
1091 corner_vals[i][k*ker_dim[1]+j] += M_e2pt[i][j];
1092 }
1093 }
1094 if (mat_indx == BoundaryType::PXYZ && kernel->k_m2l->vol_poten && kernel->k_m2t->vol_poten) { // Subtract analytical vol_poten at corners
1095 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_analytic(ker_dim[0], n_corner*ker_dim[1]); M_analytic.SetZero();
1096 kernel->k_m2l->vol_poten(&corner_pts[0], n_corner, &M_analytic[0][0]);
1097 for (size_t j = 0; j < n_surf; j++)
1098 for (int k = 0; k < ker_dim[0]; k++)
1099 for (sctl::Long i = 0; i < corner_vals.Dim(1); i++)
1100 corner_vals[j*ker_dim[0]+k][i] -= M_analytic[k][i];
1101 }
1102 }
1103
1104 constexpr int n_coeff = 8;
1105 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> V(n_coeff, n_corner), V_pinv;
1106 for (int i = 0; i < (int)n_corner; i++) { // Set Vandermonde matrix V
1107 V[0][i] = 1;
1108 V[1][i] = corner_pts[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+0];
1109 V[2][i] = corner_pts[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+1];
1110 V[3][i] = corner_pts[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+2];
1111 V[4][i] = corner_pts[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+0] * corner_pts[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+1];
1112 V[5][i] = corner_pts[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+0] * corner_pts[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+2];
1113 V[6][i] = corner_pts[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+1] * corner_pts[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+2];
1114 V[7][i] = corner_pts[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+0] * corner_pts[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+1] * corner_pts[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+2];
1115 }
1116 { // Compute V_pinv
1117 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> V_(V.Dim(0), V.Dim(1));
1118 for (int i = 0; i < (int)(V.Dim(0) * V.Dim(1)); i++) V_[0][i] = V[0][i];
1119 auto V_pinv_ = V_.pinv(0);
1120 V_pinv.ReInit(V_pinv_.Dim(0), V_pinv_.Dim(1), sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>(&V_pinv_[0][0], V_pinv_.Dim(0)*V_pinv_.Dim(1)));
1121 }
1122
1123 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_coeff; // (ker_dim[1] * n_surf * ker_dim[0], n_coeff)
1124 { // Compute coefficients for correction terms
1125 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> corner_vals_(n_surf * ker_dim[0] * n_corner, ker_dim[1], sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>((Real_t*)&corner_vals[0][0], (n_surf * ker_dim[0] * n_corner)*(ker_dim[1])));
1126 corner_vals_ = corner_vals_.Transpose();
1127 M_coeff = sctl::Matrix<Real_t>(ker_dim[1] * n_surf * ker_dim[0], n_corner, corner_vals_.begin(), false) * V_pinv;
1128 }
1129
1130 if (0) { // for debugging: print max of absolute values of each coefficient
1131 for (size_t j = 0; j < M_coeff.Dim(1); j++) {
1132 Real_t max_val = 0;
1133 for (size_t i = 0; i < M_coeff.Dim(0); i++) {
1134 max_val = std::max<Real_t>(max_val, fabs(M_coeff[i][j]));
1135 }
1136 std::cout << max_val << " ";
1137 }
1138 std::cout<<std::endl;
1139 }
1140
1141 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> CorrecVecs(n_coeff, n_surf);
1142 { // Map each coefficient to the correction vector
1143 for (int i = 0; i < (int)n_surf; i++) {
1144 CorrecVecs[0][i] = enable_flag[0] * 1;
1145 CorrecVecs[1][i] = enable_flag[1] * dn_check_surf[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+0];
1146 CorrecVecs[2][i] = enable_flag[2] * dn_check_surf[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+1];
1147 CorrecVecs[3][i] = enable_flag[3] * dn_check_surf[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+2];
1148 CorrecVecs[4][i] = enable_flag[4] * dn_check_surf[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+0]*dn_check_surf[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+1];
1149 CorrecVecs[5][i] = enable_flag[5] * dn_check_surf[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+0]*dn_check_surf[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+2];
1150 CorrecVecs[6][i] = enable_flag[6] * dn_check_surf[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+1]*dn_check_surf[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+2];
1151 CorrecVecs[7][i] = enable_flag[7] * dn_check_surf[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+0]*dn_check_surf[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+1]*dn_check_surf[i*PVFMM_COORD_DIM+2];
1152 }
1153 }
1154
1155 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_corr; // (n_surf * ker_dim[0], n_surf * ker_dim[1])
1156 { // Compute correction matrix
1157 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M0 = M_coeff * CorrecVecs;
1158 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M1(ker_dim[1], n_surf * ker_dim[0] * n_surf, M0.begin());
1159 M1 = M1.Transpose();
1160 M_corr.ReInit(n_surf * ker_dim[0], n_surf * ker_dim[1], M1.begin());
1161 }
1162 return M_corr;
1163 };
1164 M -= compute_corner_correction(M);
1165
1166 if (mult_upsample) { // downsample to mult_order0
1167 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M2M, L2L;
1168 { // Set M2M
1169 Real_t box_length = (Real_t)1;
1170 Real_t root_coord[3] = {-box_length/2, -box_length/2, -box_length/2};
1171 std::vector<Real_t> src_coord = u_equiv_surf(mult_order0, root_coord, 0);
1172 std::vector<Real_t> check_coord = u_check_surf(mult_order, root_coord, 0);
1173
1174 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M0(src_coord.size()/PVFMM_COORD_DIM*kernel->k_m2m->ker_dim[0], check_coord.size()/PVFMM_COORD_DIM*kernel->k_m2m->ker_dim[1]);
1175 kernel->k_m2m->BuildMatrix(&src_coord[0], src_coord.size()/PVFMM_COORD_DIM, &check_coord[0], check_coord.size()/PVFMM_COORD_DIM, &(M0[0][0]));
1176 M2M = (M0 * M_uc2ue0) * M_uc2ue1;
1177 }
1178 { // Set L2L
1179 Real_t box_length = (Real_t)1;
1180 Real_t root_coord[3] = {-box_length/2, -box_length/2, -box_length/2};
1181 std::vector<Real_t> src_coord = d_equiv_surf(mult_order, root_coord, 0);
1182 std::vector<Real_t> check_coord = d_check_surf(mult_order0, root_coord, 0);
1183
1184 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M0(src_coord.size()/PVFMM_COORD_DIM*kernel->k_l2l->ker_dim[0], check_coord.size()/PVFMM_COORD_DIM*kernel->k_l2l->ker_dim[1]);
1185 kernel->k_l2l->BuildMatrix(&src_coord[0], src_coord.size()/PVFMM_COORD_DIM, &check_coord[0], check_coord.size()/PVFMM_COORD_DIM, &(M0[0][0]));
1186 L2L = M_dc2de0 * (M_dc2de1 * M0);
1187 }
1188 M = M2M * M * L2L;
1189 }
1190 return M;
1191 };
1192 M = compute_bc_matrix(kernel, MultipoleOrder(), 2);
1193 #else
1194 { // Compute M
1195 M.ReInit(n_surf*ker_dim[0], n_surf*ker_dim[1]); M.SetZero();
1196
1197 Real_t dc_coord[3]={0,0,0};
1198 std::vector<Real_t> trg_coord=d_check_surf(MultipoleOrder(), dc_coord, 0);
1199
1200 int xlow,xhigh,ylow,yhigh,zlow,zhigh;
1201 switch(mat_indx){
1202 case BoundaryType::PX :
1203 xlow=-2;xhigh=2;
1204 ylow=0;yhigh=0;
1205 zlow=0;zhigh=0;
1206 break;
1207 case BoundaryType::PXY :
1208 xlow=-2;xhigh=2;
1209 ylow=-2;yhigh=2;
1210 zlow=0;zhigh=0;
1211 break;
1212 case BoundaryType::PXYZ :
1213 xlow=-2;xhigh=2;
1214 ylow=-2;yhigh=2;
1215 zlow=-2;zhigh=2;
1216 break;
1217 default:
1218 xlow=0;xhigh=0;ylow=0;yhigh=0;zlow=0;zhigh=0;
1219 break;
1220 }
1221
1222 for(int x0=xlow;x0<=xhigh;x0++)
1223 for(int x1=ylow;x1<=yhigh;x1++)
1224 for(int x2=zlow;x2<=zhigh;x2++)
1225 if(abs(x0)>1 || abs(x1)>1 || abs(x2)>1){
1226 Real_t ue_coord[3]={(Real_t)x0, (Real_t)x1, (Real_t)x2};
1227 std::vector<Real_t> src_coord=u_equiv_surf(MultipoleOrder(), ue_coord, 0);
1228
1229 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_tmp(n_surf*ker_dim[0], n_surf*ker_dim[1]);
1230 kernel->k_m2l->BuildMatrix(&src_coord[0], n_surf,
1231 &trg_coord[0], n_surf, &(M_tmp[0][0]));
1232 M+=M_tmp;
1233 }
1234 }
1235 #endif
1236 }
1237
1238 break;
1239 }
1240 default:
1241 break;
1242 }
1243
1244 //Save the matrix for future use.
1245 #pragma omp critical(PVFMM_PRECOMP_MATRIX_PTS)
1246 if(M_.Dim(0)==0 && M_.Dim(1)==0){
1247 M_=M;
1248 /*
1249 M_.ReInit(M.Dim(0),M.Dim(1));
1250 int dof=ker_dim[0]*ker_dim[1];
1251 for(int j=0;j<dof;j++){
1252 size_t a=(M.Dim(0)*M.Dim(1)* j )/dof;
1253 size_t b=(M.Dim(0)*M.Dim(1)*(j+1))/dof;
1254 #pragma omp parallel for // NUMA
1255 for(int tid=0;tid<omp_p;tid++){
1256 size_t a_=a+((b-a)* tid )/omp_p;
1257 size_t b_=a+((b-a)*(tid+1))/omp_p;
1258 sctl::omp_par::memcpy(&M_[0][a_], &M[0][a_], b_-a_);
1259 }
1260 }
1261 */
1262 }
1263
1264 return M_;
1265}
1266
1267template <class FMMNode>
1268void FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::PrecompAll(Mat_Type type, int level){
1269 if(level==-1){
1270 for(int l=0;l<sctl::MAX_DEPTH;l++){
1271 PrecompAll(type, l);
1272 }
1273 return;
1274 }
1275
1276 //Compute basic permutations.
1277 for(size_t i=0;i<Perm_Count;i++)
1278 this->PrecompPerm(type, (Perm_Type) i);
1279
1280 {
1281 //Allocate matrices.
1282 size_t mat_cnt=interac_list.ListCount((Mat_Type)type);
1283 mat->Mat(level, (Mat_Type)type, mat_cnt-1);
1284
1285 { // Compute InteracClass matrices.
1286 std::vector<size_t> indx_lst;
1287 for(size_t i=0; i<mat_cnt; i++){
1288 if(interac_list.InteracClass((Mat_Type)type,i)==i)
1289 indx_lst.push_back(i);
1290 }
1291
1292 //Compute Transformations.
1293 //#pragma omp parallel for //lets use fine grained parallelism
1294 for(size_t i=0; i<indx_lst.size(); i++){
1295 Precomp(level, (Mat_Type)type, indx_lst[i]);
1296 }
1297 }
1298
1299 //#pragma omp parallel for //lets use fine grained parallelism
1300 for(size_t mat_indx=0;mat_indx<mat_cnt;mat_indx++){
1301 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& M0=interac_list.ClassMat(level,(Mat_Type)type,mat_indx);
1302 sctl::Permutation<Real_t>& pr=interac_list.Perm_R(abs(level), (Mat_Type)type, mat_indx);
1303 sctl::Permutation<Real_t>& pc=interac_list.Perm_C(abs(level), (Mat_Type)type, mat_indx);
1304 if(pr.Dim()!=M0.Dim(0) || pc.Dim()!=M0.Dim(1)) Precomp(level, (Mat_Type)type, mat_indx);
1305 }
1306 }
1307}
1308
1309template <class FMMNode>
1310void FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::CollectNodeData(FMMTree_t* tree, std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode>>& node, std::vector<sctl::Matrix<Real_t> >& buff_list, std::vector<sctl::Vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>> >& n_list, std::vector<std::vector<sctl::Vector<Real_t>* > > vec_list){
1311 if(buff_list.size()<7) buff_list.resize(7);
1312 if(tree->node_data_buff_mirror.size()<buff_list.size()) tree->node_data_buff_mirror.resize(buff_list.size());
1313 if( n_list.size()<7) n_list.resize(7);
1314 if( vec_list.size()<7) vec_list.resize(7);
1315
1316 if(node.size()==0) return;
1317 {// 0. upward_equiv
1318 int indx=0;
1319
1320 size_t vec_sz;
1321 { // Set vec_sz
1322 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& M_uc2ue = this->interac_list.ClassMat(0, UC2UE1_Type, 0);
1323 vec_sz=M_uc2ue.Dim(1);
1324 }
1325
1326 std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>> node_lst;
1327 {// Construct node_lst
1328 node_lst.clear();
1329 std::vector<std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>> node_lst_(sctl::MAX_DEPTH+1);
1330 sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t> r_node=sctl::NullIterator<FMMNode_t>();
1331 for(size_t i=0;i<node.size();i++){
1332 if(!node[i]->IsLeaf()){
1333 node_lst_[node[i]->Depth()].push_back(node[i]);
1334 }else{
1335 node[i]->pt_cnt[0]+=node[i]-> src_coord.Dim()/PVFMM_COORD_DIM;
1336 node[i]->pt_cnt[0]+=node[i]->surf_coord.Dim()/PVFMM_COORD_DIM;
1337 if(node[i]->IsGhost()) node[i]->pt_cnt[0]++; // TODO: temporary fix, pt_cnt not known for ghost nodes
1338 }
1339 if(node[i]->Depth()==0) r_node=node[i];
1340 }
1341 size_t chld_cnt=1UL<<PVFMM_COORD_DIM;
1342 for(int i=sctl::MAX_DEPTH;i>=0;i--){
1343 for(size_t j=0;j<node_lst_[i].size();j++){
1344 for(size_t k=0;k<chld_cnt;k++){
1345 sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t> node=(sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>)node_lst_[i][j]->Child(k);
1346 node_lst_[i][j]->pt_cnt[0]+=node->pt_cnt[0];
1347 }
1348 }
1349 }
1350 for(int i=0;i<=sctl::MAX_DEPTH;i++){
1351 for(size_t j=0;j<node_lst_[i].size();j++){
1352 if(node_lst_[i][j]->pt_cnt[0]){
1353 for(size_t k=0;k<chld_cnt;k++){
1354 sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t> node=(sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>)node_lst_[i][j]->Child(k);
1355 node_lst.push_back(node);
1356 }
1357 }else{
1358 for(size_t k=0;k<chld_cnt;k++){
1359 sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t> node=(sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>)node_lst_[i][j]->Child(k);
1360 node->FMMData()->upward_equiv.ReInit(0);
1361 }
1362 }
1363 }
1364 }
1365 if(r_node!=sctl::NullIterator<FMMNode_t>()) node_lst.push_back(r_node);
1366 n_list[indx]=node_lst;
1367 }
1368
1369 std::vector<sctl::Vector<Real_t>*>& vec_lst=vec_list[indx];
1370 for(size_t i=0;i<node_lst.size();i++){ // Construct vec_lst
1371 sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t> node=node_lst[i];
1372 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& data_vec=node->FMMData()->upward_equiv;
1373 data_vec.ReInit(vec_sz,sctl::NullIterator<Real_t>(),false);
1374 vec_lst.push_back(&data_vec);
1375 }
1376 }
1377 {// 1. dnward_equiv
1378 int indx=1;
1379
1380 size_t vec_sz;
1381 { // Set vec_sz
1382 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& M_dc2de0 = this->interac_list.ClassMat(0, DC2DE0_Type, 0);
1383 vec_sz=M_dc2de0.Dim(0);
1384 }
1385
1386 std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>> node_lst;
1387 {// Construct node_lst
1388 node_lst.clear();
1389 std::vector<std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>> node_lst_(sctl::MAX_DEPTH+1);
1390 sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t> r_node=sctl::NullIterator<FMMNode_t>();
1391 for(size_t i=0;i<node.size();i++){
1392 if(!node[i]->IsLeaf()){
1393 node_lst_[node[i]->Depth()].push_back(node[i]);
1394 }else{
1395 node[i]->pt_cnt[1]+=node[i]->trg_coord.Dim()/PVFMM_COORD_DIM;
1396 }
1397 if(node[i]->Depth()==0) r_node=node[i];
1398 }
1399 size_t chld_cnt=1UL<<PVFMM_COORD_DIM;
1400 for(int i=sctl::MAX_DEPTH;i>=0;i--){
1401 for(size_t j=0;j<node_lst_[i].size();j++){
1402 for(size_t k=0;k<chld_cnt;k++){
1403 sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t> node=(sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>)node_lst_[i][j]->Child(k);
1404 node_lst_[i][j]->pt_cnt[1]+=node->pt_cnt[1];
1405 }
1406 }
1407 }
1408 for(int i=0;i<=sctl::MAX_DEPTH;i++){
1409 for(size_t j=0;j<node_lst_[i].size();j++){
1410 if(node_lst_[i][j]->pt_cnt[1]){
1411 for(size_t k=0;k<chld_cnt;k++){
1412 sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t> node=(sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>)node_lst_[i][j]->Child(k);
1413 node_lst.push_back(node);
1414 }
1415 }else{
1416 for(size_t k=0;k<chld_cnt;k++){
1417 sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t> node=(sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>)node_lst_[i][j]->Child(k);
1418 node->FMMData()->dnward_equiv.ReInit(0);
1419 }
1420 }
1421 }
1422 }
1423 if(r_node!=sctl::NullIterator<FMMNode_t>()) node_lst.push_back(r_node);
1424 n_list[indx]=node_lst;
1425 }
1426
1427 std::vector<sctl::Vector<Real_t>*>& vec_lst=vec_list[indx];
1428 for(size_t i=0;i<node_lst.size();i++){ // Construct vec_lst
1429 sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t> node=node_lst[i];
1430 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& data_vec=node->FMMData()->dnward_equiv;
1431 data_vec.ReInit(vec_sz,sctl::NullIterator<Real_t>(),false);
1432 vec_lst.push_back(&data_vec);
1433 }
1434 }
1435 {// 2. upward_equiv_fft
1436 int indx=2;
1437 std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>> node_lst;
1438 {
1439 std::vector<std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>> node_lst_(sctl::MAX_DEPTH+1);
1440 for(size_t i=0;i<node.size();i++)
1441 if(!node[i]->IsLeaf())
1442 node_lst_[node[i]->Depth()].push_back(node[i]);
1443 for(int i=0;i<=sctl::MAX_DEPTH;i++)
1444 for(size_t j=0;j<node_lst_[i].size();j++)
1445 node_lst.push_back(node_lst_[i][j]);
1446 }
1447 n_list[indx]=node_lst;
1448 }
1449 {// 3. dnward_check_fft
1450 int indx=3;
1451 std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>> node_lst;
1452 {
1453 std::vector<std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>> node_lst_(sctl::MAX_DEPTH+1);
1454 for(size_t i=0;i<node.size();i++)
1455 if(!node[i]->IsLeaf() && !node[i]->IsGhost())
1456 node_lst_[node[i]->Depth()].push_back(node[i]);
1457 for(int i=0;i<=sctl::MAX_DEPTH;i++)
1458 for(size_t j=0;j<node_lst_[i].size();j++)
1459 node_lst.push_back(node_lst_[i][j]);
1460 }
1461 n_list[indx]=node_lst;
1462 }
1463 {// 4. src_val
1464 int indx=4;
1465 int src_dof=kernel->ker_dim[0];
1466 int surf_dof=kernel->surf_dim;
1467
1468 std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>> node_lst;
1469 for(size_t i=0;i<node.size();i++){// Construct node_lst
1470 if(node[i]->IsLeaf()){
1471 node_lst.push_back(node[i]);
1472 }else{
1473 node[i]->src_value.ReInit(0);
1474 node[i]->surf_value.ReInit(0);
1475 }
1476 }
1477 n_list[indx]=node_lst;
1478
1479 std::vector<sctl::Vector<Real_t>*>& vec_lst=vec_list[indx];
1480 for(size_t i=0;i<node_lst.size();i++){ // Construct vec_lst
1481 sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t> node=node_lst[i];
1482 { // src_value
1483 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& data_vec=node->src_value;
1484 size_t vec_sz=(node->src_coord.Dim()/PVFMM_COORD_DIM)*src_dof;
1485 if((size_t)data_vec.Dim()!=vec_sz) data_vec.ReInit(vec_sz,sctl::NullIterator<Real_t>(),false);
1486 vec_lst.push_back(&data_vec);
1487 }
1488 { // surf_value
1489 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& data_vec=node->surf_value;
1490 size_t vec_sz=(node->surf_coord.Dim()/PVFMM_COORD_DIM)*surf_dof;
1491 if((size_t)data_vec.Dim()!=vec_sz) data_vec.ReInit(vec_sz,sctl::NullIterator<Real_t>(),false);
1492 vec_lst.push_back(&data_vec);
1493 }
1494 }
1495 }
1496 {// 5. trg_val
1497 int indx=5;
1498 int trg_dof=kernel->ker_dim[1];
1499
1500 std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>> node_lst;
1501 for(size_t i=0;i<node.size();i++){// Construct node_lst
1502 if(node[i]->IsLeaf() && !node[i]->IsGhost()){
1503 node_lst.push_back(node[i]);
1504 }else{
1505 node[i]->trg_value.ReInit(0);
1506 }
1507 }
1508 n_list[indx]=node_lst;
1509
1510 std::vector<sctl::Vector<Real_t>*>& vec_lst=vec_list[indx];
1511 for(size_t i=0;i<node_lst.size();i++){ // Construct vec_lst
1512 sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t> node=node_lst[i];
1513 { // trg_value
1514 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& data_vec=node->trg_value;
1515 size_t vec_sz=(node->trg_coord.Dim()/PVFMM_COORD_DIM)*trg_dof;
1516 data_vec.ReInit(vec_sz,sctl::NullIterator<Real_t>(),false);
1517 vec_lst.push_back(&data_vec);
1518 }
1519 }
1520 }
1521 {// 6. pts_coord
1522 int indx=6;
1523
1524 std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>> node_lst;
1525 for(size_t i=0;i<node.size();i++){// Construct node_lst
1526 if(node[i]->IsLeaf()){
1527 node_lst.push_back(node[i]);
1528 }else{
1529 node[i]->src_coord.ReInit(0);
1530 node[i]->surf_coord.ReInit(0);
1531 node[i]->trg_coord.ReInit(0);
1532 }
1533 }
1534 n_list[indx]=node_lst;
1535
1536 std::vector<sctl::Vector<Real_t>*>& vec_lst=vec_list[indx];
1537 for(size_t i=0;i<node_lst.size();i++){ // Construct vec_lst
1538 sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t> node=node_lst[i];
1539 { // src_coord
1540 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& data_vec=node->src_coord;
1541 vec_lst.push_back(&data_vec);
1542 }
1543 { // surf_coord
1544 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& data_vec=node->surf_coord;
1545 vec_lst.push_back(&data_vec);
1546 }
1547 { // trg_coord
1548 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& data_vec=node->trg_coord;
1549 vec_lst.push_back(&data_vec);
1550 }
1551 }
1552 { // check and equiv surfaces.
1553 if(tree->upwd_check_surf.size()==0){
1554 size_t m=MultipoleOrder();
1555 tree->upwd_check_surf.resize(sctl::MAX_DEPTH);
1556 tree->upwd_equiv_surf.resize(sctl::MAX_DEPTH);
1557 tree->dnwd_check_surf.resize(sctl::MAX_DEPTH);
1558 tree->dnwd_equiv_surf.resize(sctl::MAX_DEPTH);
1559 for(size_t depth=0;depth<sctl::MAX_DEPTH;depth++){
1560 Real_t c[3]={0.0,0.0,0.0};
1561 tree->upwd_check_surf[depth].ReInit((6*(m-1)*(m-1)+2)*PVFMM_COORD_DIM);
1562 tree->upwd_equiv_surf[depth].ReInit((6*(m-1)*(m-1)+2)*PVFMM_COORD_DIM);
1563 tree->dnwd_check_surf[depth].ReInit((6*(m-1)*(m-1)+2)*PVFMM_COORD_DIM);
1564 tree->dnwd_equiv_surf[depth].ReInit((6*(m-1)*(m-1)+2)*PVFMM_COORD_DIM);
1565 tree->upwd_check_surf[depth]=u_check_surf(m,c,depth);
1566 tree->upwd_equiv_surf[depth]=u_equiv_surf(m,c,depth);
1567 tree->dnwd_check_surf[depth]=d_check_surf(m,c,depth);
1568 tree->dnwd_equiv_surf[depth]=d_equiv_surf(m,c,depth);
1569 }
1570 }
1571 for(size_t depth=0;depth<sctl::MAX_DEPTH;depth++){
1572 vec_lst.push_back(&tree->upwd_check_surf[depth]);
1573 vec_lst.push_back(&tree->upwd_equiv_surf[depth]);
1574 vec_lst.push_back(&tree->dnwd_check_surf[depth]);
1575 vec_lst.push_back(&tree->dnwd_equiv_surf[depth]);
1576 }
1577 }
1578 }
1579
1580 // Create extra auxiliary buffer.
1581 if(buff_list.size()<=vec_list.size()) buff_list.resize(vec_list.size()+1);
1582 if(tree->node_data_buff_mirror.size()<buff_list.size()) tree->node_data_buff_mirror.resize(buff_list.size());
1583 for(size_t indx=0;indx<vec_list.size();indx++){ // Resize buffer
1584 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& buff=buff_list[indx];
1585 std::vector<sctl::Vector<Real_t>*>& vec_lst= vec_list[indx];
1586 bool keep_data=(indx==4 || indx==6);
1587 size_t n_vec=vec_lst.size();
1588
1589 { // Continue if nothing to be done.
1590 if(!n_vec) continue;
1591 if(buff.Dim(0)*buff.Dim(1)>0){
1592 bool init_buff=false;
1593 sctl::ConstIterator<Real_t> buff_start=buff.begin();
1594 sctl::ConstIterator<Real_t> buff_end=buff.begin()+buff.Dim(0)*buff.Dim(1);
1595 #pragma omp parallel for reduction(||:init_buff)
1596 for(size_t i=0;i<n_vec;i++){
1597 if(vec_lst[i]->Dim() && (vec_lst[i]->begin()<buff_start || vec_lst[i]->begin()>=buff_end)){
1598 init_buff=true;
1599 }
1600 }
1601 if(!init_buff) continue;
1602 }
1603 }
1604
1605 std::vector<size_t> vec_size(n_vec);
1606 std::vector<size_t> vec_disp(n_vec);
1607 if(n_vec){ // Set vec_size and vec_disp
1608 #pragma omp parallel for
1609 for(size_t i=0;i<n_vec;i++){ // Set vec_size
1610 vec_size[i]=vec_lst[i]->Dim();
1611 }
1612
1613 vec_disp[0]=0;
1614 sctl::omp_par::scan(&vec_size[0],&vec_disp[0],n_vec);
1615 }
1616 size_t buff_size=vec_size[n_vec-1]+vec_disp[n_vec-1];
1617 if(!buff_size) continue;
1618
1619 if(keep_data){ // Copy to dev_buffer
1620 if((size_t)dev_buffer.Dim()<buff_size*sizeof(Real_t)){ // Resize dev_buffer
1621 dev_buffer_mirror.Free(); // host buffer is about to be reallocated
1622 dev_buffer.ReInit((size_t)(buff_size*sizeof(Real_t)*1.05));
1623 }
1624
1625 #pragma omp parallel for
1626 for(size_t i=0;i<n_vec;i++){
1627 if(vec_lst[i]->begin()!=sctl::NullIterator<Real_t>()){
1628 sctl::omp_par::memcpy((sctl::Iterator<Real_t>)dev_buffer.begin()+vec_disp[i], vec_lst[i]->begin(), vec_size[i]);
1629 }
1630 }
1631 }
1632
1633 if((size_t)(buff.Dim(0)*buff.Dim(1))<buff_size){ // Resize buff
1634 tree->node_data_buff_mirror[indx].Free(); // host buffer is about to be reallocated
1635 buff.ReInit(1,(size_t)(buff_size*1.05));
1636 }
1637
1638 if(keep_data){ // Copy to buff (from dev_buffer)
1639 sctl::omp_par::memcpy(buff.begin(), (sctl::Iterator<Real_t>)dev_buffer.begin(), buff_size);
1640 }
1641
1642 #pragma omp parallel for
1643 for(size_t i=0;i<n_vec;i++){ // ReInit vectors
1644 vec_lst[i]->ReInit(vec_size[i],buff.begin()+vec_disp[i],false);
1645 }
1646 }
1647}
1648
1649
1650
1651template <class FMMNode>
1652void FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::SetupPrecomp(SetupData<FMMNode_t>& setup_data, bool device){
1653 if(setup_data.precomp_data==NULL || setup_data.level>sctl::MAX_DEPTH) return;
1654
1655 sctl::Profile::Tic("SetupPrecomp",&this->sctl_comm,true,25);
1656 if(setup_data.precomp_data_mirror) setup_data.precomp_data_mirror->Free(); // CompactData below may reallocate the host buffer
1657 { // Build precomp_data
1658 size_t precomp_offset=0;
1659 int level=setup_data.level;
1660 sctl::Matrix<char>& precomp_data=*setup_data.precomp_data;
1661 std::vector<Mat_Type>& interac_type_lst=setup_data.interac_type;
1662 for(size_t type_indx=0; type_indx<interac_type_lst.size(); type_indx++){
1663 Mat_Type& interac_type=interac_type_lst[type_indx];
1664 this->PrecompAll(interac_type, level); // Compute matrices.
1665 precomp_offset=this->mat->CompactData(level, interac_type, precomp_data, precomp_offset);
1666 }
1667 }
1668 sctl::Profile::Toc();
1669
1670 if(device){ // Host2Device
1671 sctl::Profile::Tic("Host2Device",&this->sctl_comm,false,25);
1672 setup_data.precomp_data_mirror->AllocDevice(*setup_data.precomp_data,true);
1673 sctl::Profile::Toc();
1674 }
1675}
1676
1677template <class FMMNode>
1678void FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::SetupInterac(SetupData<FMMNode_t>& setup_data, bool device){
1679 int level=setup_data.level;
1680 std::vector<Mat_Type>& interac_type_lst=setup_data.interac_type;
1681
1682 std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& nodes_in =setup_data.nodes_in ;
1683 std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& nodes_out=setup_data.nodes_out;
1684 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& input_data=*setup_data. input_data;
1685 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& output_data=*setup_data.output_data;
1686 std::vector<sctl::Vector<Real_t>*>& input_vector=setup_data. input_vector;
1687 std::vector<sctl::Vector<Real_t>*>& output_vector=setup_data.output_vector;
1688
1689 size_t n_in =nodes_in .size();
1690 size_t n_out=nodes_out.size();
1691
1692 // Setup precomputed data.
1693 if(setup_data.precomp_data->Dim(0)*setup_data.precomp_data->Dim(1)==0) SetupPrecomp(setup_data,device);
1694
1695 // Build interac_data
1696 sctl::Profile::Tic("Interac-Data",&this->sctl_comm,true,25);
1697 sctl::Matrix<char>& interac_data=setup_data.interac_data;
1698 { // Build precomp_data, interac_data
1699 std::vector<size_t> interac_mat;
1700 std::vector<size_t> interac_cnt;
1701 std::vector<size_t> interac_blk;
1702 std::vector<size_t> input_perm;
1703 std::vector<size_t> output_perm;
1704 size_t dof=0, M_dim0=0, M_dim1=0;
1705
1706 size_t precomp_offset=0;
1707 size_t buff_size=PVFMM_DEVICE_BUFFER_SIZE*1024l*1024l;
1708 if(n_out && n_in) for(size_t type_indx=0; type_indx<interac_type_lst.size(); type_indx++){
1709
1710 Mat_Type& interac_type=interac_type_lst[type_indx];
1711 size_t mat_cnt=this->interac_list.ListCount(interac_type);
1712 sctl::Matrix<size_t> precomp_data_offset;
1713 { // Load precomp_data for interac_type.
1714 struct HeaderData{
1715 size_t total_size;
1716 size_t level;
1717 size_t mat_cnt ;
1718 size_t max_depth;
1719 };
1720 sctl::Matrix<char>& precomp_data=*setup_data.precomp_data;
1721 char* indx_ptr=&precomp_data[0][0]+precomp_offset;
1722 HeaderData& header=*(HeaderData*)indx_ptr;indx_ptr+=sizeof(HeaderData);
1723 precomp_data_offset.ReInit(header.mat_cnt,(1+(2+2)*header.max_depth), sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)indx_ptr, header.mat_cnt*(1+(2+2)*header.max_depth)), false);
1724 precomp_offset+=header.total_size;
1725 }
1726
1727 sctl::Matrix<FMMNode*> src_interac_list(n_in ,mat_cnt); src_interac_list.SetZero();
1728 sctl::Matrix<FMMNode*> trg_interac_list(n_out,mat_cnt); trg_interac_list.SetZero();
1729 { // Build trg_interac_list
1730 #pragma omp parallel for
1731 for(size_t i=0;i<n_out;i++){
1732 if(!(nodes_out[i])->IsGhost() && (level==-1 || (nodes_out[i])->Depth()==level)){
1733 sctl::Vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode>>& lst=(nodes_out[i])->interac_list[interac_type];
1734 for(sctl::Long j=0;j<lst.Dim();j++){ // terminal decay into setup scratch
1735 trg_interac_list[i][j]=(lst[j]!=sctl::NullIterator<FMMNode>()?&lst[j][0]:NULL);
1736 }
1737 assert((size_t)lst.Dim()==mat_cnt);
1738 }
1739 }
1740 }
1741 { // Build src_interac_list
1742 #pragma omp parallel for
1743 for(size_t i=0;i<n_out;i++){
1744 for(size_t j=0;j<mat_cnt;j++)
1745 if(trg_interac_list[i][j]!=NULL){
1746 trg_interac_list[i][j]->node_id=n_in;
1747 }
1748 }
1749 #pragma omp parallel for
1750 for(size_t i=0;i<n_in ;i++) (nodes_in[i])->node_id=i;
1751 #pragma omp parallel for
1752 for(size_t i=0;i<n_out;i++){
1753 for(size_t j=0;j<mat_cnt;j++){
1754 if(trg_interac_list[i][j]!=NULL){
1755 if(trg_interac_list[i][j]->node_id==n_in){
1756 trg_interac_list[i][j]=NULL;
1757 }else{
1758 src_interac_list[trg_interac_list[i][j]->node_id][j]=&nodes_out[i][0]; // terminal decay into setup scratch
1759 }
1760 }
1761 }
1762 }
1763 }
1764
1765 sctl::Matrix<size_t> interac_dsp(n_out,mat_cnt);
1766 std::vector<size_t> interac_blk_dsp(1,0);
1767 { // Determine dof, M_dim0, M_dim1
1768 dof=1;
1769 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& M0 = this->interac_list.ClassMat(level, interac_type_lst[0], 0);
1770 M_dim0=M0.Dim(0); M_dim1=M0.Dim(1);
1771 }
1772 { // Determine interaction blocks which fit in memory.
1773 size_t vec_size=(M_dim0+M_dim1)*sizeof(Real_t)*dof;
1774 for(size_t j=0;j<mat_cnt;j++){// Determine minimum buff_size
1775 size_t vec_cnt=0;
1776 for(size_t i=0;i<n_out;i++){
1777 if(trg_interac_list[i][j]!=NULL) vec_cnt++;
1778 }
1779 if(buff_size<vec_cnt*vec_size)
1780 buff_size=vec_cnt*vec_size;
1781 }
1782
1783 size_t interac_dsp_=0;
1784 for(size_t j=0;j<mat_cnt;j++){
1785 for(size_t i=0;i<n_out;i++){
1786 interac_dsp[i][j]=interac_dsp_;
1787 if(trg_interac_list[i][j]!=NULL) interac_dsp_++;
1788 }
1789 if(interac_dsp_*vec_size>buff_size) // Comment to disable symmetries.
1790 {
1791 interac_blk.push_back(j-interac_blk_dsp.back());
1792 interac_blk_dsp.push_back(j);
1793
1794 size_t offset=interac_dsp[0][j];
1795 for(size_t i=0;i<n_out;i++) interac_dsp[i][j]-=offset;
1796 interac_dsp_-=offset;
1797
1798 assert(interac_dsp_*vec_size<=buff_size); // Problem too big for buff_size.
1799 }
1800 interac_mat.push_back(precomp_data_offset[this->interac_list.InteracClass(interac_type,j)][0]);
1801 interac_cnt.push_back(interac_dsp_-interac_dsp[0][j]);
1802 }
1803 interac_blk.push_back(mat_cnt-interac_blk_dsp.back());
1804 interac_blk_dsp.push_back(mat_cnt);
1805 }
1806
1807 { // Determine input_perm.
1808 size_t vec_size=M_dim0*dof;
1809 for(size_t i=0;i<n_out;i++) (nodes_out[i])->node_id=i;
1810 for(size_t k=1;k<interac_blk_dsp.size();k++){
1811 for(size_t i=0;i<n_in ;i++){
1812 for(size_t j=interac_blk_dsp[k-1];j<interac_blk_dsp[k];j++){
1813 FMMNode_t* trg_node=src_interac_list[i][j];
1814 if(trg_node!=NULL && trg_node->node_id<n_out){
1815 size_t depth=(this->ScaleInvar()?trg_node->Depth():0);
1816 input_perm .push_back(precomp_data_offset[j][1+4*depth+0]); // prem
1817 input_perm .push_back(precomp_data_offset[j][1+4*depth+1]); // scal
1818 input_perm .push_back(interac_dsp[trg_node->node_id][j]*vec_size*sizeof(Real_t)); // trg_ptr
1819 input_perm .push_back((size_t)(input_vector[i]->begin() - input_data.begin())); // src_ptr
1820 assert((size_t)input_vector[i]->Dim()==vec_size);
1821 }
1822 }
1823 }
1824 }
1825 }
1826 { // Determine output_perm
1827 size_t vec_size=M_dim1*dof;
1828 for(size_t k=1;k<interac_blk_dsp.size();k++){
1829 for(size_t i=0;i<n_out;i++){
1830 for(size_t j=interac_blk_dsp[k-1];j<interac_blk_dsp[k];j++){
1831 if(trg_interac_list[i][j]!=NULL){
1832 size_t depth=(this->ScaleInvar()?(nodes_out[i])->Depth():0);
1833 output_perm.push_back(precomp_data_offset[j][1+4*depth+2]); // prem
1834 output_perm.push_back(precomp_data_offset[j][1+4*depth+3]); // scal
1835 output_perm.push_back(interac_dsp[ i ][j]*vec_size*sizeof(Real_t)); // src_ptr
1836 output_perm.push_back((size_t)(output_vector[i]->begin() - output_data.begin())); // trg_ptr
1837 assert((size_t)output_vector[i]->Dim()==vec_size);
1838 }
1839 }
1840 }
1841 }
1842 }
1843 }
1844 if((size_t)this->dev_buffer.Dim()<buff_size){
1845 this->dev_buffer_mirror.Free(); // host buffer is about to be reallocated
1846 this->dev_buffer.ReInit(buff_size);
1847 }
1848
1849 { // Set interac_data.
1850 size_t data_size=sizeof(size_t)*4;
1851 data_size+=sizeof(size_t)+interac_blk.size()*sizeof(size_t);
1852 data_size+=sizeof(size_t)+interac_cnt.size()*sizeof(size_t);
1853 data_size+=sizeof(size_t)+interac_mat.size()*sizeof(size_t);
1854 data_size+=sizeof(size_t)+ input_perm.size()*sizeof(size_t);
1855 data_size+=sizeof(size_t)+output_perm.size()*sizeof(size_t);
1856 if((size_t)(interac_data.Dim(0)*interac_data.Dim(1))<sizeof(size_t)){
1857 data_size+=sizeof(size_t);
1858 setup_data.interac_data_mirror.Free(); // host buffer is about to be reallocated
1859 interac_data.ReInit(1,data_size);
1860 ((size_t*)&interac_data[0][0])[0]=sizeof(size_t);
1861 }else{
1862 size_t pts_data_size=*((size_t*)&interac_data[0][0]);
1863 assert((size_t)(interac_data.Dim(0)*interac_data.Dim(1))>=pts_data_size);
1864 data_size+=pts_data_size;
1865 if(data_size>(size_t)(interac_data.Dim(0)*interac_data.Dim(1))){ //Resize and copy interac_data.
1866 sctl::Matrix< char> pts_interac_data=interac_data;
1867 setup_data.interac_data_mirror.Free(); // host buffer is about to be reallocated
1868 interac_data.ReInit(1,data_size);
1869 sctl::omp_par::memcpy(interac_data.begin(), pts_interac_data.begin(), pts_data_size);
1870 }
1871 }
1872 char* data_ptr=&interac_data[0][0];
1873 data_ptr+=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0];
1874
1875 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]=data_size; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
1876 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]= M_dim0; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
1877 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]= M_dim1; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
1878 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]= dof; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
1879
1880 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]=interac_blk.size(); data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
1881 if (interac_blk.size()) memcpy(data_ptr, &interac_blk[0], interac_blk.size()*sizeof(size_t));
1882 data_ptr+=interac_blk.size()*sizeof(size_t);
1883
1884 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]=interac_cnt.size(); data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
1885 if (interac_cnt.size()) memcpy(data_ptr, &interac_cnt[0], interac_cnt.size()*sizeof(size_t));
1886 data_ptr+=interac_cnt.size()*sizeof(size_t);
1887
1888 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]=interac_mat.size(); data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
1889 if (interac_mat.size()) memcpy(data_ptr, &interac_mat[0], interac_mat.size()*sizeof(size_t));
1890 data_ptr+=interac_mat.size()*sizeof(size_t);
1891
1892 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]= input_perm.size(); data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
1893 if (input_perm.size()) memcpy(data_ptr, &input_perm[0], input_perm.size()*sizeof(size_t));
1894 data_ptr+= input_perm.size()*sizeof(size_t);
1895
1896 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]=output_perm.size(); data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
1897 if (output_perm.size()) memcpy(data_ptr, &output_perm[0], output_perm.size()*sizeof(size_t));
1898 data_ptr+=output_perm.size()*sizeof(size_t);
1899 }
1900 }
1901 sctl::Profile::Toc();
1902
1903 if(device){ // Host2Device
1904 sctl::Profile::Tic("Host2Device",&this->sctl_comm,false,25);
1905 setup_data.interac_data_mirror.AllocDevice(setup_data.interac_data,true);
1906 if((size_t)staging_buffer.Dim()<sizeof(Real_t)*output_data.Dim(0)*output_data.Dim(1)){
1907 staging_buffer_mirror.Free(); // host buffer is about to be reallocated
1908 staging_buffer.ReInit(sizeof(Real_t)*output_data.Dim(0)*output_data.Dim(1));
1909 staging_buffer.SetZero();
1910 staging_buffer_mirror.AllocDevice(staging_buffer,true);
1911 }
1912 sctl::Profile::Toc();
1913 }
1914}
1915
1916#if defined(PVFMM_HAVE_CUDA)
1917#include <fmm_pts_gpu.hpp>
1918
1919template <class FMMNode_t, int SYNC>
1920void EvalListGPU(SetupData<FMMNode_t>& setup_data, sctl::Vector<char>& dev_buffer, DeviceMirror& dev_buffer_mirror, const sctl::Comm& comm) {
1921 typedef typename FMMNode_t::Real_t Real_t;
1922 cudaStream_t* stream = pvfmm::CUDA_Lock::acquire_stream();
1923
1924 sctl::Profile::Tic("Host2Device",&comm,false,25);
1925 DeviceMatrix<char> interac_data;
1926 DeviceVector<char> buff;
1927 DeviceMatrix<char> precomp_data_d;
1928 DeviceMatrix<char> interac_data_d;
1929 DeviceMatrix<Real_t> input_data_d;
1930 DeviceMatrix<Real_t> output_data_d;
1931
1932 interac_data = setup_data.interac_data;
1933 buff = dev_buffer_mirror.AllocDevice(dev_buffer,false);
1934 precomp_data_d= setup_data.precomp_data_mirror->AllocDevice(*setup_data.precomp_data,false);
1935 interac_data_d= setup_data.interac_data_mirror.AllocDevice(setup_data.interac_data,false);
1936 input_data_d = setup_data.input_data_mirror->AllocDevice(*setup_data.input_data,false);
1937 output_data_d = setup_data.output_data_mirror->AllocDevice(*setup_data.output_data,false);
1938 sctl::Profile::Toc();
1939
1940 sctl::Profile::Tic("DeviceComp",&comm,false,20);
1941 { // Offloaded computation.
1942 size_t data_size, M_dim0, M_dim1, dof;
1943 sctl::Vector<size_t> interac_blk;
1944 sctl::Vector<size_t> interac_cnt;
1945 sctl::Vector<size_t> interac_mat;
1946 sctl::Vector<size_t> input_perm_d;
1947 sctl::Vector<size_t> output_perm_d;
1948
1949 { // Set interac_data.
1950 char* data_ptr=&interac_data [0][0];
1951 char* dev_ptr=&interac_data_d[0][0];
1952
1953 data_size=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]; data_ptr+=data_size; dev_ptr += data_size;
1954 data_size=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t); dev_ptr += sizeof(size_t);
1955 M_dim0 =((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t); dev_ptr += sizeof(size_t);
1956 M_dim1 =((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t); dev_ptr += sizeof(size_t);
1957 dof =((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t); dev_ptr += sizeof(size_t);
1958
1959 { size_t N=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0];
1960 interac_blk.ReInit(N,sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)(data_ptr+sizeof(size_t)),N),false); }
1961 data_ptr += sizeof(size_t) + sizeof(size_t)*interac_blk.Dim();
1962 dev_ptr += sizeof(size_t) + sizeof(size_t)*interac_blk.Dim();
1963
1964 { size_t N=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0];
1965 interac_cnt.ReInit(N,sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)(data_ptr+sizeof(size_t)),N),false); }
1966 data_ptr += sizeof(size_t) + sizeof(size_t)*interac_cnt.Dim();
1967 dev_ptr += sizeof(size_t) + sizeof(size_t)*interac_cnt.Dim();
1968
1969 { size_t N=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0];
1970 interac_mat.ReInit(N,sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)(data_ptr+sizeof(size_t)),N),false); }
1971 data_ptr += sizeof(size_t) + sizeof(size_t)*interac_mat.Dim();
1972 dev_ptr += sizeof(size_t) + sizeof(size_t)*interac_mat.Dim();
1973
1974 { size_t N=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0];
1975 input_perm_d.ReInit(N,sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)(dev_ptr+sizeof(size_t)),N),false); }
1976 data_ptr += sizeof(size_t) + sizeof(size_t)*input_perm_d.Dim();
1977 dev_ptr += sizeof(size_t) + sizeof(size_t)*input_perm_d.Dim();
1978
1979 { size_t N=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0];
1980 output_perm_d.ReInit(N,sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)(dev_ptr+sizeof(size_t)),N),false); }
1981 data_ptr += sizeof(size_t) + sizeof(size_t)*output_perm_d.Dim();
1982 dev_ptr += sizeof(size_t) + sizeof(size_t)*output_perm_d.Dim();
1983 }
1984
1985 { // interactions
1986 size_t interac_indx = 0;
1987 size_t interac_blk_dsp = 0;
1988 for (size_t k = 0; k < (size_t)interac_blk.Dim(); k++) {
1989 size_t vec_cnt=0;
1990 for(size_t j=interac_blk_dsp;j<interac_blk_dsp+interac_blk[k];j++) vec_cnt+=interac_cnt[j];
1991 if(vec_cnt==0){
1992 //interac_indx += vec_cnt;
1993 interac_blk_dsp += interac_blk[k];
1994 continue;
1995 }
1996
1997 char *buff_in_d =&buff[0];
1998 char *buff_out_d =&buff[vec_cnt*dof*M_dim0*sizeof(Real_t)];
1999
2000 { // Input permutation.
2001 in_perm_gpu<Real_t>(&precomp_data_d[0][0], &input_data_d[0][0], buff_in_d,
2002 &input_perm_d[interac_indx*4], vec_cnt, M_dim0, stream);
2003 }
2004
2005 size_t vec_cnt0 = 0;
2006 for (size_t j = interac_blk_dsp; j < interac_blk_dsp + interac_blk[k];) {
2007 size_t vec_cnt1 = 0;
2008 size_t interac_mat0 = interac_mat[j];
2009 for (; j < interac_blk_dsp + interac_blk[k] && interac_mat[j] == interac_mat0; j++) vec_cnt1 += interac_cnt[j];
2010 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M_d(M_dim0, M_dim1, sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>((Real_t*)(precomp_data_d.dev_ptr + interac_mat0), (M_dim0)*(M_dim1)), false);
2011 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> Ms_d(dof*vec_cnt1, M_dim0, sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>((Real_t*)(buff_in_d + M_dim0*vec_cnt0*dof*sizeof(Real_t)), (dof*vec_cnt1)*(M_dim0)), false);
2012 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> Mt_d(dof*vec_cnt1, M_dim1, sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>((Real_t*)(buff_out_d + M_dim1*vec_cnt0*dof*sizeof(Real_t)), (dof*vec_cnt1)*(M_dim1)), false);
2013 CUBLASGEMM(Mt_d, Ms_d, M_d);
2014 vec_cnt0 += vec_cnt1;
2015 }
2016
2017 { // Output permutation.
2018 out_perm_gpu<Real_t>(&precomp_data_d[0][0], &output_data_d[0][0], buff_out_d,
2019 &output_perm_d[interac_indx*4], vec_cnt, M_dim1, stream);
2020 }
2021
2022 interac_indx += vec_cnt;
2023 interac_blk_dsp += interac_blk[k];
2024 }
2025 }
2026 }
2027 sctl::Profile::Toc();
2028
2029 if(SYNC) CUDA_Lock::wait();
2030}
2031#endif
2032
2033template <class FMMNode>
2034template <int SYNC>
2035void FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::EvalList(SetupData<FMMNode_t>& setup_data, bool device){
2036 if(setup_data.interac_data.Dim(0)==0 || setup_data.interac_data.Dim(1)==0){
2037 sctl::Profile::Tic("Host2Device",&this->sctl_comm,false,25);
2038 sctl::Profile::Toc();
2039 sctl::Profile::Tic("DeviceComp",&this->sctl_comm,false,20);
2040 sctl::Profile::Toc();
2041 return;
2042 }
2043
2044#if defined(PVFMM_HAVE_CUDA)
2045 if (device) {
2046 EvalListGPU<FMMNode_t, SYNC>(setup_data, this->dev_buffer, this->dev_buffer_mirror, this->sctl_comm);
2047 return;
2048 }
2049#endif
2050
2051 sctl::Profile::Tic("Host2Device",&this->sctl_comm,false,25);
2052 DeviceVector<char> buff;
2053 DeviceMatrix<char> precomp_data;
2054 DeviceMatrix<char> interac_data;
2055 DeviceMatrix<Real_t> input_data;
2056 DeviceMatrix<Real_t> output_data;
2057 if(device){
2058 buff = this->dev_buffer_mirror.AllocDevice(this->dev_buffer,false);
2059 precomp_data= setup_data.precomp_data_mirror->AllocDevice(*setup_data.precomp_data,false);
2060 interac_data= setup_data.interac_data_mirror.AllocDevice(setup_data.interac_data,false);
2061 input_data = setup_data.input_data_mirror->AllocDevice(*setup_data.input_data,false);
2062 output_data = setup_data.output_data_mirror->AllocDevice(*setup_data.output_data,false);
2063 }else{
2064 buff = this-> dev_buffer;
2065 precomp_data=*setup_data.precomp_data;
2066 interac_data= setup_data.interac_data;
2067 input_data =*setup_data. input_data;
2068 output_data =*setup_data. output_data;
2069 }
2070 sctl::Profile::Toc();
2071
2072 sctl::Profile::Tic("DeviceComp",&this->sctl_comm,false,20);
2073 { // Computation (host; the CUDA device path returns above).
2074
2075 // Set interac_data.
2076 size_t data_size, M_dim0, M_dim1, dof;
2077 sctl::Vector<size_t> interac_blk;
2078 sctl::Vector<size_t> interac_cnt;
2079 sctl::Vector<size_t> interac_mat;
2080 sctl::Vector<size_t> input_perm;
2081 sctl::Vector<size_t> output_perm;
2082 { // Set interac_data.
2083 char* data_ptr=&interac_data[0][0];
2084
2085 data_size=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]; data_ptr+=data_size;
2086 data_size=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
2087 M_dim0 =((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
2088 M_dim1 =((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
2089 dof =((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
2090
2091 { size_t N=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0];
2092 interac_blk.ReInit(N,sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)(data_ptr+sizeof(size_t)),N),false); }
2093 data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t)+interac_blk.Dim()*sizeof(size_t);
2094
2095 { size_t N=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0];
2096 interac_cnt.ReInit(N,sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)(data_ptr+sizeof(size_t)),N),false); }
2097 data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t)+interac_cnt.Dim()*sizeof(size_t);
2098
2099 { size_t N=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0];
2100 interac_mat.ReInit(N,sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)(data_ptr+sizeof(size_t)),N),false); }
2101 data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t)+interac_mat.Dim()*sizeof(size_t);
2102
2103 { size_t N=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0];
2104 input_perm .ReInit(N,sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)(data_ptr+sizeof(size_t)),N),false); }
2105 data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t)+ input_perm.Dim()*sizeof(size_t);
2106
2107 { size_t N=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0];
2108 output_perm.ReInit(N,sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)(data_ptr+sizeof(size_t)),N),false); }
2109 data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t)+output_perm.Dim()*sizeof(size_t);
2110 }
2111
2112
2113 //Compute interaction from Chebyshev source density.
2114 { // interactions
2115 int omp_p=omp_get_max_threads();
2116 size_t interac_indx=0;
2117 size_t interac_blk_dsp=0;
2118 for(sctl::Long k=0;k<interac_blk.Dim();k++){
2119 size_t vec_cnt=0;
2120 for(size_t j=interac_blk_dsp;j<interac_blk_dsp+interac_blk[k];j++) vec_cnt+=interac_cnt[j];
2121 if(vec_cnt==0){
2122 //interac_indx += vec_cnt;
2123 interac_blk_dsp += interac_blk[k];
2124 continue;
2125 }
2126
2127 char* buff_in =&buff[0];
2128 char* buff_out=&buff[vec_cnt*dof*M_dim0*sizeof(Real_t)];
2129
2130 // Input permutation.
2131 #pragma omp parallel for
2132 for(int tid=0;tid<omp_p;tid++){
2133 size_t a=( tid *vec_cnt)/omp_p;
2134 size_t b=((tid+1)*vec_cnt)/omp_p;
2135
2136 for(size_t i=a;i<b;i++){
2137 const PVFMM_PERM_INT_T* perm=(PVFMM_PERM_INT_T*)(precomp_data[0]+input_perm[(interac_indx+i)*4+0]);
2138 const Real_t* scal=( Real_t*)(precomp_data[0]+input_perm[(interac_indx+i)*4+1]);
2139 const Real_t* v_in =( Real_t*)( input_data[0]+input_perm[(interac_indx+i)*4+3]);
2140 Real_t* v_out=( Real_t*)( buff_in +input_perm[(interac_indx+i)*4+2]);
2141
2142 // TODO: Fix for dof>1
2143 for(size_t j=0;j<M_dim0;j++ ){
2144 v_out[j]=v_in[perm[j]]*scal[j];
2145 }
2146 }
2147 }
2148
2149 size_t vec_cnt0=0;
2150 for(size_t j=interac_blk_dsp;j<interac_blk_dsp+interac_blk[k];){
2151 size_t vec_cnt1=0;
2152 size_t interac_mat0=interac_mat[j];
2153 for(;j<interac_blk_dsp+interac_blk[k] && interac_mat[j]==interac_mat0;j++) vec_cnt1+=interac_cnt[j];
2154 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M(M_dim0, M_dim1, sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>((Real_t*)(precomp_data[0]+interac_mat0), (M_dim0)*(M_dim1)), false);
2155 #pragma omp parallel for
2156 for(int tid=0;tid<omp_p;tid++){
2157 size_t a=(dof*vec_cnt1*(tid ))/omp_p;
2158 size_t b=(dof*vec_cnt1*(tid+1))/omp_p;
2159 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> Ms(b-a, M_dim0, sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>((Real_t*)(buff_in +M_dim0*vec_cnt0*dof*sizeof(Real_t))+M_dim0*a, (b-a)*(M_dim0)), false);
2160 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> Mt(b-a, M_dim1, sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>((Real_t*)(buff_out+M_dim1*vec_cnt0*dof*sizeof(Real_t))+M_dim1*a, (b-a)*(M_dim1)), false);
2161 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>::GEMM(Mt,Ms,M);
2162 }
2163 vec_cnt0+=vec_cnt1;
2164 }
2165
2166 // Output permutation.
2167 #pragma omp parallel for
2168 for(int tid=0;tid<omp_p;tid++){
2169 size_t a=( tid *vec_cnt)/omp_p;
2170 size_t b=((tid+1)*vec_cnt)/omp_p;
2171
2172 if(tid> 0 && a<vec_cnt){ // Find 'a' independent of other threads.
2173 size_t out_ptr=output_perm[(interac_indx+a)*4+3];
2174 if(tid> 0) while(a<vec_cnt && out_ptr==output_perm[(interac_indx+a)*4+3]) a++;
2175 }
2176 if(tid<omp_p-1 && b<vec_cnt){ // Find 'b' independent of other threads.
2177 size_t out_ptr=output_perm[(interac_indx+b)*4+3];
2178 if(tid<omp_p-1) while(b<vec_cnt && out_ptr==output_perm[(interac_indx+b)*4+3]) b++;
2179 }
2180 for(size_t i=a;i<b;i++){ // Compute permutations.
2181 const PVFMM_PERM_INT_T* perm=(PVFMM_PERM_INT_T*)(precomp_data[0]+output_perm[(interac_indx+i)*4+0]);
2182 const Real_t* scal=( Real_t*)(precomp_data[0]+output_perm[(interac_indx+i)*4+1]);
2183 const Real_t* v_in =( Real_t*)( buff_out +output_perm[(interac_indx+i)*4+2]);
2184 Real_t* v_out=( Real_t*)( output_data[0]+output_perm[(interac_indx+i)*4+3]);
2185
2186 // TODO: Fix for dof>1
2187 for(size_t j=0;j<M_dim1;j++ ){
2188 v_out[j]+=v_in[perm[j]]*scal[j];
2189 }
2190 }
2191 }
2192
2193 interac_indx+=vec_cnt;
2194 interac_blk_dsp+=interac_blk[k];
2195 }
2196 }
2197
2198 }
2199
2200
2201 sctl::Profile::Toc();
2202}

2206template <class FMMNode>
2207void FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::Source2UpSetup(SetupData<FMMNode_t>& setup_data, FMMTree_t* tree, std::vector<sctl::Matrix<Real_t> >& buff, std::vector<sctl::Vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>> >& n_list, int level, bool device){
2208 if(!this->MultipoleOrder()) return;
2209 { // Set setup_data
2210 setup_data. level=level;
2211 setup_data.kernel=kernel->k_s2m;
2212 setup_data. input_data=&buff[4];
2213 setup_data.input_data_mirror=&tree->node_data_buff_mirror[4];
2214 setup_data.output_data=&buff[0];
2215 setup_data.output_data_mirror=&tree->node_data_buff_mirror[0];
2216 setup_data. coord_data=&buff[6];
2217 setup_data.coord_data_mirror=&tree->node_data_buff_mirror[6];
2218 sctl::Vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& nodes_in =n_list[4];
2219 sctl::Vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& nodes_out=n_list[0];
2220
2221 setup_data.nodes_in .clear();
2222 setup_data.nodes_out.clear();
2223 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<nodes_in .Dim();i++) if((nodes_in [i]->Depth()==level || level==-1) && (nodes_in [i]->src_coord.Dim() || nodes_in [i]->surf_coord.Dim()) && nodes_in [i]->IsLeaf() && !nodes_in [i]->IsGhost()) setup_data.nodes_in .push_back(nodes_in [i]);
2224 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<nodes_out.Dim();i++) if((nodes_out[i]->Depth()==level || level==-1) && (nodes_out[i]->src_coord.Dim() || nodes_out[i]->surf_coord.Dim()) && nodes_out[i]->IsLeaf() && !nodes_out[i]->IsGhost()) setup_data.nodes_out.push_back(nodes_out[i]);
2225 }
