2227 struct PackedData{
2228 size_t len;
2229 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>* ptr;
2230 sctl::Vector<size_t> cnt;
2231 sctl::Vector<size_t> dsp;
2232 };
2233 struct InteracData{
2234 sctl::Vector<size_t> in_node;
2235 sctl::Vector<size_t> scal_idx;
2236 sctl::Vector<Real_t> coord_shift;
2237 sctl::Vector<size_t> interac_cnt;
2238 sctl::Vector<size_t> interac_dsp;
2239 sctl::Vector<size_t> interac_cst;
2240 sctl::Vector<Real_t> scal[4*sctl::MAX_DEPTH];
2241 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M[4];
2242 };
2243 struct ptSetupData{
2244 int level;
2245 const Kernel<Real_t>* kernel;
2246
2247 PackedData src_coord; // Src coord
2248 PackedData src_value; // Src density
2249 PackedData srf_coord; // Srf coord
2250 PackedData srf_value; // Srf density
2251 PackedData trg_coord; // Trg coord
2252 PackedData trg_value; // Trg potential
2253
2254 InteracData interac_data;
2255 };
2256
2257 ptSetupData data;
2258 data. level=setup_data. level;
2259 data.kernel=setup_data.kernel;
2260 std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& nodes_in =setup_data.nodes_in ;
2261 std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& nodes_out=setup_data.nodes_out;
2262
2263 { // Set src data
2264 std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& nodes=nodes_in;
2265 PackedData& coord=data.src_coord;
2266 PackedData& value=data.src_value;
2267 coord.ptr=setup_data. coord_data;
2268 value.ptr=setup_data. input_data;
2269
2270 coord.len=coord.ptr->Dim(0)*coord.ptr->Dim(1);
2271 value.len=value.ptr->Dim(0)*value.ptr->Dim(1);
2272
2273 coord.cnt.ReInit(nodes.size());
2274 coord.dsp.ReInit(nodes.size());
2275 value.cnt.ReInit(nodes.size());
2276 value.dsp.ReInit(nodes.size());
2277
2278 #pragma omp parallel for
2279 for(size_t i=0;i<nodes.size();i++){
2280 (nodes[i])->node_id=i;
2281 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& coord_vec=(nodes[i])->src_coord;
2282 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& value_vec=(nodes[i])->src_value;
2283 if(coord_vec.Dim()){
2284 coord.dsp[i]=&coord_vec[0]-&coord.ptr[0][0][0];
2285 assert(coord.dsp[i]<coord.len);
2286 coord.cnt[i]=coord_vec.Dim();
2287 }else{
2288 coord.dsp[i]=0;
2289 coord.cnt[i]=0;
2290 }
2291 if(value_vec.Dim()){
2292 value.dsp[i]=&value_vec[0]-&value.ptr[0][0][0];
2293 assert(value.dsp[i]<value.len);
2294 value.cnt[i]=value_vec.Dim();
2295 }else{
2296 value.dsp[i]=0;
2297 value.cnt[i]=0;
2298 }
2299 }
2300 }
2301 { // Set srf data
2302 std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& nodes=nodes_in;
2303 PackedData& coord=data.srf_coord;
2304 PackedData& value=data.srf_value;
2305 coord.ptr=setup_data. coord_data;
2306 value.ptr=setup_data. input_data;
2307
2308 coord.len=coord.ptr->Dim(0)*coord.ptr->Dim(1);
2309 value.len=value.ptr->Dim(0)*value.ptr->Dim(1);
2310
2311 coord.cnt.ReInit(nodes.size());
2312 coord.dsp.ReInit(nodes.size());
2313 value.cnt.ReInit(nodes.size());
2314 value.dsp.ReInit(nodes.size());
2315
2316 #pragma omp parallel for
2317 for(size_t i=0;i<nodes.size();i++){
2318 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& coord_vec=(nodes[i])->surf_coord;
2319 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& value_vec=(nodes[i])->surf_value;
2320 if(coord_vec.Dim()){
2321 coord.dsp[i]=&coord_vec[0]-&coord.ptr[0][0][0];
2322 assert(coord.dsp[i]<coord.len);
2323 coord.cnt[i]=coord_vec.Dim();
2324 }else{
2325 coord.dsp[i]=0;
2326 coord.cnt[i]=0;
2327 }
2328 if(value_vec.Dim()){
2329 value.dsp[i]=&value_vec[0]-&value.ptr[0][0][0];
2330 assert(value.dsp[i]<value.len);
2331 value.cnt[i]=value_vec.Dim();
2332 }else{
2333 value.dsp[i]=0;
2334 value.cnt[i]=0;
2335 }
2336 }
2337 }
2338 { // Set trg data
2339 std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& nodes=nodes_out;
2340 PackedData& coord=data.trg_coord;
2341 PackedData& value=data.trg_value;
2342 coord.ptr=setup_data. coord_data;
2343 value.ptr=setup_data.output_data;
2344
2345 coord.len=coord.ptr->Dim(0)*coord.ptr->Dim(1);
2346 value.len=value.ptr->Dim(0)*value.ptr->Dim(1);
2347
2348 coord.cnt.ReInit(nodes.size());
2349 coord.dsp.ReInit(nodes.size());
2350 value.cnt.ReInit(nodes.size());
2351 value.dsp.ReInit(nodes.size());
2352
2353 #pragma omp parallel for
2354 for(size_t i=0;i<nodes.size();i++){
2355 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& coord_vec=tree->upwd_check_surf[(nodes[i])->Depth()];
2356 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& value_vec=((FMMData*)(nodes[i])->FMMData())->upward_equiv;
2357 if(coord_vec.Dim()){
2358 coord.dsp[i]=&coord_vec[0]-&coord.ptr[0][0][0];
2359 assert(coord.dsp[i]<coord.len);
2360 coord.cnt[i]=coord_vec.Dim();
2361 }else{
2362 coord.dsp[i]=0;
2363 coord.cnt[i]=0;
2364 }
2365 if(value_vec.Dim()){
2366 value.dsp[i]=&value_vec[0]-&value.ptr[0][0][0];
2367 assert(value.dsp[i]<value.len);
2368 value.cnt[i]=value_vec.Dim();
2369 }else{
2370 value.dsp[i]=0;
2371 value.cnt[i]=0;
2372 }
2373 }
2374 }
2375 { // Set interac_data
2376 int omp_p=omp_get_max_threads();
2377 std::vector<std::vector<size_t> > in_node_(omp_p);
2378 std::vector<std::vector<size_t> > scal_idx_(omp_p);
2379 std::vector<std::vector<Real_t> > coord_shift_(omp_p);
2380 std::vector<std::vector<size_t> > interac_cnt_(omp_p);
2381 if(this->ScaleInvar()){ // Set scal
2382 const Kernel<Real_t>* ker=kernel->k_m2m;
2383 for(size_t l=0;l<sctl::MAX_DEPTH;l++){ // scal[l*4+2]
2384 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& scal=data.interac_data.scal[l*4+2];
2385 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& scal_exp=ker->trg_scal;
2386 scal.ReInit(scal_exp.Dim());
2387 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<scal.Dim();i++){
2388 scal[i]=sctl::pow<Real_t>(2.0,-scal_exp[i]*l);
2389 }
2390 }
2391 for(size_t l=0;l<sctl::MAX_DEPTH;l++){ // scal[l*4+3]
2392 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& scal=data.interac_data.scal[l*4+3];
2393 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& scal_exp=ker->src_scal;
2394 scal.ReInit(scal_exp.Dim());
2395 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<scal.Dim();i++){
2396 scal[i]=sctl::pow<Real_t>(2.0,-scal_exp[i]*l);
2397 }
2398 }
2399 }
2400
2401 #pragma omp parallel for
2402 for(int tid=0;tid<omp_p;tid++){
2403 std::vector<size_t>& in_node =in_node_[tid] ;
2404 std::vector<size_t>& scal_idx =scal_idx_[tid] ;
2405 std::vector<Real_t>& coord_shift=coord_shift_[tid];
2406 std::vector<size_t>& interac_cnt=interac_cnt_[tid];
2407
2408 size_t a=(nodes_out.size()*(tid+0))/omp_p;
2409 size_t b=(nodes_out.size()*(tid+1))/omp_p;
2410 for(size_t i=a;i<b;i++){
2411 sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t> tnode=nodes_out[i];
2412 Real_t s=sctl::pow<Real_t>(0.5,tnode->Depth());
2413
2414 size_t interac_cnt_=0;
2415 { // S2U_Type
2416 Mat_Type type=S2U_Type;
2417 sctl::Vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& intlst=tnode->interac_list[type];
2418 for(sctl::Long j=0;j<intlst.Dim();j++) if(intlst[j]!=sctl::NullIterator<FMMNode_t>()){
2419 sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t> snode=intlst[j];
2420 size_t snode_id=snode->node_id;
2421 if(snode_id>=nodes_in.size() || nodes_in[snode_id]!=snode) continue;
2422 in_node.push_back(snode_id);
2423 scal_idx.push_back(snode->Depth());
2424 { // set coord_shift
2425 const int* rel_coord=interac_list.RelativeCoord(type,j);
2426 const Real_t* scoord=snode->Coord();
2427 //const Real_t* tcoord=tnode->Coord();
2428 Real_t shift[PVFMM_COORD_DIM];
2429 shift[0]=rel_coord[0]*(Real_t)0.5*s-(scoord[0]+(Real_t)0.5*s)+(0+(Real_t)0.5*s);
2430 shift[1]=rel_coord[1]*(Real_t)0.5*s-(scoord[1]+(Real_t)0.5*s)+(0+(Real_t)0.5*s);
2431 shift[2]=rel_coord[2]*(Real_t)0.5*s-(scoord[2]+(Real_t)0.5*s)+(0+(Real_t)0.5*s);
2432 coord_shift.push_back(shift[0]);
2433 coord_shift.push_back(shift[1]);
2434 coord_shift.push_back(shift[2]);
2435 }
2436 interac_cnt_++;
2437 }
2438 }
2439 interac_cnt.push_back(interac_cnt_);
2440 }
2441 }
2442 { // Combine interac data
2443 InteracData& interac_data=data.interac_data;
2444 { // in_node
2445 typedef size_t ElemType;
2446 std::vector<std::vector<ElemType> >& vec_=in_node_;
2447 sctl::Vector<ElemType>& vec=interac_data.in_node;
2448
2449 std::vector<size_t> vec_dsp(omp_p+1,0);
2450 for(int tid=0;tid<omp_p;tid++){
2451 vec_dsp[tid+1]=vec_dsp[tid]+vec_[tid].size();
2452 }
2453 vec.ReInit(vec_dsp[omp_p]);
2454 #pragma omp parallel for
2455 for(int tid=0;tid<omp_p;tid++){
2456 if (vec_[tid].size()) memcpy(&vec[0]+vec_dsp[tid],&vec_[tid][0],vec_[tid].size()*sizeof(ElemType));
2457 }
2458 }
2459 { // scal_idx
2460 typedef size_t ElemType;
2461 std::vector<std::vector<ElemType> >& vec_=scal_idx_;
2462 sctl::Vector<ElemType>& vec=interac_data.scal_idx;
2463
2464 std::vector<size_t> vec_dsp(omp_p+1,0);
2465 for(int tid=0;tid<omp_p;tid++){
2466 vec_dsp[tid+1]=vec_dsp[tid]+vec_[tid].size();
2467 }
2468 vec.ReInit(vec_dsp[omp_p]);
2469 #pragma omp parallel for
2470 for(int tid=0;tid<omp_p;tid++){
2471 if (vec_[tid].size()) memcpy(&vec[0]+vec_dsp[tid],&vec_[tid][0],vec_[tid].size()*sizeof(ElemType));
2472 }
2473 }
2474 { // coord_shift
2475 typedef Real_t ElemType;
2476 std::vector<std::vector<ElemType> >& vec_=coord_shift_;
2477 sctl::Vector<ElemType>& vec=interac_data.coord_shift;
2478
2479 std::vector<size_t> vec_dsp(omp_p+1,0);
2480 for(int tid=0;tid<omp_p;tid++){
2481 vec_dsp[tid+1]=vec_dsp[tid]+vec_[tid].size();
2482 }
2483 vec.ReInit(vec_dsp[omp_p]);
2484 #pragma omp parallel for
2485 for(int tid=0;tid<omp_p;tid++){
2486 if (vec_[tid].size()) memcpy(&vec[0]+vec_dsp[tid],&vec_[tid][0],vec_[tid].size()*sizeof(ElemType));
2487 }
2488 }
2489 { // interac_cnt
2490 typedef size_t ElemType;
2491 std::vector<std::vector<ElemType> >& vec_=interac_cnt_;
2492 sctl::Vector<ElemType>& vec=interac_data.interac_cnt;
2493
2494 std::vector<size_t> vec_dsp(omp_p+1,0);
2495 for(int tid=0;tid<omp_p;tid++){
2496 vec_dsp[tid+1]=vec_dsp[tid]+vec_[tid].size();
2497 }
2498 vec.ReInit(vec_dsp[omp_p]);
2499 #pragma omp parallel for
2500 for(int tid=0;tid<omp_p;tid++){
2501 if (vec_[tid].size()) memcpy(&vec[0]+vec_dsp[tid],&vec_[tid][0],vec_[tid].size()*sizeof(ElemType));
2502 }
2503 }
2504 { // interac_dsp
2505 sctl::Vector<size_t>& cnt=interac_data.interac_cnt;
2506 sctl::Vector<size_t>& dsp=interac_data.interac_dsp;
2507 dsp.ReInit(cnt.Dim()); if(dsp.Dim()) dsp[0]=0;
2508 if (dsp.Dim()) sctl::omp_par::scan(&cnt[0],&dsp[0],dsp.Dim());
2509 }
2510 }
2511 { // Set M[2], M[3]
2512 InteracData& interac_data=data.interac_data;
2513 sctl::Vector<size_t>& cnt=interac_data.interac_cnt;
2514 sctl::Vector<size_t>& dsp=interac_data.interac_dsp;
2515 if(cnt.Dim() && cnt[cnt.Dim()-1]+dsp[dsp.Dim()-1]){
2516 data.interac_data.M[2]=this->mat->Mat(level, UC2UE0_Type, 0);
2517 data.interac_data.M[3]=this->mat->Mat(level, UC2UE1_Type, 0);
2518 }else{
2519 data.interac_data.M[2].ReInit(0,0);
2520 data.interac_data.M[3].ReInit(0,0);
2521 }
2522 }
2523 }
2524
2525 PtSetup(setup_data, &data);
2526}

2536template <class FMMNode>
2537void FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::Up2UpSetup(SetupData<FMMNode_t>& setup_data, FMMTree_t* tree, std::vector<sctl::Matrix<Real_t> >& buff, std::vector<sctl::Vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>> >& n_list, int level, bool device){
2538 if(!this->MultipoleOrder()) return;
2539 { // Set setup_data
2540 setup_data.level=level;
2541 setup_data.kernel=kernel->k_m2m;
2542 setup_data.interac_type.resize(1);
2543 setup_data.interac_type[0]=U2U_Type;
2544
2545 setup_data. input_data=&buff[0];
2546 setup_data.input_data_mirror=&tree->node_data_buff_mirror[0];
2547 setup_data.output_data=&buff[0];
2548 setup_data.output_data_mirror=&tree->node_data_buff_mirror[0];
2549 sctl::Vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& nodes_in =n_list[0];
2550 sctl::Vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& nodes_out=n_list[0];
2551
2552 setup_data.nodes_in .clear();
2553 setup_data.nodes_out.clear();
2554 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<nodes_in .Dim();i++) if((nodes_in [i]->Depth()==level+1) && nodes_in [i]->pt_cnt[0]) setup_data.nodes_in .push_back(nodes_in [i]);
2555 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<nodes_out.Dim();i++) if((nodes_out[i]->Depth()==level ) && nodes_out[i]->pt_cnt[0]) setup_data.nodes_out.push_back(nodes_out[i]);
2556 }
2557
2558 std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& nodes_in =setup_data.nodes_in ;
2559 std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& nodes_out=setup_data.nodes_out;
2560 std::vector<sctl::Vector<Real_t>*>& input_vector=setup_data. input_vector; input_vector.clear();
2561 std::vector<sctl::Vector<Real_t>*>& output_vector=setup_data.output_vector; output_vector.clear();
2562 for(size_t i=0;i<nodes_in .size();i++) input_vector.push_back(&((FMMData*)(nodes_in[i])->FMMData())->upward_equiv);
2563 for(size_t i=0;i<nodes_out.size();i++) output_vector.push_back(&((FMMData*)(nodes_out[i])->FMMData())->upward_equiv);
2564
2565 SetupInterac(setup_data,device);
2566}

2568template <class FMMNode>
2569void FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::Up2Up (SetupData<FMMNode_t>& setup_data, bool device){
2570 if(!this->MultipoleOrder()) return;
2571 //Add Up2Up contribution.
2572 EvalList(setup_data, device);
2573}

2576
2577template <class FMMNode>
2578void FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::PeriodicBC(FMMNode* node, BoundaryType bndry_cond){
2579 if(!this->ScaleInvar() || this->MultipoleOrder()==0 || bndry_cond==BoundaryType::FreeSpace) return;
2580 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& M = Precomp(0, BC_Type, bndry_cond);
2581
2582 assert(node->FMMData()->upward_equiv.Dim()>0);
2583 int dof=1;
2584
2585 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& upward_equiv=node->FMMData()->upward_equiv;
2586 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& dnward_equiv=node->FMMData()->dnward_equiv;
2587 assert(upward_equiv.Dim()==M.Dim(0)*dof);
2588 assert(dnward_equiv.Dim()==M.Dim(1)*dof);
2589 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> d_equiv(dof,M.Dim(1), sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>(&dnward_equiv[0], (dof)*(M.Dim(1))),false);
2590 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> u_equiv(dof,M.Dim(0), sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>(&upward_equiv[0], (dof)*(M.Dim(0))),false);
2591 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>::GEMM(d_equiv,u_equiv,M);
2592
2593#ifdef PVFMM_EXTENDED_BC
2594 if(m2c!=NULL){
2595 const int mi = dnward_equiv.Dim();
2596 const int nj = upward_equiv.Dim();
2597 if(mi!=nj){
2598 printf("PVFMM_EXTENDED_BC operator size error\n");
2599 exit(1);
2600 }
2601 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M2C(mi,nj, sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>(m2c, (mi)*(nj)),false);
2602 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> d_check=d_equiv;
2603 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>::GEMM(d_check,u_equiv,M2C);
2604 d_equiv += d_check;
2605 // for (int i = 0; i < mi; i++) {
2606 // double temp = 0;
2607 // for (int j = 0; j < nj; j++) {
2608 // temp += m2c[i * nj + j] * upward_equiv[j];
2609 // }
2610 // dnward_equiv[i] += temp;
2611 // }
2612 }
2613#endif
2614}
2615
2616template <class FMMNode>
2617void FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::SetM2C(Real_t* dataPtr){
2618#ifdef PVFMM_EXTENDED_BC
2619 if(dataPtr!=NULL)
2620 m2c=dataPtr;
2621#endif
2622}
2623
2624
2625template <class FMMNode>
2626void FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::FFT_UpEquiv(size_t dof, size_t m, size_t ker_dim0, sctl::Vector<size_t>& fft_vec, sctl::Vector<Real_t>& fft_scal,
2627 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& input_data, sctl::Vector<Real_t>& output_data, sctl::Vector<Real_t>& buffer_){
2628
2629 size_t n1=m*2;
2630 size_t n2=n1*n1;
2631 size_t n3=n1*n2;
2632 size_t n3_=n2*(n1/2+1);
2633 size_t chld_cnt=1UL<<PVFMM_COORD_DIM;
2634 size_t fftsize_in =2*n3_*chld_cnt*ker_dim0*dof;
2635 int omp_p=omp_get_max_threads();
2636
2637 size_t n=6*(m-1)*(m-1)+2;
2638 sctl::Vector<size_t>& map = vlist_fft_map;
2639 { // Build map to reorder upward_equiv
2640 size_t n_old=map.Dim();
2641 if(n_old!=n){
2642 Real_t c[3]={0,0,0};
2643 sctl::Vector<Real_t> surf(surface(m, c, (Real_t)(m-1), 0));
2644 map.ReInit(surf.Dim()/PVFMM_COORD_DIM);
2645 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<map.Dim();i++)
2646 map[i]=((size_t)(m-1-surf[i*3]+0.5))+((size_t)(m-1-surf[i*3+1]+0.5))*n1+((size_t)(m-1-surf[i*3+2]+0.5))*n2;
2647 }
2648 }
2649 { // Build FFTW plan.
2650 if(!vlist_fft_flag){
2651 sctl::Vector<sctl::Long> dim_vec(PVFMM_COORD_DIM); dim_vec[0]=(sctl::Long)n1; dim_vec[1]=(sctl::Long)n1; dim_vec[2]=(sctl::Long)n1;
2652 vlist_fft.Setup(sctl::FFT_Type::R2C, ker_dim0*chld_cnt, dim_vec);
2653 vlist_fft_flag=true;
2654 }
2655 }
2656
2657 { // Offload section
2658 size_t n_in = fft_vec.Dim();
2659 #pragma omp parallel for
2660 for(int pid=0; pid<omp_p; pid++){
2661 size_t node_start=(n_in*(pid ))/omp_p;
2662 size_t node_end =(n_in*(pid+1))/omp_p;
2663 sctl::Vector<Real_t> buffer(fftsize_in, sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>(&buffer_[fftsize_in*pid], fftsize_in), false);
2664 for(size_t node_idx=node_start; node_idx<node_end; node_idx++){
2665 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> upward_equiv(chld_cnt,n*ker_dim0*dof, sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>(&input_data[0] + fft_vec[node_idx], (chld_cnt)*(n*ker_dim0*dof)),false);
2666 sctl::Vector<Real_t> upward_equiv_fft(fftsize_in, sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>(&output_data[fftsize_in *node_idx], fftsize_in), false);
2667 upward_equiv_fft.SetZero();
2668
2669 // Rearrange upward equivalent data.
2670 for(size_t k=0;k<n;k++){
2671 size_t idx=map[k];
2672 for(size_t j1=0;j1<dof;j1++)
2673 for(size_t j0=0;j0<chld_cnt;j0++)
2674 for(size_t i=0;i<ker_dim0;i++)
2675 upward_equiv_fft[idx+(j0+(i+j1*ker_dim0)*chld_cnt)*n3]=upward_equiv[j0][ker_dim0*(n*j1+k)+i]*fft_scal[ker_dim0*node_idx+i];
2676 }
2677
2678 // Compute FFT.
2679 for(size_t i=0;i<dof;i++){
2680 sctl::Vector<Real_t> in_ ( n3 *ker_dim0*chld_cnt, upward_equiv_fft.begin()+i*n3*ker_dim0*chld_cnt, false);
2681 sctl::Vector<Real_t> out_(2*n3_*ker_dim0*chld_cnt, buffer.begin()+i*2*n3_*ker_dim0*chld_cnt, false);
2682 vlist_fft.Execute(in_, out_);
2683 }
2684
2685 for(size_t i=0;i<ker_dim0*dof;i++)
2686 for(size_t j=0;j<n3_;j++)
2687 for(size_t k=0;k<chld_cnt;k++){
2688 upward_equiv_fft[2*(chld_cnt*(n3_*i+j)+k)+0]=buffer[2*(n3_*(chld_cnt*i+k)+j)+0];
2689 upward_equiv_fft[2*(chld_cnt*(n3_*i+j)+k)+1]=buffer[2*(n3_*(chld_cnt*i+k)+j)+1];
2690 }
2691 }
2692 }
2693 }
2694}
2695
2696template <class FMMNode>
2697void FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::FFT_Check2Equiv(size_t dof, size_t m, size_t ker_dim1, sctl::Vector<size_t>& ifft_vec, sctl::Vector<Real_t>& ifft_scal,
2698 sctl::Vector<Real_t>& input_data, sctl::Vector<Real_t>& output_data, sctl::Vector<Real_t>& buffer_){
2699
2700 size_t n1=m*2;
2701 size_t n2=n1*n1;
2702 size_t n3=n1*n2;
2703 size_t n3_=n2*(n1/2+1);
2704 size_t chld_cnt=1UL<<PVFMM_COORD_DIM;
2705 size_t fftsize_out=2*n3_*dof*ker_dim1*chld_cnt;
2706 int omp_p=omp_get_max_threads();
2707
2708 size_t n=6*(m-1)*(m-1)+2;
2709 sctl::Vector<size_t>& map = vlist_ifft_map;
2710 { // Build map to reorder dnward_check
2711 size_t n_old=map.Dim();
2712 if(n_old!=n){
2713 Real_t c[3]={0,0,0};
2714 sctl::Vector<Real_t> surf(surface(m, c, (Real_t)(m-1), 0));
2715 map.ReInit(surf.Dim()/PVFMM_COORD_DIM);
2716 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<map.Dim();i++)
2717 map[i]=((size_t)(m*2-0.5-surf[i*3]))+((size_t)(m*2-0.5-surf[i*3+1]))*n1+((size_t)(m*2-0.5-surf[i*3+2]))*n2;
2718 //map;//.AllocDevice(true);
2719 }
2720 }
2721 { // Build FFTW plan.
2722 if(!vlist_ifft_flag){
2723 sctl::Vector<sctl::Long> dim_vec(PVFMM_COORD_DIM); dim_vec[0]=(sctl::Long)n1; dim_vec[1]=(sctl::Long)n1; dim_vec[2]=(sctl::Long)n1;
2724 vlist_ifft.Setup(sctl::FFT_Type::C2R, ker_dim1*chld_cnt, dim_vec);
2725 vlist_ifft_flag=true;
2726 }
2727 }
2728
2729 { // Offload section
2730 assert((size_t)buffer_.Dim()>=(size_t)(2*fftsize_out*omp_p));
2731 size_t n_out=ifft_vec.Dim();
2732 #pragma omp parallel for
2733 for(int pid=0; pid<omp_p; pid++){
2734 size_t node_start=(n_out*(pid ))/omp_p;
2735 size_t node_end =(n_out*(pid+1))/omp_p;
2736 sctl::Vector<Real_t> buffer0(fftsize_out, sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>(&buffer_[fftsize_out*(2*pid+0)], fftsize_out), false);
2737 sctl::Vector<Real_t> buffer1(fftsize_out, sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>(&buffer_[fftsize_out*(2*pid+1)], fftsize_out), false);
2738 for(size_t node_idx=node_start; node_idx<node_end; node_idx++){
2739 sctl::Vector<Real_t> dnward_check_fft(fftsize_out, sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>(&input_data[fftsize_out*node_idx], fftsize_out), false);
2740 sctl::Vector<Real_t> dnward_equiv(ker_dim1*n*dof*chld_cnt,sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>(&output_data[0] + ifft_vec[node_idx], ker_dim1*n*dof*chld_cnt),false);
2741
2742 //De-interleave data.
2743 for(size_t i=0;i<ker_dim1*dof;i++)
2744 for(size_t j=0;j<n3_;j++)
2745 for(size_t k=0;k<chld_cnt;k++){
2746 buffer0[2*(n3_*(ker_dim1*dof*k+i)+j)+0]=dnward_check_fft[2*(chld_cnt*(n3_*i+j)+k)+0];
2747 buffer0[2*(n3_*(ker_dim1*dof*k+i)+j)+1]=dnward_check_fft[2*(chld_cnt*(n3_*i+j)+k)+1];
2748 }
2749
2750 // Compute FFT.
2751 for(size_t i=0;i<dof;i++){
2752 sctl::Vector<Real_t> in_ (2*n3_*ker_dim1*chld_cnt, buffer0.begin()+i*2*n3_*ker_dim1*chld_cnt, false);
2753 sctl::Vector<Real_t> out_( n3 *ker_dim1*chld_cnt, buffer1.begin()+i*n3*ker_dim1*chld_cnt, false);
2754 vlist_ifft.Execute(in_, out_);
2755 }
2756
2757 // Rearrange downward check data.
2758 for(size_t k=0;k<n;k++){
2759 size_t idx=map[k];
2760 for(size_t j1=0;j1<dof;j1++)
2761 for(size_t j0=0;j0<chld_cnt;j0++)
2762 for(size_t i=0;i<ker_dim1;i++)
2763 dnward_equiv[ker_dim1*(n*(dof*j0+j1)+k)+i]+=buffer1[idx+(i+(j1+j0*dof)*ker_dim1)*n3]*ifft_scal[ker_dim1*node_idx+i];
2764 }
2765 }
2766 }
2767 }
2768}
2769
2770template<class Real_t>
2771inline void matmult_8x8x2(Real_t*& M_, Real_t*& IN0, Real_t*& IN1, Real_t*& OUT0, Real_t*& OUT1){
2772 // Generic code.
2773 Real_t out_reg000, out_reg001, out_reg010, out_reg011;
2774 Real_t out_reg100, out_reg101, out_reg110, out_reg111;
2775 Real_t in_reg000, in_reg001, in_reg010, in_reg011;
2776 Real_t in_reg100, in_reg101, in_reg110, in_reg111;
2777 Real_t m_reg000, m_reg001, m_reg010, m_reg011;
2778 Real_t m_reg100, m_reg101, m_reg110, m_reg111;
2779 //#pragma unroll
2780 for(int i1=0;i1<8;i1+=2){
2781 Real_t* IN0_=IN0;
2782 Real_t* IN1_=IN1;
2783
2784 out_reg000=OUT0[ 0]; out_reg001=OUT0[ 1];
2785 out_reg010=OUT0[ 2]; out_reg011=OUT0[ 3];
2786 out_reg100=OUT1[ 0]; out_reg101=OUT1[ 1];
2787 out_reg110=OUT1[ 2]; out_reg111=OUT1[ 3];
2788 //#pragma unroll
2789 for(int i2=0;i2<8;i2+=2){
2790 m_reg000=M_[ 0]; m_reg001=M_[ 1];
2791 m_reg010=M_[ 2]; m_reg011=M_[ 3];
2792 m_reg100=M_[16]; m_reg101=M_[17];
2793 m_reg110=M_[18]; m_reg111=M_[19];
2794
2795 in_reg000=IN0_[0]; in_reg001=IN0_[1];
2796 in_reg010=IN0_[2]; in_reg011=IN0_[3];
2797 in_reg100=IN1_[0]; in_reg101=IN1_[1];
2798 in_reg110=IN1_[2]; in_reg111=IN1_[3];
2799
2800 out_reg000 += m_reg000*in_reg000 - m_reg001*in_reg001;
2801 out_reg001 += m_reg000*in_reg001 + m_reg001*in_reg000;
2802 out_reg010 += m_reg010*in_reg000 - m_reg011*in_reg001;
2803 out_reg011 += m_reg010*in_reg001 + m_reg011*in_reg000;
2804
2805 out_reg000 += m_reg100*in_reg010 - m_reg101*in_reg011;
2806 out_reg001 += m_reg100*in_reg011 + m_reg101*in_reg010;
2807 out_reg010 += m_reg110*in_reg010 - m_reg111*in_reg011;
2808 out_reg011 += m_reg110*in_reg011 + m_reg111*in_reg010;
2809
2810 out_reg100 += m_reg000*in_reg100 - m_reg001*in_reg101;
2811 out_reg101 += m_reg000*in_reg101 + m_reg001*in_reg100;
2812 out_reg110 += m_reg010*in_reg100 - m_reg011*in_reg101;
2813 out_reg111 += m_reg010*in_reg101 + m_reg011*in_reg100;
2814
2815 out_reg100 += m_reg100*in_reg110 - m_reg101*in_reg111;
2816 out_reg101 += m_reg100*in_reg111 + m_reg101*in_reg110;
2817 out_reg110 += m_reg110*in_reg110 - m_reg111*in_reg111;
2818 out_reg111 += m_reg110*in_reg111 + m_reg111*in_reg110;
2819
2820 M_+=32; // Jump to (column+2).
2821 IN0_+=4;
2822 IN1_+=4;
2823 }
2824 OUT0[ 0]=out_reg000; OUT0[ 1]=out_reg001;
2825 OUT0[ 2]=out_reg010; OUT0[ 3]=out_reg011;
2826 OUT1[ 0]=out_reg100; OUT1[ 1]=out_reg101;
2827 OUT1[ 2]=out_reg110; OUT1[ 3]=out_reg111;
2828 M_+=4-64*2; // Jump back to first column (row+2).
2829 OUT0+=4;
2830 OUT1+=4;
2831 }
2832}
2833
2834#if defined(__AVX__) || defined(__SSE3__)
2835template<>
2836inline void matmult_8x8x2<double>(double*& M_, double*& IN0, double*& IN1, double*& OUT0, double*& OUT1){
2837#ifdef __AVX__ //AVX code.
2838 __m256d out00,out01,out10,out11;
2839 __m256d out20,out21,out30,out31;
2840 double* in0__ = IN0;
2841 double* in1__ = IN1;
2842
2843 out00 = _mm256_load_pd(OUT0);
2844 out01 = _mm256_load_pd(OUT1);
2845 out10 = _mm256_load_pd(OUT0+4);
2846 out11 = _mm256_load_pd(OUT1+4);
2847 out20 = _mm256_load_pd(OUT0+8);
2848 out21 = _mm256_load_pd(OUT1+8);
2849 out30 = _mm256_load_pd(OUT0+12);
2850 out31 = _mm256_load_pd(OUT1+12);
2851 for(int i2=0;i2<8;i2+=2){
2852 __m256d m00;
2853 __m256d ot00;
2854 __m256d mt0,mtt0;
2855 __m256d in00,in00_r,in01,in01_r;
2856 in00 = _mm256_broadcast_pd((const __m128d*)in0__);
2857 in00_r = _mm256_permute_pd(in00,5);
2858 in01 = _mm256_broadcast_pd((const __m128d*)in1__);
2859 in01_r = _mm256_permute_pd(in01,5);
2860
2861 m00 = _mm256_load_pd(M_);
2862
2863 mt0 = _mm256_unpacklo_pd(m00,m00);
2864 ot00 = _mm256_mul_pd(mt0,in00);
2865 mtt0 = _mm256_unpackhi_pd(m00,m00);
2866 out00 = _mm256_add_pd(out00,_mm256_addsub_pd(ot00,_mm256_mul_pd(mtt0,in00_r)));
2867
2868 ot00 = _mm256_mul_pd(mt0,in01);
2869 out01 = _mm256_add_pd(out01,_mm256_addsub_pd(ot00,_mm256_mul_pd(mtt0,in01_r)));
2870
2871 m00 = _mm256_load_pd(M_+4);
2872
2873 mt0 = _mm256_unpacklo_pd(m00,m00);
2874 ot00 = _mm256_mul_pd(mt0,in00);
2875 mtt0 = _mm256_unpackhi_pd(m00,m00);
2876 out10 = _mm256_add_pd(out10,_mm256_addsub_pd(ot00,_mm256_mul_pd(mtt0,in00_r)));
2877
2878 ot00 = _mm256_mul_pd(mt0,in01);
2879 out11 = _mm256_add_pd(out11,_mm256_addsub_pd(ot00,_mm256_mul_pd(mtt0,in01_r)));
2880
2881 m00 = _mm256_load_pd(M_+8);
2882
2883 mt0 = _mm256_unpacklo_pd(m00,m00);
2884 ot00 = _mm256_mul_pd(mt0,in00);
2885 mtt0 = _mm256_unpackhi_pd(m00,m00);
2886 out20 = _mm256_add_pd(out20,_mm256_addsub_pd(ot00,_mm256_mul_pd(mtt0,in00_r)));
2887
2888 ot00 = _mm256_mul_pd(mt0,in01);
2889 out21 = _mm256_add_pd(out21,_mm256_addsub_pd(ot00,_mm256_mul_pd(mtt0,in01_r)));
2890
2891 m00 = _mm256_load_pd(M_+12);
2892
2893 mt0 = _mm256_unpacklo_pd(m00,m00);
2894 ot00 = _mm256_mul_pd(mt0,in00);
2895 mtt0 = _mm256_unpackhi_pd(m00,m00);
2896 out30 = _mm256_add_pd(out30,_mm256_addsub_pd(ot00,_mm256_mul_pd(mtt0,in00_r)));
2897
2898 ot00 = _mm256_mul_pd(mt0,in01);
2899 out31 = _mm256_add_pd(out31,_mm256_addsub_pd(ot00,_mm256_mul_pd(mtt0,in01_r)));
2900
2901
2902 in00 = _mm256_broadcast_pd((const __m128d*) (in0__+2));
2903 in00_r = _mm256_permute_pd(in00,5);
2904 in01 = _mm256_broadcast_pd((const __m128d*) (in1__+2));
2905 in01_r = _mm256_permute_pd(in01,5);
2906
2907 m00 = _mm256_load_pd(M_+16);
2908
2909 mt0 = _mm256_unpacklo_pd(m00,m00);
2910 ot00 = _mm256_mul_pd(mt0,in00);
2911 mtt0 = _mm256_unpackhi_pd(m00,m00);
2912 out00 = _mm256_add_pd(out00,_mm256_addsub_pd(ot00,_mm256_mul_pd(mtt0,in00_r)));
2913
2914 ot00 = _mm256_mul_pd(mt0,in01);
2915 out01 = _mm256_add_pd(out01,_mm256_addsub_pd(ot00,_mm256_mul_pd(mtt0,in01_r)));
2916
2917 m00 = _mm256_load_pd(M_+20);
2918
2919 mt0 = _mm256_unpacklo_pd(m00,m00);
2920 ot00 = _mm256_mul_pd(mt0,in00);
2921 mtt0 = _mm256_unpackhi_pd(m00,m00);
2922 out10 = _mm256_add_pd(out10,_mm256_addsub_pd(ot00,_mm256_mul_pd(mtt0,in00_r)));
2923
2924 ot00 = _mm256_mul_pd(mt0,in01);
2925 out11 = _mm256_add_pd(out11,_mm256_addsub_pd(ot00,_mm256_mul_pd(mtt0,in01_r)));
2926
2927 m00 = _mm256_load_pd(M_+24);
2928
2929 mt0 = _mm256_unpacklo_pd(m00,m00);
2930 ot00 = _mm256_mul_pd(mt0,in00);
2931 mtt0 = _mm256_unpackhi_pd(m00,m00);
2932 out20 = _mm256_add_pd(out20,_mm256_addsub_pd(ot00,_mm256_mul_pd(mtt0,in00_r)));
2933
2934 ot00 = _mm256_mul_pd(mt0,in01);
2935 out21 = _mm256_add_pd(out21,_mm256_addsub_pd(ot00,_mm256_mul_pd(mtt0,in01_r)));
2936
2937 m00 = _mm256_load_pd(M_+28);
2938
2939 mt0 = _mm256_unpacklo_pd(m00,m00);
2940 ot00 = _mm256_mul_pd(mt0,in00);
2941 mtt0 = _mm256_unpackhi_pd(m00,m00);
2942 out30 = _mm256_add_pd(out30,_mm256_addsub_pd(ot00,_mm256_mul_pd(mtt0,in00_r)));
2943
2944 ot00 = _mm256_mul_pd(mt0,in01);
2945 out31 = _mm256_add_pd(out31,_mm256_addsub_pd(ot00,_mm256_mul_pd(mtt0,in01_r)));
2946
2947 M_ += 32;
2948 in0__ += 4;
2949 in1__ += 4;
2950 }
2951 _mm256_store_pd(OUT0,out00);
2952 _mm256_store_pd(OUT1,out01);
2953 _mm256_store_pd(OUT0+4,out10);
2954 _mm256_store_pd(OUT1+4,out11);
2955 _mm256_store_pd(OUT0+8,out20);
2956 _mm256_store_pd(OUT1+8,out21);
2957 _mm256_store_pd(OUT0+12,out30);
2958 _mm256_store_pd(OUT1+12,out31);
2959#elif defined __SSE3__ // SSE code.
2960 __m128d out00, out01, out10, out11;
2961 __m128d in00, in01, in10, in11;
2962 __m128d m00, m01, m10, m11;
2963 //#pragma unroll
2964 for(int i1=0;i1<8;i1+=2){
2965 double* IN0_=IN0;
2966 double* IN1_=IN1;
2967
2968 out00 =_mm_load_pd (OUT0 );
2969 out10 =_mm_load_pd (OUT0+2);
2970 out01 =_mm_load_pd (OUT1 );
2971 out11 =_mm_load_pd (OUT1+2);
2972 //#pragma unroll
2973 for(int i2=0;i2<8;i2+=2){
2974 m00 =_mm_load1_pd (M_ );
2975 m10 =_mm_load1_pd (M_+ 2);
2976 m01 =_mm_load1_pd (M_+16);
2977 m11 =_mm_load1_pd (M_+18);
2978
2979 in00 =_mm_load_pd (IN0_ );
2980 in10 =_mm_load_pd (IN0_+2);
2981 in01 =_mm_load_pd (IN1_ );
2982 in11 =_mm_load_pd (IN1_+2);
2983
2984 out00 = _mm_add_pd (out00, _mm_mul_pd(m00 , in00 ));
2985 out00 = _mm_add_pd (out00, _mm_mul_pd(m01 , in10 ));
2986 out01 = _mm_add_pd (out01, _mm_mul_pd(m00 , in01 ));
2987 out01 = _mm_add_pd (out01, _mm_mul_pd(m01 , in11 ));
2988 out10 = _mm_add_pd (out10, _mm_mul_pd(m10 , in00 ));
2989 out10 = _mm_add_pd (out10, _mm_mul_pd(m11 , in10 ));
2990 out11 = _mm_add_pd (out11, _mm_mul_pd(m10 , in01 ));
2991 out11 = _mm_add_pd (out11, _mm_mul_pd(m11 , in11 ));
2992
2993
2994 m00 =_mm_load1_pd (M_+ 1);
2995 m10 =_mm_load1_pd (M_+ 2+1);
2996 m01 =_mm_load1_pd (M_+16+1);
2997 m11 =_mm_load1_pd (M_+18+1);
2998 in00 =_mm_shuffle_pd (in00,in00,_MM_SHUFFLE2(0,1));
2999 in01 =_mm_shuffle_pd (in01,in01,_MM_SHUFFLE2(0,1));
3000 in10 =_mm_shuffle_pd (in10,in10,_MM_SHUFFLE2(0,1));
3001 in11 =_mm_shuffle_pd (in11,in11,_MM_SHUFFLE2(0,1));
3002 out00 = _mm_addsub_pd(out00, _mm_mul_pd(m00, in00));
3003 out00 = _mm_addsub_pd(out00, _mm_mul_pd(m01, in10));
3004 out01 = _mm_addsub_pd(out01, _mm_mul_pd(m00, in01));
3005 out01 = _mm_addsub_pd(out01, _mm_mul_pd(m01, in11));
3006 out10 = _mm_addsub_pd(out10, _mm_mul_pd(m10, in00));
3007 out10 = _mm_addsub_pd(out10, _mm_mul_pd(m11, in10));
3008 out11 = _mm_addsub_pd(out11, _mm_mul_pd(m10, in01));
3009 out11 = _mm_addsub_pd(out11, _mm_mul_pd(m11, in11));
3010
3011 M_+=32; // Jump to (column+2).
3012 IN0_+=4;
3013 IN1_+=4;
3014 }
3015 _mm_store_pd (OUT0 ,out00);
3016 _mm_store_pd (OUT0+2,out10);
3017 _mm_store_pd (OUT1 ,out01);
3018 _mm_store_pd (OUT1+2,out11);
3019 M_+=4-64*2; // Jump back to first column (row+2).
3020 OUT0+=4;
3021 OUT1+=4;
3022 }
3023#endif
3024}

3159
3160template<class Real_t>
3161inline void matmult_8x8x1(Real_t*& M_, Real_t*& IN0, Real_t*& OUT0){
3162 // Generic code: single-vector variant of matmult_8x8x2, used for the odd
3163 // tail of an interaction block (no dummy padding lanes needed).
3164 Real_t out_reg000, out_reg001, out_reg010, out_reg011;
3165 Real_t in_reg000, in_reg001, in_reg010, in_reg011;
3166 Real_t m_reg000, m_reg001, m_reg010, m_reg011;
3167 Real_t m_reg100, m_reg101, m_reg110, m_reg111;
3168 for(int i1=0;i1<8;i1+=2){
3169 Real_t* IN0_=IN0;
3170 out_reg000=OUT0[ 0]; out_reg001=OUT0[ 1];
3171 out_reg010=OUT0[ 2]; out_reg011=OUT0[ 3];
3172 for(int i2=0;i2<8;i2+=2){
3173 m_reg000=M_[ 0]; m_reg001=M_[ 1];
3174 m_reg010=M_[ 2]; m_reg011=M_[ 3];
3175 m_reg100=M_[16]; m_reg101=M_[17];
3176 m_reg110=M_[18]; m_reg111=M_[19];
3177
3178 in_reg000=IN0_[0]; in_reg001=IN0_[1];
3179 in_reg010=IN0_[2]; in_reg011=IN0_[3];
3180
3181 out_reg000 += m_reg000*in_reg000 - m_reg001*in_reg001;
3182 out_reg001 += m_reg000*in_reg001 + m_reg001*in_reg000;
3183 out_reg010 += m_reg010*in_reg000 - m_reg011*in_reg001;
3184 out_reg011 += m_reg010*in_reg001 + m_reg011*in_reg000;
3185
3186 out_reg000 += m_reg100*in_reg010 - m_reg101*in_reg011;
3187 out_reg001 += m_reg100*in_reg011 + m_reg101*in_reg010;
3188 out_reg010 += m_reg110*in_reg010 - m_reg111*in_reg011;
3189 out_reg011 += m_reg110*in_reg011 + m_reg111*in_reg010;
3190
3191 M_+=32; // Jump to (column+2).
3192 IN0_+=4;
3193 }
3194 OUT0[ 0]=out_reg000; OUT0[ 1]=out_reg001;
3195 OUT0[ 2]=out_reg010; OUT0[ 3]=out_reg011;
3196 M_+=4-64*2; // Jump back to first column (row+2).
3197 OUT0+=4;
3198 }
3199}

3405template <class Real_t>
3406void VListHadamard(size_t dof, size_t M_dim, size_t ker_dim0, size_t ker_dim1, sctl::Vector<size_t>& interac_dsp,
3407 sctl::Vector<size_t>& interac_vec, sctl::Vector<Real_t*>& precomp_mat, sctl::Vector<Real_t>& fft_in, sctl::Vector<Real_t>& fft_out){
3408
3409 size_t chld_cnt=1UL<<PVFMM_COORD_DIM;
3410 size_t fftsize_out=M_dim*ker_dim1*chld_cnt*2;
3411 size_t n_out=fft_out.Dim()/fftsize_out;
3412
3413 // Set buff_out to zero.
3414 #pragma omp parallel for
3415 for(size_t k=0;k<n_out;k++){
3416 sctl::Vector<Real_t> dnward_check_fft(fftsize_out, sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>(&fft_out[k*fftsize_out], fftsize_out), false);
3417 dnward_check_fft.SetZero();
3418 }
3419
3420 // Build list of interaction pairs (in, out vectors).
3421 size_t mat_cnt=precomp_mat.Dim();
3422 size_t blk1_cnt=interac_dsp.Dim()/mat_cnt;
3423 const size_t V_BLK_SIZE=PVFMM_V_BLK_CACHE*64/sizeof(Real_t);
3424 // The pointer tables are fully (re)written each call, so recycled scratch
3425 // pages are fine here; ScratchBuf avoids per-call mmap/page-fault churn.
3426 sctl::ScratchBuf<Real_t*> IN_scratch (2*V_BLK_SIZE*blk1_cnt*mat_cnt);
3427 sctl::ScratchBuf<Real_t*> OUT_scratch(2*V_BLK_SIZE*blk1_cnt*mat_cnt);
3428 Real_t** IN_ = &IN_scratch .begin()[0];
3429 Real_t** OUT_ = &OUT_scratch.begin()[0];
3430 #pragma omp parallel for
3431 for(size_t interac_blk1=0; interac_blk1<blk1_cnt*mat_cnt; interac_blk1++){
3432 size_t interac_dsp0 = (interac_blk1==0?0:interac_dsp[interac_blk1-1]);
3433 size_t interac_dsp1 = interac_dsp[interac_blk1 ] ;
3434 size_t interac_cnt = interac_dsp1-interac_dsp0;
3435 for(size_t j=0;j<interac_cnt;j++){
3436 IN_ [2*V_BLK_SIZE*interac_blk1 +j]=&fft_in [interac_vec[(interac_dsp0+j)*2+0]];
3437 OUT_[2*V_BLK_SIZE*interac_blk1 +j]=&fft_out[interac_vec[(interac_dsp0+j)*2+1]];
3438 }
3439 }
3440
3441 int omp_p=omp_get_max_threads();
3442 #pragma omp parallel for
3443 for(int pid=0; pid<omp_p; pid++){
3444 size_t a=( pid *M_dim)/omp_p;
3445 size_t b=((pid+1)*M_dim)/omp_p;
3446
3447 for(size_t in_dim=0;in_dim<ker_dim0;in_dim++)
3448 for(size_t ot_dim=0;ot_dim<ker_dim1;ot_dim++)
3449 for(size_t blk1=0; blk1<blk1_cnt; blk1++)
3450 for(size_t k=a; k< b; k++)
3451 for(size_t mat_indx=0; mat_indx< mat_cnt;mat_indx++){
3452 size_t interac_blk1 = blk1*mat_cnt+mat_indx;
3453 size_t interac_dsp0 = (interac_blk1==0?0:interac_dsp[interac_blk1-1]);
3454 size_t interac_dsp1 = interac_dsp[interac_blk1 ] ;
3455 size_t interac_cnt = interac_dsp1-interac_dsp0;
3456
3457 Real_t** IN = IN_ + 2*V_BLK_SIZE*interac_blk1;
3458 Real_t** OUT= OUT_+ 2*V_BLK_SIZE*interac_blk1;
3459
3460 Real_t* M = precomp_mat[mat_indx] + k*chld_cnt*chld_cnt*2 + (ot_dim+in_dim*ker_dim1)*M_dim*128;
3461 {
3462 for(size_t j=0;j+1<interac_cnt;j+=2){
3463 Real_t* M_ = M;
3464 Real_t* IN0 = IN [j+0] + (in_dim*M_dim+k)*chld_cnt*2;
3465 Real_t* IN1 = IN [j+1] + (in_dim*M_dim+k)*chld_cnt*2;
3466 Real_t* OUT0 = OUT[j+0] + (ot_dim*M_dim+k)*chld_cnt*2;
3467 Real_t* OUT1 = OUT[j+1] + (ot_dim*M_dim+k)*chld_cnt*2;
3468
3469#ifdef __SSE__
3470 if (j+2 < interac_cnt) { // Prefetch
3471 _mm_prefetch(((char *)(IN[j+2] + (in_dim*M_dim+k)*chld_cnt*2)), _MM_HINT_T0);
3472 _mm_prefetch(((char *)(IN[j+2] + (in_dim*M_dim+k)*chld_cnt*2) + 64), _MM_HINT_T0);
3473 _mm_prefetch(((char *)(IN[j+3] + (in_dim*M_dim+k)*chld_cnt*2)), _MM_HINT_T0);
3474 _mm_prefetch(((char *)(IN[j+3] + (in_dim*M_dim+k)*chld_cnt*2) + 64), _MM_HINT_T0);
3475
3476 _mm_prefetch(((char *)(OUT[j+2] + (ot_dim*M_dim+k)*chld_cnt*2)), _MM_HINT_T0);
3477 _mm_prefetch(((char *)(OUT[j+2] + (ot_dim*M_dim+k)*chld_cnt*2) + 64), _MM_HINT_T0);
3478 _mm_prefetch(((char *)(OUT[j+3] + (ot_dim*M_dim+k)*chld_cnt*2)), _MM_HINT_T0);
3479 _mm_prefetch(((char *)(OUT[j+3] + (ot_dim*M_dim+k)*chld_cnt*2) + 64), _MM_HINT_T0);
3480 }
3481#endif
3482
3483 matmult_8x8x2(M_, IN0, IN1, OUT0, OUT1);
3484 }
3485 if(interac_cnt & 1){ // Odd tail: single-vector kernel, no dummy lanes.
3486 size_t j=interac_cnt-1;
3487 Real_t* M_ = M;
3488 Real_t* IN0 = IN [j] + (in_dim*M_dim+k)*chld_cnt*2;
3489 Real_t* OUT0 = OUT[j] + (ot_dim*M_dim+k)*chld_cnt*2;
3490 matmult_8x8x1(M_, IN0, OUT0);
3491 }
3492 }
3493 }
3494 }
3495
3496 // Compute flops.
3497 {
3498 sctl::Profile::IncrementCounter(sctl::ProfileCounter::FLOP, 8*8*8*(interac_vec.Dim()/2)*M_dim*ker_dim0*ker_dim1*dof);
3499 }
3500
3501 // IN_/OUT_ scratch freed automatically at scope exit (LIFO).
3502}
3503
3504template <class FMMNode>
3505void FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::V_ListSetup(SetupData<FMMNode_t>& setup_data, FMMTree_t* tree, std::vector<sctl::Matrix<Real_t> >& buff, std::vector<sctl::Vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>> >& n_list, int level, bool device){
3506 if(!this->MultipoleOrder()) return;
3507 if(level==0) return;
3508 { // Set setup_data
3509 setup_data.level=level;
3510 setup_data.kernel=kernel->k_m2l;
3511 setup_data.interac_type.resize(1);
3512 setup_data.interac_type[0]=V1_Type;
3513
3514 setup_data. input_data=&buff[0];
3515 setup_data.input_data_mirror=&tree->node_data_buff_mirror[0];
3516 setup_data.output_data=&buff[1];
3517 setup_data.output_data_mirror=&tree->node_data_buff_mirror[1];
3518 sctl::Vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& nodes_in =n_list[2];
3519 sctl::Vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& nodes_out=n_list[3];
3520
3521 setup_data.nodes_in .clear();
3522 setup_data.nodes_out.clear();
3523 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<nodes_in .Dim();i++) if((nodes_in [i]->Depth()==level-1 || level==-1) && nodes_in [i]->pt_cnt[0]) setup_data.nodes_in .push_back(nodes_in [i]);
3524 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<nodes_out.Dim();i++) if((nodes_out[i]->Depth()==level-1 || level==-1) && nodes_out[i]->pt_cnt[1]) setup_data.nodes_out.push_back(nodes_out[i]);
3525 }
3526 std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& nodes_in =setup_data.nodes_in ;
3527 std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& nodes_out=setup_data.nodes_out;
3528 std::vector<sctl::Vector<Real_t>*>& input_vector=setup_data. input_vector; input_vector.clear();
3529 std::vector<sctl::Vector<Real_t>*>& output_vector=setup_data.output_vector; output_vector.clear();
3530 for(size_t i=0;i<nodes_in .size();i++) input_vector.push_back(&((FMMData*)((sctl::Iterator<FMMNode>)(nodes_in[i])->Child(0))->FMMData())->upward_equiv);
3531 for(size_t i=0;i<nodes_out.size();i++) output_vector.push_back(&((FMMData*)((sctl::Iterator<FMMNode>)(nodes_out[i])->Child(0))->FMMData())->dnward_equiv);
3532
3533 /////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
3534
3535 size_t n_in =nodes_in .size();
3536 size_t n_out=nodes_out.size();
3537
3538 // Setup precomputed data.
3539 //if(setup_data.precomp_data->Dim(0)*setup_data.precomp_data->Dim(1)==0) SetupPrecomp(setup_data,device);
3540
3541 // Build interac_data
3542 sctl::Profile::Tic("Interac-Data",&this->sctl_comm,true,25);
3543 sctl::Matrix<char>& interac_data=setup_data.interac_data;
3544 if(n_out>0 && n_in >0){ // Build precomp_data, interac_data
3545
3546 //size_t precomp_offset=0;
3547 Mat_Type& interac_type=setup_data.interac_type[0];
3548 size_t mat_cnt=this->interac_list.ListCount(interac_type);
3549 sctl::Matrix<size_t> precomp_data_offset;
3550 std::vector<size_t> interac_mat;
3551 std::vector<Real_t*> interac_mat_ptr;
3552#if 0 // Since we skip SetupPrecomp for V-list
3553 { // Load precomp_data for interac_type.
3554 struct HeaderData{
3555 size_t total_size;
3556 size_t level;
3557 size_t mat_cnt ;
3558 size_t max_depth;
3559 };
3560 sctl::Matrix<char>& precomp_data=*setup_data.precomp_data;
3561 char* indx_ptr=&precomp_data[0][0]+precomp_offset;
3562 HeaderData& header=*(HeaderData*)indx_ptr;indx_ptr+=sizeof(HeaderData);
3563 precomp_data_offset.ReInit(header.mat_cnt,1+(2+2)*header.max_depth, (size_t*)indx_ptr, false);
3564 precomp_offset+=header.total_size;
3565 for(size_t mat_id=0;mat_id<mat_cnt;mat_id++){
3566 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& M0 = this->mat->Mat(level, interac_type, mat_id);
3567 assert(M0.Dim(0)>0 && M0.Dim(1)>0); PVFMM_UNUSED(M0);
3568 interac_mat.push_back(precomp_data_offset[mat_id][0]);
3569 }
3570 }
3571#else
3572 {
3573 for(size_t mat_id=0;mat_id<mat_cnt;mat_id++){
3574 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& M = this->mat->Mat(level, interac_type, mat_id);
3575 interac_mat_ptr.push_back(&M[0][0]);
3576 }
3577 }
3578#endif
3579
3580 size_t dof;
3581 size_t m=MultipoleOrder();
3582 size_t ker_dim0=setup_data.kernel->ker_dim[0];
3583 size_t ker_dim1=setup_data.kernel->ker_dim[1];
3584 size_t fftsize;
3585 {
3586 size_t n1=m*2;
3587 size_t n2=n1*n1;
3588 size_t n3_=n2*(n1/2+1);
3589 size_t chld_cnt=1UL<<PVFMM_COORD_DIM;
3590 fftsize=2*n3_*chld_cnt;
3591 dof=1;
3592 }
3593
3594 int omp_p=omp_get_max_threads();
3595 size_t buff_size=PVFMM_DEVICE_BUFFER_SIZE*1024l*1024l;
3596 size_t n_blk0=2*fftsize*dof*(ker_dim0*n_in +ker_dim1*n_out)*sizeof(Real_t)/buff_size;
3597 if(n_blk0==0) n_blk0=1;
3598
3599 std::vector<std::vector<size_t> > fft_vec(n_blk0);
3600 std::vector<std::vector<size_t> > ifft_vec(n_blk0);
3601 std::vector<std::vector<Real_t> > fft_scl(n_blk0);
3602 std::vector<std::vector<Real_t> > ifft_scl(n_blk0);
3603 std::vector<std::vector<size_t> > interac_vec(n_blk0);
3604 std::vector<std::vector<size_t> > interac_dsp(n_blk0);
3605 {
3606 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& input_data=*setup_data. input_data;
3607 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>& output_data=*setup_data.output_data;
3608 std::vector<std::vector<FMMNode*> > nodes_blk_in (n_blk0);
3609 std::vector<std::vector<FMMNode*> > nodes_blk_out(n_blk0);
3610
3611 sctl::Vector<Real_t> src_scal=this->kernel->k_m2l->src_scal;
3612 sctl::Vector<Real_t> trg_scal=this->kernel->k_m2l->trg_scal;
3613
3614 for(size_t i=0;i<n_in;i++) (nodes_in[i])->node_id=i;
3615 for(size_t blk0=0;blk0<n_blk0;blk0++){
3616 size_t blk0_start=(n_out* blk0 )/n_blk0;
3617 size_t blk0_end =(n_out*(blk0+1))/n_blk0;
3618
3619 std::vector<FMMNode*>& nodes_in_ =nodes_blk_in [blk0];
3620 std::vector<FMMNode*>& nodes_out_=nodes_blk_out[blk0];
3621 { // Build node list for blk0.
3622 std::set<void*> nodes_in;
3623 for(size_t i=blk0_start;i<blk0_end;i++){
3624 nodes_out_.push_back(&nodes_out[i][0]); // terminal decay (read-only block scratch)
3625 sctl::Vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode>>& lst=(nodes_out[i])->interac_list[interac_type];
3626 for(size_t k=0;k<mat_cnt;k++) if(lst[k]!=sctl::NullIterator<FMMNode>() && lst[k]->pt_cnt[0]) nodes_in.insert(&lst[k][0]);
3627 }
3628 for(std::set<void*>::iterator node=nodes_in.begin(); node != nodes_in.end(); node++){
3629 nodes_in_.push_back((FMMNode*)*node);
3630 }
3631
3632 size_t input_dim=nodes_in_ .size()*ker_dim0*dof*fftsize;
3633 size_t output_dim=nodes_out_.size()*ker_dim1*dof*fftsize;
3634 size_t buffer_dim=2*(ker_dim0+ker_dim1)*dof*fftsize*omp_p;
3635 if(buff_size<(input_dim + output_dim + buffer_dim)*sizeof(Real_t))
3636 buff_size=(input_dim + output_dim + buffer_dim)*sizeof(Real_t);
3637 }
3638
3639 { // Set fft vectors.
3640 for(size_t i=0;i<nodes_in_ .size();i++) fft_vec[blk0].push_back((size_t)(& input_vector[nodes_in_[i]->node_id][0][0]-& input_data[0][0]));
3641 for(size_t i=0;i<nodes_out_.size();i++)ifft_vec[blk0].push_back((size_t)(&output_vector[blk0_start + i ][0][0]-&output_data[0][0]));
3642
3643 size_t scal_dim0=src_scal.Dim();
3644 size_t scal_dim1=trg_scal.Dim();
3645 fft_scl [blk0].resize(nodes_in_ .size()*scal_dim0);
3646 ifft_scl[blk0].resize(nodes_out_.size()*scal_dim1);
3647 for(size_t i=0;i<nodes_in_ .size();i++){
3648 size_t depth=nodes_in_[i]->Depth()+1;
3649 for(size_t j=0;j<scal_dim0;j++){
3650 fft_scl[blk0][i*scal_dim0+j]=sctl::pow<Real_t>(2.0, src_scal[j]*depth);
3651 }
3652 }
3653 for(size_t i=0;i<nodes_out_.size();i++){
3654 size_t depth=nodes_out_[i]->Depth()+1;
3655 for(size_t j=0;j<scal_dim1;j++){
3656 ifft_scl[blk0][i*scal_dim1+j]=sctl::pow<Real_t>(2.0, trg_scal[j]*depth);
3657 }
3658 }
3659 }
3660 }
3661
3662 for(size_t blk0=0;blk0<n_blk0;blk0++){ // Hadamard interactions.
3663 std::vector<FMMNode*>& nodes_in_ =nodes_blk_in [blk0];
3664 std::vector<FMMNode*>& nodes_out_=nodes_blk_out[blk0];
3665 for(size_t i=0;i<nodes_in_.size();i++) nodes_in_[i]->node_id=i;
3666 { // Next blocking level.
3667 size_t n_blk1=nodes_out_.size()*(2)*sizeof(Real_t)/(64*PVFMM_V_BLK_CACHE);
3668 if(n_blk1==0) n_blk1=1;
3669
3670 size_t interac_dsp_=0;
3671 for(size_t blk1=0;blk1<n_blk1;blk1++){
3672 size_t blk1_start=(nodes_out_.size()* blk1 )/n_blk1;
3673 size_t blk1_end =(nodes_out_.size()*(blk1+1))/n_blk1;
3674 for(size_t k=0;k<mat_cnt;k++){
3675 for(size_t i=blk1_start;i<blk1_end;i++){
3676 sctl::Vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode>>& lst=(nodes_out_[i])->interac_list[interac_type];
3677 if(lst[k]!=sctl::NullIterator<FMMNode>() && lst[k]->pt_cnt[0]){
3678 interac_vec[blk0].push_back(lst[k]->node_id*fftsize*ker_dim0*dof);
3679 interac_vec[blk0].push_back( i *fftsize*ker_dim1*dof);
3680 interac_dsp_++;
3681 }
3682 }
3683 interac_dsp[blk0].push_back(interac_dsp_);
3684 }
3685 }
3686 }
3687 }
3688 }
3689
3690 { // Set interac_data.
3691 size_t data_size=sizeof(size_t)*6; // buff_size, m, dof, ker_dim0, ker_dim1, n_blk0
3692 for(size_t blk0=0;blk0<n_blk0;blk0++){
3693 data_size+=sizeof(size_t)+ fft_vec[blk0].size()*sizeof(size_t);
3694 data_size+=sizeof(size_t)+ ifft_vec[blk0].size()*sizeof(size_t);
3695 data_size+=sizeof(size_t)+(( fft_scl[blk0].size()*sizeof(Real_t)+sizeof(size_t)-1)/sizeof(size_t))*sizeof(size_t); // Real_t segments are padded to size_t alignment
3696 data_size+=sizeof(size_t)+(( ifft_scl[blk0].size()*sizeof(Real_t)+sizeof(size_t)-1)/sizeof(size_t))*sizeof(size_t);
3697 data_size+=sizeof(size_t)+interac_vec[blk0].size()*sizeof(size_t);
3698 data_size+=sizeof(size_t)+interac_dsp[blk0].size()*sizeof(size_t);
3699 }
3700 data_size+=sizeof(size_t)+interac_mat.size()*sizeof(size_t);
3701 data_size+=sizeof(size_t)+interac_mat_ptr.size()*sizeof(Real_t*);
3702 if(data_size>(size_t)(interac_data.Dim(0)*interac_data.Dim(1))){
3703 setup_data.interac_data_mirror.Free(); // host buffer is about to be reallocated
3704 interac_data.ReInit(1,data_size);
3705 }
3706 char* data_ptr=&interac_data[0][0];
3707
3708 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]=buff_size; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
3709 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]= m; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
3710 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]= dof; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
3711 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]= ker_dim0; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
3712 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]= ker_dim1; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
3713 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]= n_blk0; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
3714
3715 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]= interac_mat.size(); data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
3716 if (interac_mat.size()) memcpy(data_ptr, &interac_mat[0], interac_mat.size()*sizeof(size_t));
3717 data_ptr+=interac_mat.size()*sizeof(size_t);
3718
3719 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]= interac_mat_ptr.size(); data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
3720 if (interac_mat_ptr.size()) memcpy(data_ptr, &interac_mat_ptr[0], interac_mat_ptr.size()*sizeof(Real_t*));
3721 data_ptr+=interac_mat_ptr.size()*sizeof(Real_t*);
3722
3723 for(size_t blk0=0;blk0<n_blk0;blk0++){
3724 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]= fft_vec[blk0].size(); data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
3725 if (fft_vec[blk0].size()) memcpy(data_ptr, & fft_vec[blk0][0], fft_vec[blk0].size()*sizeof(size_t));
3726 data_ptr+= fft_vec[blk0].size()*sizeof(size_t);
3727
3728 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]=ifft_vec[blk0].size(); data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
3729 if (ifft_vec[blk0].size()) memcpy(data_ptr, &ifft_vec[blk0][0], ifft_vec[blk0].size()*sizeof(size_t));
3730 data_ptr+=ifft_vec[blk0].size()*sizeof(size_t);
3731
3732 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]= fft_scl[blk0].size(); data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
3733 if (fft_scl[blk0].size()) memcpy(data_ptr, & fft_scl[blk0][0], fft_scl[blk0].size()*sizeof(Real_t));
3734 data_ptr+=(( fft_scl[blk0].size()*sizeof(Real_t)+sizeof(size_t)-1)/sizeof(size_t))*sizeof(size_t);
3735
3736 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]=ifft_scl[blk0].size(); data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
3737 if (ifft_scl[blk0].size()) memcpy(data_ptr, &ifft_scl[blk0][0], ifft_scl[blk0].size()*sizeof(Real_t));
3738 data_ptr+=((ifft_scl[blk0].size()*sizeof(Real_t)+sizeof(size_t)-1)/sizeof(size_t))*sizeof(size_t);
3739
3740 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]=interac_vec[blk0].size(); data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
3741 if (interac_vec[blk0].size()) memcpy(data_ptr, &interac_vec[blk0][0], interac_vec[blk0].size()*sizeof(size_t));
3742 data_ptr+=interac_vec[blk0].size()*sizeof(size_t);
3743
3744 ((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]=interac_dsp[blk0].size(); data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
3745 if (interac_dsp[blk0].size()) memcpy(data_ptr, &interac_dsp[blk0][0], interac_dsp[blk0].size()*sizeof(size_t));
3746 data_ptr+=interac_dsp[blk0].size()*sizeof(size_t);
3747 }
3748 }
3749 }
3750 sctl::Profile::Toc();
3751
3752 if(device){ // Host2Device
3753 sctl::Profile::Tic("Host2Device",&this->sctl_comm,false,25);
3754 setup_data.interac_data_mirror.AllocDevice(setup_data.interac_data,true);
3755 sctl::Profile::Toc();
3756 }
3757}
3758
3759template <class FMMNode>
3760void FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::V_List (SetupData<FMMNode_t>& setup_data, bool device){
3761 if(!this->MultipoleOrder()) return;
3762 assert(!device); //Can not run on accelerator yet.
3763
3764 int np;
3765 np = sctl_comm.Size();
3766 if(setup_data.interac_data.Dim(0)==0 || setup_data.interac_data.Dim(1)==0){
3767 if(np>1) sctl::Profile::Tic("Host2Device",&this->sctl_comm,false,25);
3768 if(np>1) sctl::Profile::Toc();
3769 return;
3770 }
3771
3772 sctl::Profile::Tic("Host2Device",&this->sctl_comm,false,25);
3773 //int level=setup_data.level;
3774 size_t buff_size=*((size_t*)&setup_data.interac_data[0][0]);
3775 DeviceVector<char> buff;
3776 //DeviceMatrix<char> precomp_data;
3777 DeviceMatrix<char> interac_data;
3778 DeviceMatrix<Real_t> input_data;
3779 DeviceMatrix<Real_t> output_data;
3780
3781 if(device){
3782 if((size_t)this->dev_buffer.Dim()<buff_size){
3783 this->dev_buffer_mirror.Free(); // host buffer is about to be reallocated
3784 this->dev_buffer.ReInit(buff_size);
3785 }
3786 buff = this->dev_buffer_mirror.AllocDevice(this->dev_buffer,false);
3787 //precomp_data= setup_data.precomp_data_mirror->AllocDevice(*setup_data.precomp_data,false);
3788 interac_data= setup_data.interac_data_mirror.AllocDevice(setup_data.interac_data,false);
3789 input_data = setup_data.input_data_mirror->AllocDevice(*setup_data.input_data,false);
3790 output_data = setup_data.output_data_mirror->AllocDevice(*setup_data.output_data,false);
3791 }else{
3792 if((size_t)this->dev_buffer.Dim()<buff_size){
3793 this->dev_buffer_mirror.Free(); // host buffer is about to be reallocated
3794 this->dev_buffer.ReInit(buff_size);
3795 }
3796 buff = this-> dev_buffer;
3797 //precomp_data=*setup_data.precomp_data;
3798 interac_data= setup_data.interac_data;
3799 input_data =*setup_data. input_data;
3800 output_data =*setup_data. output_data;
3801 }
3802 sctl::Profile::Toc();
3803
3804 { // Offloaded computation.
3805
3806 // Set interac_data.
3807 size_t m, dof, ker_dim0, ker_dim1, n_blk0;
3808 std::vector<sctl::Vector<size_t> > fft_vec;
3809 std::vector<sctl::Vector<size_t> > ifft_vec;
3810 std::vector<sctl::Vector<Real_t> > fft_scl;
3811 std::vector<sctl::Vector<Real_t> > ifft_scl;
3812 std::vector<sctl::Vector<size_t> > interac_vec;
3813 std::vector<sctl::Vector<size_t> > interac_dsp;
3814 sctl::Vector<Real_t*> precomp_mat;
3815 { // Set interac_data.
3816 char* data_ptr=&interac_data[0][0];
3817
3818 buff_size=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
3819 m =((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
3820 dof =((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
3821 ker_dim0 =((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
3822 ker_dim1 =((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
3823 n_blk0 =((size_t*)data_ptr)[0]; data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t);
3824
3825 fft_vec .resize(n_blk0);
3826 ifft_vec.resize(n_blk0);
3827 fft_scl .resize(n_blk0);
3828 ifft_scl.resize(n_blk0);
3829 interac_vec.resize(n_blk0);
3830 interac_dsp.resize(n_blk0);
3831
3832 sctl::Vector<size_t> interac_mat;
3833 { size_t N=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0];
3834 interac_mat.ReInit(N,sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)(data_ptr+sizeof(size_t)),N),false); }
3835 data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t)+interac_mat.Dim()*sizeof(size_t);
3836
3837 sctl::Vector<Real_t*> interac_mat_ptr;
3838 { size_t N=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0];
3839 interac_mat_ptr.ReInit(N,sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t*>((Real_t**)(data_ptr+sizeof(size_t)),N),false); }
3840 data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t)+interac_mat_ptr.Dim()*sizeof(Real_t*);
3841
3842#if 0 // Since we skip SetupPrecomp for V-list
3843 precomp_mat.ReInit(interac_mat.Dim());
3844 for(size_t i=0;i<interac_mat.Dim();i++){
3845 precomp_mat[i]=(Real_t*)(precomp_data[0]+interac_mat[i]);
3846 }
3847#else
3848 precomp_mat.ReInit(interac_mat_ptr.Dim());
3849 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<interac_mat_ptr.Dim();i++){
3850 precomp_mat[i]=interac_mat_ptr[i];
3851 }
3852#endif
3853
3854 for(size_t blk0=0;blk0<n_blk0;blk0++){
3855 { size_t N=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0];
3856 fft_vec[blk0].ReInit(N,sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)(data_ptr+sizeof(size_t)),N),false); }
3857 data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t)+fft_vec[blk0].Dim()*sizeof(size_t);
3858
3859 { size_t N=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0];
3860 ifft_vec[blk0].ReInit(N,sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)(data_ptr+sizeof(size_t)),N),false); }
3861 data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t)+ifft_vec[blk0].Dim()*sizeof(size_t);
3862
3863 { size_t N=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0];
3864 fft_scl[blk0].ReInit(N,sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>((Real_t*)(data_ptr+sizeof(size_t)),N),false); }
3865 data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t)+((fft_scl[blk0].Dim()*sizeof(Real_t)+sizeof(size_t)-1)/sizeof(size_t))*sizeof(size_t);
3866
3867 { size_t N=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0];
3868 ifft_scl[blk0].ReInit(N,sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>((Real_t*)(data_ptr+sizeof(size_t)),N),false); }
3869 data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t)+((ifft_scl[blk0].Dim()*sizeof(Real_t)+sizeof(size_t)-1)/sizeof(size_t))*sizeof(size_t);
3870
3871 { size_t N=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0];
3872 interac_vec[blk0].ReInit(N,sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)(data_ptr+sizeof(size_t)),N),false); }
3873 data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t)+interac_vec[blk0].Dim()*sizeof(size_t);
3874
3875 { size_t N=((size_t*)data_ptr)[0];
3876 interac_dsp[blk0].ReInit(N,sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)(data_ptr+sizeof(size_t)),N),false); }
3877 data_ptr+=sizeof(size_t)+interac_dsp[blk0].Dim()*sizeof(size_t);
3878 }
3879 }
3880
3881 int omp_p=omp_get_max_threads();
3882 size_t M_dim, fftsize;
3883 {
3884 size_t n1=m*2;
3885 size_t n2=n1*n1;
3886 size_t n3_=n2*(n1/2+1);
3887 size_t chld_cnt=1UL<<PVFMM_COORD_DIM;
3888 fftsize=2*n3_*chld_cnt;
3889 M_dim=n3_;
3890 }
3891
3892 for(size_t blk0=0;blk0<n_blk0;blk0++){ // interactions
3893 size_t n_in = fft_vec[blk0].Dim();
3894 size_t n_out=ifft_vec[blk0].Dim();
3895
3896 size_t input_dim=n_in *ker_dim0*dof*fftsize;
3897 size_t output_dim=n_out*ker_dim1*dof*fftsize;
3898 size_t buffer_dim=2*(ker_dim0+ker_dim1)*dof*fftsize*omp_p;
3899
3900 sctl::Vector<Real_t> fft_in (input_dim, sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>((Real_t*)&buff[ 0 ], input_dim),false);
3901 sctl::Vector<Real_t> fft_out(output_dim, sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>((Real_t*)&buff[ input_dim *sizeof(Real_t)], output_dim),false);
3902 sctl::Vector<Real_t> buffer(buffer_dim, sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>((Real_t*)&buff[(input_dim+output_dim)*sizeof(Real_t)], buffer_dim),false);
3903
3904 { // FFT
3905 if(np==1) sctl::Profile::Tic("FFT",&this->sctl_comm,false,100);
3906 sctl::Vector<Real_t> input_data_( input_data.dim[0]* input_data.dim[1], sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>(input_data[0], input_data.dim[0]*input_data.dim[1]), false);
3907 FFT_UpEquiv(dof, m, ker_dim0, fft_vec[blk0], fft_scl[blk0], input_data_, fft_in, buffer);
3908 if(np==1) sctl::Profile::Toc();
3909 }
3910 { // Hadamard
3911#ifdef PVFMM_HAVE_PAPI
3912#ifdef SCTL_VERBOSE
3913 std::cout << "Starting counters new\n";
3914 if (PAPI_start(EventSet) != PAPI_OK) std::cout << "handle_error3" << std::endl;
3915#endif
3916#endif
3917 if(np==1) sctl::Profile::Tic("HadamardProduct",&this->sctl_comm,false,100);
3918 VListHadamard<Real_t>(dof, M_dim, ker_dim0, ker_dim1, interac_dsp[blk0], interac_vec[blk0], precomp_mat, fft_in, fft_out);
3919 if(np==1) sctl::Profile::Toc();
3920#ifdef PVFMM_HAVE_PAPI
3921#ifdef SCTL_VERBOSE
3922 if (PAPI_stop(EventSet, values) != PAPI_OK) std::cout << "handle_error4" << std::endl;
3923 std::cout << "Stopping counters\n";
3924#endif
3925#endif
3926 }
3927 { // IFFT
3928 if(np==1) sctl::Profile::Tic("IFFT",&this->sctl_comm,false,100);
3929 sctl::Vector<Real_t> output_data_(output_data.dim[0]*output_data.dim[1], sctl::Ptr2Itr<Real_t>(output_data[0], output_data.dim[0]*output_data.dim[1]), false);
3930 FFT_Check2Equiv(dof, m, ker_dim1, ifft_vec[blk0], ifft_scl[blk0], fft_out, output_data_, buffer);
3931 if(np==1) sctl::Profile::Toc();
3932 }
3933 }
3934 }
3935}

3937
3938template <class FMMNode>
3939void FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::Down2DownSetup(SetupData<FMMNode_t>& setup_data, FMMTree_t* tree, std::vector<sctl::Matrix<Real_t> >& buff, std::vector<sctl::Vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>> >& n_list, int level, bool device){
3940 if(!this->MultipoleOrder()) return;
3941 { // Set setup_data
3942 setup_data.level=level;
3943 setup_data.kernel=kernel->k_l2l;
3944 setup_data.interac_type.resize(1);
3945 setup_data.interac_type[0]=D2D_Type;
3946
3947 setup_data. input_data=&buff[1];
3948 setup_data.input_data_mirror=&tree->node_data_buff_mirror[1];
3949 setup_data.output_data=&buff[1];
3950 setup_data.output_data_mirror=&tree->node_data_buff_mirror[1];
3951 sctl::Vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& nodes_in =n_list[1];
3952 sctl::Vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& nodes_out=n_list[1];
3953
3954 setup_data.nodes_in .clear();
3955 setup_data.nodes_out.clear();
3956 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<nodes_in .Dim();i++) if((nodes_in [i]->Depth()==level-1) && nodes_in [i]->pt_cnt[1]) setup_data.nodes_in .push_back(nodes_in [i]);
3957 for(sctl::Long i=0;i<nodes_out.Dim();i++) if((nodes_out[i]->Depth()==level ) && nodes_out[i]->pt_cnt[1]) setup_data.nodes_out.push_back(nodes_out[i]);
3958 }
3959
3960 std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& nodes_in =setup_data.nodes_in ;
3961 std::vector<sctl::Iterator<FMMNode_t>>& nodes_out=setup_data.nodes_out;
3962 std::vector<sctl::Vector<Real_t>*>& input_vector=setup_data. input_vector; input_vector.clear();
3963 std::vector<sctl::Vector<Real_t>*>& output_vector=setup_data.output_vector; output_vector.clear();
3964 for(size_t i=0;i<nodes_in .size();i++) input_vector.push_back(&((FMMData*)(nodes_in[i])->FMMData())->dnward_equiv);
3965 for(size_t i=0;i<nodes_out.size();i++) output_vector.push_back(&((FMMData*)(nodes_out[i])->FMMData())->dnward_equiv);
3966
3967 SetupInterac(setup_data,device);
3968}
3969
3970template <class FMMNode>
3971void FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::Down2Down (SetupData<FMMNode_t>& setup_data, bool device){
3972 if(!this->MultipoleOrder()) return;
3973 //Add Down2Down contribution.
3974 EvalList(setup_data, device);
3975}

3978template <class FMMNode>
3979void FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::PtSetup(SetupData<FMMNode_t>& setup_data, void* data_){
3980 struct PackedData{
3981 size_t len;
3982 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>* ptr;
3983 sctl::Vector<size_t> cnt;
3984 sctl::Vector<size_t> dsp;
3985 };
3986 struct InteracData{
3987 sctl::Vector<size_t> in_node;
3988 sctl::Vector<size_t> scal_idx;
3989 sctl::Vector<Real_t> coord_shift;
3990 sctl::Vector<size_t> interac_cnt;
3991 sctl::Vector<size_t> interac_dsp;
3992 sctl::Vector<size_t> interac_cst;
3993 sctl::Vector<Real_t> scal[4*sctl::MAX_DEPTH];
3994 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M[4];
3995 };
3996 struct ptSetupData{
3997 int level;
3998 const Kernel<Real_t>* kernel;
3999
4000 PackedData src_coord; // Src coord
4001 PackedData src_value; // Src density
4002 PackedData srf_coord; // Srf coord
4003 PackedData srf_value; // Srf density
4004 PackedData trg_coord; // Trg coord
4005 PackedData trg_value; // Trg potential
4006
4007 InteracData interac_data;
4008 };
4009 ptSetupData& data=*(ptSetupData*)data_;
4010 if(data.interac_data.interac_cnt.Dim()){ // Set data.interac_data.interac_cst
4011 InteracData& intdata=data.interac_data;
4012
4013 sctl::Vector<size_t> cnt;
4014 sctl::Vector<size_t>& dsp=intdata.interac_cst;
4015 cnt.ReInit(intdata.interac_cnt.Dim());
4016 dsp.ReInit(intdata.interac_dsp.Dim());
4017 #pragma omp parallel for
4018 for(sctl::Long trg=0;trg<cnt.Dim();trg++){
4019 size_t trg_cnt=data.trg_coord.cnt[trg];
4020
4021 cnt[trg]=0;
4022 for(size_t i=0;i<intdata.interac_cnt[trg];i++){
4023 size_t int_id=intdata.interac_dsp[trg]+i;
4024 size_t src=intdata.in_node[int_id];
4025
4026 size_t src_cnt=data.src_coord.cnt[src];
4027 size_t srf_cnt=data.srf_coord.cnt[src];
4028 cnt[trg]+=(src_cnt+srf_cnt)*trg_cnt;
4029 }
4030 }
4031
4032 dsp[0]=cnt[0];
4033 sctl::omp_par::scan(&cnt[0],&dsp[0],dsp.Dim());
4034 }
4035 { // pack data
4036 struct PackedSetupData{
4037 size_t size;
4038
4039 int level;
4040 const Kernel<Real_t>* kernel;
4041
4042 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>* src_coord; // Src coord
4043 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>* src_value; // Src density
4044 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>* srf_coord; // Srf coord
4045 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>* srf_value; // Srf density
4046 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>* trg_coord; // Trg coord
4047 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>* trg_value; // Trg potential
4048
4049 size_t src_coord_cnt_size; size_t src_coord_cnt_offset;
4050 size_t src_coord_dsp_size; size_t src_coord_dsp_offset;
4051 size_t src_value_cnt_size; size_t src_value_cnt_offset;
4052 size_t src_value_dsp_size; size_t src_value_dsp_offset;
4053
4054 size_t srf_coord_cnt_size; size_t srf_coord_cnt_offset;
4055 size_t srf_coord_dsp_size; size_t srf_coord_dsp_offset;
4056 size_t srf_value_cnt_size; size_t srf_value_cnt_offset;
4057 size_t srf_value_dsp_size; size_t srf_value_dsp_offset;
4058
4059 size_t trg_coord_cnt_size; size_t trg_coord_cnt_offset;
4060 size_t trg_coord_dsp_size; size_t trg_coord_dsp_offset;
4061 size_t trg_value_cnt_size; size_t trg_value_cnt_offset;
4062 size_t trg_value_dsp_size; size_t trg_value_dsp_offset;
4063
4064 // interac_data
4065 size_t in_node_size; size_t in_node_offset;
4066 size_t scal_idx_size; size_t scal_idx_offset;
4067 size_t coord_shift_size; size_t coord_shift_offset;
4068 size_t interac_cnt_size; size_t interac_cnt_offset;
4069 size_t interac_dsp_size; size_t interac_dsp_offset;
4070 size_t interac_cst_size; size_t interac_cst_offset;
4071 size_t scal_dim[4*sctl::MAX_DEPTH]; size_t scal_offset[4*sctl::MAX_DEPTH];
4072 size_t Mdim[4][2]; size_t M_offset[4];
4073 };
4074 PackedSetupData pkd_data;
4075 { // Set pkd_data
4076 size_t offset=mem::align_ptr(sizeof(PackedSetupData));
4077
4078 pkd_data. level=data. level;
4079 pkd_data.kernel=data.kernel;
4080
4081 pkd_data.src_coord=data.src_coord.ptr;
4082 pkd_data.src_value=data.src_value.ptr;
4083 pkd_data.srf_coord=data.srf_coord.ptr;
4084 pkd_data.srf_value=data.srf_value.ptr;
4085 pkd_data.trg_coord=data.trg_coord.ptr;
4086 pkd_data.trg_value=data.trg_value.ptr;
4087
4088 pkd_data.src_coord_cnt_offset=offset; pkd_data.src_coord_cnt_size=data.src_coord.cnt.Dim(); offset+=mem::align_ptr(sizeof(size_t)*pkd_data.src_coord_cnt_size);
4089 pkd_data.src_coord_dsp_offset=offset; pkd_data.src_coord_dsp_size=data.src_coord.dsp.Dim(); offset+=mem::align_ptr(sizeof(size_t)*pkd_data.src_coord_dsp_size);
4090 pkd_data.src_value_cnt_offset=offset; pkd_data.src_value_cnt_size=data.src_value.cnt.Dim(); offset+=mem::align_ptr(sizeof(size_t)*pkd_data.src_value_cnt_size);
4091 pkd_data.src_value_dsp_offset=offset; pkd_data.src_value_dsp_size=data.src_value.dsp.Dim(); offset+=mem::align_ptr(sizeof(size_t)*pkd_data.src_value_dsp_size);
4092
4093 pkd_data.srf_coord_cnt_offset=offset; pkd_data.srf_coord_cnt_size=data.srf_coord.cnt.Dim(); offset+=mem::align_ptr(sizeof(size_t)*pkd_data.srf_coord_cnt_size);
4094 pkd_data.srf_coord_dsp_offset=offset; pkd_data.srf_coord_dsp_size=data.srf_coord.dsp.Dim(); offset+=mem::align_ptr(sizeof(size_t)*pkd_data.srf_coord_dsp_size);
4095 pkd_data.srf_value_cnt_offset=offset; pkd_data.srf_value_cnt_size=data.srf_value.cnt.Dim(); offset+=mem::align_ptr(sizeof(size_t)*pkd_data.srf_value_cnt_size);
4096 pkd_data.srf_value_dsp_offset=offset; pkd_data.srf_value_dsp_size=data.srf_value.dsp.Dim(); offset+=mem::align_ptr(sizeof(size_t)*pkd_data.srf_value_dsp_size);
4097
4098 pkd_data.trg_coord_cnt_offset=offset; pkd_data.trg_coord_cnt_size=data.trg_coord.cnt.Dim(); offset+=mem::align_ptr(sizeof(size_t)*pkd_data.trg_coord_cnt_size);
4099 pkd_data.trg_coord_dsp_offset=offset; pkd_data.trg_coord_dsp_size=data.trg_coord.dsp.Dim(); offset+=mem::align_ptr(sizeof(size_t)*pkd_data.trg_coord_dsp_size);
4100 pkd_data.trg_value_cnt_offset=offset; pkd_data.trg_value_cnt_size=data.trg_value.cnt.Dim(); offset+=mem::align_ptr(sizeof(size_t)*pkd_data.trg_value_cnt_size);
4101 pkd_data.trg_value_dsp_offset=offset; pkd_data.trg_value_dsp_size=data.trg_value.dsp.Dim(); offset+=mem::align_ptr(sizeof(size_t)*pkd_data.trg_value_dsp_size);
4102
4103
4104 InteracData& intdata=data.interac_data;
4105 pkd_data. in_node_offset=offset; pkd_data. in_node_size=intdata. in_node.Dim(); offset+=mem::align_ptr(sizeof(size_t)*pkd_data. in_node_size);
4106 pkd_data. scal_idx_offset=offset; pkd_data. scal_idx_size=intdata. scal_idx.Dim(); offset+=mem::align_ptr(sizeof(size_t)*pkd_data. scal_idx_size);
4107 pkd_data.coord_shift_offset=offset; pkd_data.coord_shift_size=intdata.coord_shift.Dim(); offset+=mem::align_ptr(sizeof(Real_t)*pkd_data.coord_shift_size);
4108 pkd_data.interac_cnt_offset=offset; pkd_data.interac_cnt_size=intdata.interac_cnt.Dim(); offset+=mem::align_ptr(sizeof(size_t)*pkd_data.interac_cnt_size);
4109 pkd_data.interac_dsp_offset=offset; pkd_data.interac_dsp_size=intdata.interac_dsp.Dim(); offset+=mem::align_ptr(sizeof(size_t)*pkd_data.interac_dsp_size);
4110 pkd_data.interac_cst_offset=offset; pkd_data.interac_cst_size=intdata.interac_cst.Dim(); offset+=mem::align_ptr(sizeof(size_t)*pkd_data.interac_cst_size);
4111
4112 for(size_t i=0;i<4*sctl::MAX_DEPTH;i++){
4113 pkd_data.scal_offset[i]=offset; pkd_data.scal_dim[i]=intdata.scal[i].Dim(); offset+=mem::align_ptr(sizeof(Real_t)*pkd_data.scal_dim[i]);
4114 }
4115 for(size_t i=0;i<4;i++){
4116 size_t& Mdim0=pkd_data.Mdim[i][0];
4117 size_t& Mdim1=pkd_data.Mdim[i][1];
4118 pkd_data.M_offset[i]=offset; Mdim0=intdata.M[i].Dim(0); Mdim1=intdata.M[i].Dim(1); offset+=mem::align_ptr(sizeof(Real_t)*Mdim0*Mdim1);
4119 }
4120
4121 pkd_data.size=offset;
4122 }
4123 { // Set setup_data.interac_data
4124 sctl::Matrix<char>& buff=setup_data.interac_data;
4125 if(pkd_data.size>(size_t)(buff.Dim(0)*buff.Dim(1))){
4126 setup_data.interac_data_mirror.Free(); // host buffer is about to be reallocated
4127 buff.ReInit(1,pkd_data.size);
4128 }
4129 ((PackedSetupData*)&buff[0][0])[0]=pkd_data;
4130
4131 if(pkd_data.src_coord_cnt_size) memcpy(&buff[0][pkd_data.src_coord_cnt_offset], &data.src_coord.cnt[0], pkd_data.src_coord_cnt_size*sizeof(size_t));
4132 if(pkd_data.src_coord_dsp_size) memcpy(&buff[0][pkd_data.src_coord_dsp_offset], &data.src_coord.dsp[0], pkd_data.src_coord_dsp_size*sizeof(size_t));
4133 if(pkd_data.src_value_cnt_size) memcpy(&buff[0][pkd_data.src_value_cnt_offset], &data.src_value.cnt[0], pkd_data.src_value_cnt_size*sizeof(size_t));
4134 if(pkd_data.src_value_dsp_size) memcpy(&buff[0][pkd_data.src_value_dsp_offset], &data.src_value.dsp[0], pkd_data.src_value_dsp_size*sizeof(size_t));
4135
4136 if(pkd_data.srf_coord_cnt_size) memcpy(&buff[0][pkd_data.srf_coord_cnt_offset], &data.srf_coord.cnt[0], pkd_data.srf_coord_cnt_size*sizeof(size_t));
4137 if(pkd_data.srf_coord_dsp_size) memcpy(&buff[0][pkd_data.srf_coord_dsp_offset], &data.srf_coord.dsp[0], pkd_data.srf_coord_dsp_size*sizeof(size_t));
4138 if(pkd_data.srf_value_cnt_size) memcpy(&buff[0][pkd_data.srf_value_cnt_offset], &data.srf_value.cnt[0], pkd_data.srf_value_cnt_size*sizeof(size_t));
4139 if(pkd_data.srf_value_dsp_size) memcpy(&buff[0][pkd_data.srf_value_dsp_offset], &data.srf_value.dsp[0], pkd_data.srf_value_dsp_size*sizeof(size_t));
4140
4141 if(pkd_data.trg_coord_cnt_size) memcpy(&buff[0][pkd_data.trg_coord_cnt_offset], &data.trg_coord.cnt[0], pkd_data.trg_coord_cnt_size*sizeof(size_t));
4142 if(pkd_data.trg_coord_dsp_size) memcpy(&buff[0][pkd_data.trg_coord_dsp_offset], &data.trg_coord.dsp[0], pkd_data.trg_coord_dsp_size*sizeof(size_t));
4143 if(pkd_data.trg_value_cnt_size) memcpy(&buff[0][pkd_data.trg_value_cnt_offset], &data.trg_value.cnt[0], pkd_data.trg_value_cnt_size*sizeof(size_t));
4144 if(pkd_data.trg_value_dsp_size) memcpy(&buff[0][pkd_data.trg_value_dsp_offset], &data.trg_value.dsp[0], pkd_data.trg_value_dsp_size*sizeof(size_t));
4145
4146
4147 InteracData& intdata=data.interac_data;
4148 if(pkd_data. in_node_size) memcpy(&buff[0][pkd_data. in_node_offset], &intdata. in_node[0], pkd_data. in_node_size*sizeof(size_t));
4149 if(pkd_data. scal_idx_size) memcpy(&buff[0][pkd_data. scal_idx_offset], &intdata. scal_idx[0], pkd_data. scal_idx_size*sizeof(size_t));
4150 if(pkd_data.coord_shift_size) memcpy(&buff[0][pkd_data.coord_shift_offset], &intdata.coord_shift[0], pkd_data.coord_shift_size*sizeof(Real_t));
4151 if(pkd_data.interac_cnt_size) memcpy(&buff[0][pkd_data.interac_cnt_offset], &intdata.interac_cnt[0], pkd_data.interac_cnt_size*sizeof(size_t));
4152 if(pkd_data.interac_dsp_size) memcpy(&buff[0][pkd_data.interac_dsp_offset], &intdata.interac_dsp[0], pkd_data.interac_dsp_size*sizeof(size_t));
4153 if(pkd_data.interac_cst_size) memcpy(&buff[0][pkd_data.interac_cst_offset], &intdata.interac_cst[0], pkd_data.interac_cst_size*sizeof(size_t));
4154 for(size_t i=0;i<4*sctl::MAX_DEPTH;i++){
4155 if(intdata.scal[i].Dim()) memcpy(&buff[0][pkd_data.scal_offset[i]], &intdata.scal[i][0], intdata.scal[i].Dim()*sizeof(Real_t));
4156 }
4157 for(size_t i=0;i<4;i++){
4158 if(intdata.M[i].Dim(0) && intdata.M[i].Dim(1)) memcpy(&buff[0][pkd_data.M_offset[i]], &intdata.M[i][0][0], intdata.M[i].Dim(0)*intdata.M[i].Dim(1)*sizeof(Real_t));
4159 }
4160 }
4161 }
4162 { // Resize device buffer
4163 size_t n=setup_data.output_data->Dim(0)*setup_data.output_data->Dim(1)*sizeof(Real_t);
4164 if((size_t)this->dev_buffer.Dim()<n){
4165 this->dev_buffer_mirror.Free(); // host buffer is about to be reallocated
4166 this->dev_buffer.ReInit(n);
4167 }
4168 }
4169}
4170
4171template <class FMMNode>
4172template <int SYNC>
4173void FMM_Pts<FMMNode>::EvalListPts(SetupData<FMMNode_t>& setup_data, bool device){
4174 if(setup_data.kernel->ker_dim[0]*setup_data.kernel->ker_dim[1]==0) return;
4175 if(setup_data.interac_data.Dim(0)==0 || setup_data.interac_data.Dim(1)==0){
4176 sctl::Profile::Tic("Host2Device",&this->sctl_comm,false,25);
4177 sctl::Profile::Toc();
4178 sctl::Profile::Tic("DeviceComp",&this->sctl_comm,false,20);
4179 sctl::Profile::Toc();
4180 return;
4181 }
4182
4183 bool have_gpu=false;
4184 #if defined(PVFMM_HAVE_CUDA)
4185 have_gpu=true;
4186 #endif
4187
4188 sctl::Profile::Tic("Host2Device",&this->sctl_comm,false,25);
4189 DeviceVector<char> dev_buff;
4190 DeviceMatrix<char> interac_data;
4191 DeviceMatrix<Real_t> coord_data;
4192 DeviceMatrix<Real_t> input_data;
4193 DeviceMatrix<Real_t> output_data;
4194 size_t ptr_single_layer_kernel=(size_t)NULL;
4195 size_t ptr_double_layer_kernel=(size_t)NULL;
4196 if(device && !have_gpu){
4197 dev_buff = this->dev_buffer_mirror.AllocDevice(this->dev_buffer,false);
4198 interac_data= setup_data.interac_data_mirror.AllocDevice(setup_data.interac_data,false);
4199 if(setup_data. coord_data!=NULL) coord_data = setup_data.coord_data_mirror->AllocDevice(*setup_data.coord_data,false);
4200 if(setup_data. input_data!=NULL) input_data = setup_data.input_data_mirror->AllocDevice(*setup_data.input_data,false);
4201 if(setup_data. output_data!=NULL) output_data = setup_data.output_data_mirror->AllocDevice(*setup_data.output_data,false);
4202 ptr_single_layer_kernel=setup_data.kernel->dev_ker_poten;
4203 ptr_double_layer_kernel=setup_data.kernel->dev_dbl_layer_poten;
4204 }else{
4205 dev_buff = this-> dev_buffer;
4206 interac_data= setup_data.interac_data;
4207 if(setup_data. coord_data!=NULL) coord_data =*setup_data. coord_data;
4208 if(setup_data. input_data!=NULL) input_data =*setup_data. input_data;
4209 if(setup_data. output_data!=NULL) output_data =*setup_data. output_data;
4210 ptr_single_layer_kernel=(size_t)setup_data.kernel->ker_poten;
4211 ptr_double_layer_kernel=(size_t)setup_data.kernel->dbl_layer_poten;
4212 }
4213 sctl::Profile::Toc();
4214
4215 sctl::Profile::Tic("DeviceComp",&this->sctl_comm,false,20);
4216 { // Computation (host; the CUDA device path returns above).
4217 struct PackedData{

4221 sctl::Vector<size_t> dsp;

4230 sctl::Vector<Real_t> scal[4*sctl::MAX_DEPTH];
4231 sctl::Matrix<Real_t> M[4];
4232 };

4247 ptSetupData data;
4248 { // Initialize data
4249 struct PackedSetupData{
4250 size_t size;
4251

4253 const Kernel<Real_t>* kernel;
4254
4255 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>* src_coord; // Src coord
4256 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>* src_value; // Src density
4257 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>* srf_coord; // Srf coord
4258 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>* srf_value; // Srf density

4260 sctl::Matrix<Real_t>* trg_value; // Trg potential

4263 size_t src_coord_dsp_size; size_t src_coord_dsp_offset;
4264 size_t src_value_cnt_size; size_t src_value_cnt_offset;
4265 size_t src_value_dsp_size; size_t src_value_dsp_offset;
4266

4268 size_t srf_coord_dsp_size; size_t srf_coord_dsp_offset;

4274 size_t trg_value_cnt_size; size_t trg_value_cnt_offset;
4275 size_t trg_value_dsp_size; size_t trg_value_dsp_offset;
4276
4277 // interac_data
4278 size_t in_node_size; size_t in_node_offset;
4279 size_t scal_idx_size; size_t scal_idx_offset;
4280 size_t coord_shift_size; size_t coord_shift_offset;

4282 size_t interac_dsp_size; size_t interac_dsp_offset;

4285 size_t Mdim[4][2]; size_t M_offset[4];
4286 };
4287 DeviceMatrix<char>& setupdata=interac_data;
4288 PackedSetupData& pkd_data=*((PackedSetupData*)setupdata[0]);
4289
4290 data. level=pkd_data. level;
4291 data.kernel=pkd_data.kernel;
4292
4293 data.src_coord.ptr=pkd_data.src_coord;
4294 data.src_value.ptr=pkd_data.src_value;

4296 data.srf_value.ptr=pkd_data.srf_value;
4297 data.trg_coord.ptr=pkd_data.trg_coord;
4298 data.trg_value.ptr=pkd_data.trg_value;
4299
4300
4301 data.src_coord.cnt.ReInit(pkd_data.src_coord_cnt_size, sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)&setupdata[0][pkd_data.src_coord_cnt_offset], pkd_data.src_coord_cnt_size), false);
4302 data.src_coord.dsp.ReInit(pkd_data.src_coord_dsp_size, sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)&setupdata[0][pkd_data.src_coord_dsp_offset], pkd_data.src_coord_dsp_size), false);
4303 data.src_value.cnt.ReInit(pkd_data.src_value_cnt_size, sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)&setupdata[0][pkd_data.src_value_cnt_offset], pkd_data.src_value_cnt_size), false);
4304 data.src_value.dsp.ReInit(pkd_data.src_value_dsp_size, sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)&setupdata[0][pkd_data.src_value_dsp_offset], pkd_data.src_value_dsp_size), false);
4305
4306 data.srf_coord.cnt.ReInit(pkd_data.srf_coord_cnt_size, sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)&setupdata[0][pkd_data.srf_coord_cnt_offset], pkd_data.srf_coord_cnt_size), false);
4307 data.srf_coord.dsp.ReInit(pkd_data.srf_coord_dsp_size, sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)&setupdata[0][pkd_data.srf_coord_dsp_offset], pkd_data.srf_coord_dsp_size), false);
4308 data.srf_value.cnt.ReInit(pkd_data.srf_value_cnt_size, sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)&setupdata[0][pkd_data.srf_value_cnt_offset], pkd_data.srf_value_cnt_size), false);
4309 data.srf_value.dsp.ReInit(pkd_data.srf_value_dsp_size, sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)&setupdata[0][pkd_data.srf_value_dsp_offset], pkd_data.srf_value_dsp_size), false);
4310
4311 data.trg_coord.cnt.ReInit(pkd_data.trg_coord_cnt_size, sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)&setupdata[0][pkd_data.trg_coord_cnt_offset], pkd_data.trg_coord_cnt_size), false);
4312 data.trg_coord.dsp.ReInit(pkd_data.trg_coord_dsp_size, sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)&setupdata[0][pkd_data.trg_coord_dsp_offset], pkd_data.trg_coord_dsp_size), false);
4313 data.trg_value.cnt.ReInit(pkd_data.trg_value_cnt_size, sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)&setupdata[0][pkd_data.trg_value_cnt_offset], pkd_data.trg_value_cnt_size), false);
4314 data.trg_value.dsp.ReInit(pkd_data.trg_value_dsp_size, sctl::Ptr2Itr<size_t>((size_t*)&setupdata[0][pkd_data.trg_value_dsp_offset], pkd_data.trg_value_dsp_size), false);
4315
